````math
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~-\sum_{j\neq j_{2}\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})}\mathbf{logit}_{5,j}\cdot\Big(\sum_{r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}}\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda_{5,j,r})\cdot\Big(V_{j,r}(g_{1})-\Lambda_{5,j,r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~\pm\sum_{j\notin\tau(\mathcal{Y})}\mathbf{logit}_{5,j}\widetilde{O}(\sigma^{q}_{0})\bigg)\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s,\tau(x_{0})=s^{\prime}}\Bigg].`$ |  |

###### Proof.

For $`\ell=1`$,

- •
  for the diagonal entry $`[\mathbf{Q}_{4,3}]_{s,s}`$ with
  $`s\in\tau(\mathcal{X})`$,

  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}_{4,3}}\mathsf{Loss}^{2,1}_{5}\Big]_{s,s}=\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}_{{\mathsf{ans},0}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}\cdot\bigg(\sum_{j\in[d]}\mathcal{E}_{5,j}(\mathbf{Z}^{2,0})\sum_{r\in[m]}\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}\big(\Lambda_{5,j,r}\big)\cdot`$ |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~\Big(\langle\mathbf{W}_{5,j,r},\mathbf{Z}_{\mathsf{pred},1}\rangle-\Lambda_{5,j,r}+b_{5,j,r}\Big)\bigg)\mathds{1}_{s=\tau(x_{0})}\Bigg]`$ |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}_{{\mathsf{ans},0}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}\cdot\bigg((1-\mathbf{logit}_{5,j_{1}})\cdot\sum_{r\in[m]}\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda_{5,j_{1},r})\Big(V_{j_{1},r}(g_{1})-\Lambda_{5,j_{1},r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)`$ |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~-\sum_{j\neq j_{1}\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})}\mathbf{logit}_{5,j}\cdot\sum_{r\in[m]}\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda_{5,j,r})\Big(V_{j,r}(g_{1})-\Lambda_{5,j,r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)`$ |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~-\sum_{j\notin\tau(\mathcal{Y})}\mathbf{logit}_{5,j}\cdot\sum_{r\in[m]}\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda_{5,j,r})\Big(\langle\mathbf{W}_{5,j,r},\mathbf{Z}_{\mathsf{pred},1}\rangle-\Lambda_{5,j,r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\bigg)\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{0})=s}\Bigg]`$ |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}_{{\mathsf{ans},0}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}\cdot`$ |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~~\bigg((1-\mathbf{logit}_{5,j_{1}})\cdot\Big(\sum_{r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j_{1}}}\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda_{5,j_{1},r})\cdot\Big(V_{j_{1},r}(g_{1})-\Lambda_{5,j_{1},r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)`$ |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~-\sum_{j\neq j_{1}\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})}\mathbf{logit}_{5,j}\cdot\Big(\sum_{r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}}\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda_{5,j,r})\cdot\Big(V_{j,r}(g_{1})-\Lambda_{5,j,r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)`$ |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~\pm\sum_{j\notin\tau(\mathcal{Y})}\mathbf{logit}_{5,j}\widetilde{O}(\sigma^{q}_{0})\bigg)\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{0})=s}\Bigg],`$ |  |

  where the last equality follows from
  [Lemma D.6](#A4.Thmlemma6 "Lemma D.6 (Characterizations of Lambda). ‣ Notations for activated neurons. ‣ D.2 Some Useful Bounds for Gradients ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
  and
  [Lemma D.7](#A4.Thmlemma7 "Lemma D.7. ‣ Notations for activated neurons. ‣ D.2 Some Useful Bounds for Gradients ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization").
- •
  for the non-diagonal entry $`[\mathbf{Q}_{4,3}]_{s,s^{\prime}}`$ with
  $`s\not=s^{\prime}\in\tau(\mathcal{X})`$, the analysis is similar
  unless the condition that the gradient is non-zero only when
  $`s=\tau(x_{0})`$ and $`s^{\prime}=\tau(x_{1})`$. Thus, we have

  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}_{4,3}}\mathsf{Loss}^{2,1}_{5}\Big]_{s,s^{\prime}}=\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}_{{\mathsf{ans},0}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\cdot\bigg(\sum_{j\in[d]}\mathcal{E}_{5,j}(\mathbf{Z}^{2,0})\sum_{r\in[m]}\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}\big(\Lambda_{5,j,r}\big)\cdot`$ |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~\Big(\langle\mathbf{W}_{5,j,r},\mathbf{Z}_{\mathsf{pred},2}\rangle-\Lambda_{5,j,r}+b_{5,j,r}\Big)\bigg)\mathds{1}_{s=\tau(x_{0}),s^{\prime}=\tau(x_{1})}\Bigg]`$ |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}_{{\mathsf{ans},0}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\cdot`$ |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~~\bigg((1-\mathbf{logit}_{5,j_{1}})\cdot\Big(\sum_{r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j_{1}}}\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda_{5,j_{1},r})\cdot\Big(V_{j_{1},r}(g_{2})-\Lambda_{5,j_{1},r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)`$ |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~-\sum_{j\neq j_{1}\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})}\mathbf{logit}_{5,j}\cdot\Big(\sum_{r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}}\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda_{5,j,r})\cdot\Big(V_{j,r}(g_{2})-\Lambda_{5,j,r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)`$ |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~\pm\sum_{j\notin\tau(\mathcal{Y})}\mathbf{logit}_{5,j}\widetilde{O}(\sigma^{q}_{0})\bigg)\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{0})=s,\tau(x_{1})=s^{\prime}}\Bigg].`$ |  |

For $`\ell=2`$,

- •
  for the diagonal entry $`[\mathbf{Q}_{4,3}]_{s,s}`$ with
  $`s\in\tau(\mathcal{X})`$,

  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}_{4,3}}\mathsf{Loss}^{2,2}_{5}\Big]_{s,s}=\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\cdot\bigg(\sum_{j\in[d]}\mathcal{E}_{5,j}(\mathbf{Z}^{2,1})\sum_{r\in[m]}\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}\big(\Lambda_{5,j,r}\big)\cdot`$ |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~\Big(\langle\mathbf{W}_{5,j,r},\mathbf{Z}_{\mathsf{pred},2}\rangle-\Lambda_{5,j,r}+b_{5,j,r}\Big)\bigg)\mathds{1}_{s=\tau(x_{1})}\Bigg]`$ |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\cdot\bigg((1-\mathbf{logit}_{5,j_{2}})\cdot\sum_{r\in[m]}\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda_{5,j_{2},r})\Big(V_{j_{2},r}(g_{2})-\Lambda_{5,j_{2},r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)`$ |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~-\sum_{j\neq j_{2}\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})}\mathbf{logit}_{5,j}\cdot\sum_{r\in[m]}\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda_{5,j,r})\Big(V_{j,r}(g_{2})-\Lambda_{5,j,r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)`$ |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~-\sum_{j\notin\tau(\mathcal{Y})}\mathbf{logit}_{5,j}\cdot\sum_{r\in[m]}\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda_{5,j,r})\Big(\langle\mathbf{W}_{5,j,r},\mathbf{Z}_{\mathsf{pred},2}\rangle-\Lambda_{5,j,r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\bigg)\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s}\Bigg]`$ |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\cdot`$ |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~~\bigg((1-\mathbf{logit}_{5,j_{2}})\cdot\Big(\sum_{r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j_{2}}}\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda_{5,j_{2},r})\cdot\Big(V_{j_{2},r}(g_{2})-\Lambda_{5,j_{2},r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)`$ |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~-\sum_{j\neq j_{2}\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})}\mathbf{logit}_{5,j}\cdot\Big(\sum_{r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}}\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda_{5,j,r})\cdot\Big(V_{j,r}(g_{2})-\Lambda_{5,j,r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)`$ |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~\pm\sum_{j\notin\tau(\mathcal{Y})}\mathbf{logit}_{5,j}\widetilde{O}(\sigma^{q}_{0})\bigg)\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s}\Bigg],`$ |  |

  where the last equality follows from
  [Lemma D.6](#A4.Thmlemma6 "Lemma D.6 (Characterizations of Lambda). ‣ Notations for activated neurons. ‣ D.2 Some Useful Bounds for Gradients ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
  and
  [Lemma D.7](#A4.Thmlemma7 "Lemma D.7. ‣ Notations for activated neurons. ‣ D.2 Some Useful Bounds for Gradients ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization").
- •
  for the non-diagonal entry $`[\mathbf{Q}_{4,3}]_{s,s^{\prime}}`$ with
  $`s\not=s^{\prime}\in\tau(\mathcal{X})`$, the analysis is similar
  unless the condition that the gradient is non-zero only when
  $`s=\tau(x_{0})`$ and $`s^{\prime}=\tau(x_{1})`$. Thus, we have

  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}_{4,3}}\mathsf{Loss}^{2,2}_{5}\Big]_{s,s^{\prime}}=\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}\cdot\bigg(\sum_{j\in[d]}\mathcal{E}_{5,j}(\mathbf{Z}^{2,1})\sum_{r\in[m]}\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}\big(\Lambda_{5,j,r}\big)\cdot`$ |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~\Big(\langle\mathbf{W}_{5,j,r},\mathbf{Z}_{\mathsf{pred},1}\rangle-\Lambda_{5,j,r}+b_{5,j,r}\Big)\bigg)\mathds{1}_{s=\tau(x_{1}),s^{\prime}=\tau(x_{0})}\Bigg]`$ |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}\cdot`$ |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~~\bigg((1-\mathbf{logit}_{5,j_{2}})\cdot\Big(\sum_{r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j_{2}}}\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda_{5,j_{2},r})\cdot\Big(V_{j_{2},r}(g_{1})-\Lambda_{5,j_{2},r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)`$ |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~-\sum_{j\neq j_{2}\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})}\mathbf{logit}_{5,j}\cdot\Big(\sum_{r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}}\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda_{5,j,r})\cdot\Big(V_{j,r}(g_{1})-\Lambda_{5,j,r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)`$ |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~\pm\sum_{j\notin\tau(\mathcal{Y})}\mathbf{logit}_{5,j}\widetilde{O}(\sigma^{q}_{0})\bigg)\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s,\tau(x_{0})=s^{\prime}}\Bigg].`$ |  |

Therefore, we complete the proof. ∎

###### Lemma D.9 (Refined expression for the gradient of $`\mathbf{Q}_{4,4}`$).

Given $`s\in\tau(\mathcal{X})`$, for the diagonal entry
$`[\mathbf{Q}_{4,4}]_{s,s}`$ of the block $`\mathbf{Q}_{4,4}`$, letting
$`j_{1}=\tau(g_{1}(y_{0}))`$ and $`j_{2}=\tau(g_{2}(y_{1}))`$, we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}_{4,4}}\mathsf{Loss}^{2,1}_{5}\Big]_{s,s}=\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}_{{\mathsf{ans},0}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~~\bigg((1-\mathbf{logit}_{5,j_{1}})\cdot\Big(\sum_{r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j_{1}}}\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda_{5,j_{1},r})\cdot\Big(V_{j_{1},r}(y_{0})-\Lambda_{5,j_{1},r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~-\sum_{j\neq j_{1}\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})}\mathbf{logit}_{5,j}\cdot\Big(\sum_{r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}}\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda_{5,j,r})\cdot\Big(V_{j,r}(y_{0})-\Lambda_{5,j,r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~\pm\sum_{j\notin\tau(\mathcal{Y})}\mathbf{logit}_{5,j}\widetilde{O}(\sigma^{q}_{0})\bigg)\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{0})=s}\Bigg].`$ |  |

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}_{4,4}}\mathsf{Loss}^{2,2}_{5}\Big]_{s,s}=\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}\cdot`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~~\bigg((1-\mathbf{logit}_{5,j_{2}})\cdot\Big(\sum_{r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j_{2}}}\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda_{5,j_{2},r})\cdot\Big(V_{j_{2},r}(y_{1})-\Lambda_{5,j_{2},r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~-\sum_{j\neq j_{2}\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})}\mathbf{logit}_{5,j}\cdot\Big(\sum_{r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}}\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda_{5,j,r})\cdot\Big(V_{j,r}(y_{1})-\Lambda_{5,j,r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~\pm\sum_{j\notin\tau(\mathcal{Y})}\mathbf{logit}_{5,j}\widetilde{O}(\sigma^{q}_{0})\bigg)\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s}\Bigg].`$ |  |

Moreover, for the off-diagonal entries
$`[\mathbf{Q}_{4,4}]_{s,s^{\prime}}`$ with $`s\neq s^{\prime}`$, we have
$`[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}_{4,4}}\mathsf{Loss}^{2,1}_{5}]_{s,s^{\prime}}=0`$,
and

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}_{4,4}}\mathsf{Loss}^{2,2}_{5}\Big]_{s,s^{\prime}}=\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~~\bigg((1-\mathbf{logit}_{5,j_{2}})\cdot\Big(\sum_{r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j_{2}}}\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda_{5,j_{2},r})\cdot\Big(V_{j_{2},r}(y_{0})-\Lambda_{5,j_{2},r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~-\sum_{j\neq j_{2}\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})}\mathbf{logit}_{5,j}\cdot\Big(\sum_{r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}}\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda_{5,j,r})\cdot\Big(V_{j,r}(y_{0})-\Lambda_{5,j,r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~\pm\sum_{j\notin\tau(\mathcal{Y})}\mathbf{logit}_{5,j}\widetilde{O}(\sigma^{q}_{0})\bigg)\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s,\tau(x_{0})=s^{\prime}}\Bigg].`$ |  |

The proof is similar to
[Lemma D.8](#A4.Thmlemma8 "Lemma D.8 (Refined expression for the gradient of 𝐐_{4,3}). ‣ Notations for activated neurons. ‣ D.2 Some Useful Bounds for Gradients ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
and we omit it here.

##### Notations for gradient decompositions.

We shall define some useful notations to further simplify the
expressions of the gradient.

- •
  for $`\ell=1`$,

  - –
    for $`[\mathbf{Q}_{4,3}]_{s,s}`$ with $`s\in\tau(\mathcal{X})`$, we
    have
    $`[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}_{4,3}}\mathsf{Loss}_{5}^{2,1}]_{s,s}=\mathcal{N}_{s,3,1,i}+\mathcal{N}_{s,3,1,ii}+\mathcal{N}_{s,3,1,iii}`$,
    where

    |  |  |  |  |  |
    |----|----|----|----|----|
    |  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{s,3,1,i}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}_{{\mathsf{ans},0}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}\cdot(1-\mathbf{logit}_{5,j_{1}})\cdot`$ |  | (50) |
    |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~\Big(\sum_{r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j_{1}}}\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda_{5,j_{1},r})\cdot\Big(V_{j_{1},r}(g_{1})-\Lambda_{5,j_{1},r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{0})=s}\Bigg],`$ |  |  |

    |  |  |  |  |  |
    |----|----|----|----|----|
    |  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{s,3,1,ii}`$ | $`\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}_{{\mathsf{ans},0}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}\cdot\sum_{j\neq j_{1}\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})}\mathbf{logit}_{5,j}\cdot`$ |  | (51) |
    |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~\Big(\sum_{r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}}\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda_{5,j,r})\cdot\Big(V_{j,r}(g_{1})-\Lambda_{5,j,r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{0})=s}\Bigg],`$ |  |  |

    |  |  |  |  |  |
    |----|----|----|----|----|
    |  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{s,3,1,iii}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\pm\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}_{{\mathsf{ans},0}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}\cdot\sum_{j\notin\tau(\mathcal{Y})}\mathbf{logit}_{5,j}\widetilde{O}(\sigma^{q}_{0})\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{0})=s}\Bigg].~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~`$ |  | (52) |
  - –
    for $`[\mathbf{Q}_{4,4}]_{s,s}`$ with $`s\in\tau(\mathcal{X})`$, we
    have
    $`[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}_{4,4}}\mathsf{Loss}_{5}^{2,1}]_{s,s}=\mathcal{N}_{s,4,1,i}+\mathcal{N}_{s,4,1,ii}+\mathcal{N}_{s,4,1,iii}`$,
    where

    |  |  |  |  |  |
    |----|----|----|----|----|
    |  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{s,4,1,i}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}_{{\mathsf{ans},0}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot(1-\mathbf{logit}_{5,j_{1}})\cdot`$ |  | (53) |
    |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~\Big(\sum_{r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j_{1}}}\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda_{5,j_{1},r})\cdot\Big(V_{j_{1},r}(y_{0})-\Lambda_{5,j_{1},r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{0})=s}\Bigg],`$ |  |  |

    |  |  |  |  |  |
    |----|----|----|----|----|
    |  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{s,4,1,ii}`$ | $`\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}_{{\mathsf{ans},0}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot\sum_{j\neq j_{1}\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})}\mathbf{logit}_{5,j}\cdot`$ |  | (54) |
    |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~\Big(\sum_{r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}}\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda_{5,j,r})\cdot\Big(V_{j,r}(y_{0})-\Lambda_{5,j,r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{0})=s}\Bigg],`$ |  |  |

    |  |  |  |  |  |
    |----|----|----|----|----|
    |  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{s,4,1,iii}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\pm\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}_{{\mathsf{ans},0}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot\sum_{j\notin\tau(\mathcal{Y})}\mathbf{logit}_{5,j}\widetilde{O}(\sigma^{q}_{0})\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{0})=s}\Bigg].~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~`$ |  | (55) |
- •
  for $`\ell=2`$,

  - –
    for $`[\mathbf{Q}_{4,3}]_{s,s}`$ with $`s\in\tau(\mathcal{X})`$, we
    have
    $`[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}_{4,3}}\mathsf{Loss}_{5}^{2,2}]_{s,s}=\mathcal{N}_{s,3,2,i}+\mathcal{N}_{s,3,2,ii}+\mathcal{N}_{s,3,2,iii}`$,
    where

    |  |  |  |  |  |
    |----|----|----|----|----|
    |  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{s,3,2,i}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\cdot(1-\mathbf{logit}_{5,j_{2}})\cdot`$ |  | (56) |
    |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~\Big(\sum_{r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j_{2}}}\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda_{5,j_{2},r})\cdot\Big(V_{j_{2},r}(g_{2})-\Lambda_{5,j_{2},r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s}\Bigg],`$ |  |  |

    |  |  |  |  |  |
    |----|----|----|----|----|
    |  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{s,3,2,ii}`$ | $`\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\cdot\sum_{j\neq j_{2}\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})}\mathbf{logit}_{5,j}\cdot`$ |  | (57) |
    |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~\Big(\sum_{r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}}\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda_{5,j,r})\cdot\Big(V_{j,r}(g_{2})-\Lambda_{5,j,r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s}\Bigg],`$ |  |  |

    |  |  |  |  |  |
    |----|----|----|----|----|
    |  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{s,3,2,iii}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\pm\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\cdot\sum_{j\notin\tau(\mathcal{Y})}\mathbf{logit}_{5,j}\widetilde{O}(\sigma^{q}_{0})\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s}\Bigg].~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~`$ |  | (58) |
  - –
    for $`[\mathbf{Q}_{4,4}]_{s,s}`$ with $`s\in\tau(\mathcal{X})`$, we
    have
    $`[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}_{4,4}}\mathsf{Loss}_{5}^{2,2}]_{s,s}=\mathcal{N}_{s,4,2,i}+\mathcal{N}_{s,4,2,ii}+\mathcal{N}_{s,4,2,iii}`$,
    where

    |  |  |  |  |  |
    |----|----|----|----|----|
    |  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{s,4,2,i}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}\cdot(1-\mathbf{logit}_{5,j_{2}})\cdot`$ |  | (59) |
    |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~\Big(\sum_{r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j_{2}}}\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda_{5,j_{2},r})\cdot\Big(V_{j_{2},r}(y_{1})-\Lambda_{5,j_{2},r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s}\Bigg],`$ |  |  |

    |  |  |  |  |  |
    |----|----|----|----|----|
    |  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{s,4,2,ii}`$ | $`\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}\cdot\sum_{j\neq j_{2}\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})}\mathbf{logit}_{5,j}\cdot`$ |  | (60) |
    |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~\Big(\sum_{r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}}\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda_{5,j,r})\cdot\Big(V_{j,r}(y_{1})-\Lambda_{5,j,r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s}\Bigg],`$ |  |  |

    |  |  |  |  |  |
    |----|----|----|----|----|
    |  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{s,4,2,iii}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\pm\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}\cdot\sum_{j\notin\tau(\mathcal{Y})}\mathbf{logit}_{5,j}\widetilde{O}(\sigma^{q}_{0})\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s}\Bigg].~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~`$ |  | (61) |

##### Probabilistic Events.

We conclude this subsection by introducing several probabilistic events
that will be used to simplify the characterization of activated neurons
in the subsequent analysis.

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{1}`$ | $`\displaystyle\triangleq\Big\{g_{1}\not=g_{2}\Big\},`$ |  | (62) |
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{2}`$ | $`\displaystyle\triangleq\Big\{g_{\ell_{1}}(y_{\ell^{\prime}_{1}})\not=g_{\ell_{2}}(y_{\ell^{\prime}_{2}}),\text{ for any }(\ell_{1},\ell^{\prime}_{1})\not=(\ell_{2},\ell^{\prime}_{2}),\text{ where }\ell_{k}\in[2],\ell^{\prime}_{k}\in\{0,1\}\Big\}.`$ |  | (63) |

It is easy to see that $`\mathcal{E}_{1}`$ and $`\mathcal{E}_{2}`$ hold
with high probability $`1-O\big(\frac{1}{\log d}\big)`$.

### D.3 Stage 2.1: Initial Growth of Gap

At the beginning of stage 2, since $`\mathbf{Q}`$ has not been trained
for long, we have the attention score is still close to the uniform
structure. Therefore, for $`\ell=1`$, we have

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\Lambda_{5,j,r}(\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1})\approx\frac{1}{3}V_{j,r}(g_{1})+\frac{1}{3}V_{j,r}(g_{2})+\frac{1}{3}V_{j,r}(y_{0})\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0}).`$ |  |

If $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1}\in\mathcal{E}_{1}`$,

- •
  for $`j=j_{1}`$, for $`r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j_{1}}`$, only
  $`r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}`$ is activated since
  $`\Lambda_{5,j_{1},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}\approx\frac{1}{3}B`$ and
  $`\Lambda_{5,j_{1},r_{g_{2}\cdot g_{2}^{-1}(j_{1})}}\approx-\frac{1}{3}B\ll-\varrho`$;
- •
  for $`j=j^{\prime}_{1}\triangleq\tau\big(g_{2}(y_{0})\big)`$, only
  $`r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}`$ is activated since
  $`\Lambda_{5,j^{\prime}_{1},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}\approx\frac{1}{3}B`$
  and
  $`\Lambda_{5,j^{\prime}_{1},r_{g_{1}\cdot g_{2}^{-1}(j^{\prime}_{1})}}\approx-\frac{1}{3}B\ll-\varrho`$;
- •
  for other $`j\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})`$, we have
  $`\Lambda_{5,j,r}\leq-\frac{1}{3}B`$ for all
  $`r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}`$, thus no activation.

Moreover, for $`\ell=2`$, we have

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\Lambda_{5,j,r}(\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1})\approx\frac{1}{4}V_{j,r}(g_{1})+\frac{1}{4}V_{j,r}(g_{2})+\frac{1}{4}V_{j,r}(y_{0})+\frac{1}{4}V_{j,r}(y_{1})\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0}).`$ |  |

If $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1}\in\mathcal{E}_{2}`$,

- •
  for
  $`j\in\{\tau(g_{\ell}(y_{\ell^{\prime}}))\}_{\ell\in[2],\ell^{\prime}\in\{0,1\}}`$,
  only the corresponding $`r_{g_{\ell}\cdot y_{\ell^{\prime}}}`$ is
  activated in the smoothed regime since
  $`|\Lambda_{5,j,r_{g_{\ell}\cdot y_{\ell^{\prime}}}}|=O(\delta)`$ and
  $`\Lambda_{5,j,r}\approx-\frac{1}{2}B\ll-\varrho`$ for
  $`r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}\setminus\{r_{g_{\ell}\cdot y_{\ell^{\prime}}}\}`$;
- •
  for other $`j\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})`$, we have
  $`\Lambda_{5,j,r}\leq-\frac{1}{2}B`$ for all
  $`r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}`$, thus no activation.

Here, activation means that the corresponding
$`\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda_{5,j,r})`$ is non-zero, which is
crucial for the gradient computation. Based on the above observations,
we can see that the gradient from $`\ell=2`$ is relatively small since
$`\Lambda`$ is only activated in the smoothed regime. Thus, initially,
the learning process is dominated by
$`\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}}\mathsf{Loss}^{2,1}_{5}`$. Moreover, if we take a
closer look at the gradient from $`\ell=1`$, we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{s,3,1,ii}`$ | $`\displaystyle\approx-\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}_{{\mathsf{ans},0}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}\cdot\mathbf{logit}_{5,j^{\prime}_{1}}\cdot`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\Big(\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda_{5,j^{\prime}_{1},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}})\cdot\Big(V_{j^{\prime}_{1},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}(g_{1})-\Lambda_{5,j^{\prime}_{1},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{0})=s}\Bigg]\geq 0,`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{s,4,1,ii}`$ | $`\displaystyle\approx-\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}_{{\mathsf{ans},0}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot\mathbf{logit}_{5,j^{\prime}_{1}}\cdot`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\Big(\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda_{5,j^{\prime}_{1},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}})\cdot\Big(V_{j^{\prime}_{1},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}(y_{0})-\Lambda_{5,j^{\prime}_{1},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\leq 0,`$ |  |

since $`V_{j^{\prime}_{1},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}(y_{0})\geq\Omega(B)`$
while $`V_{j^{\prime}_{1},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}(g_{1})\leq-\Omega(B)`$.
Thus, $`[\mathbf{Q}_{4,3}]_{s,s}`$ will have a significant positive
gradient while $`[\mathbf{Q}_{4,4}]_{s,s}`$ will have a negative
counterpart. This will lead to the growth of the gap between
$`[\mathbf{Q}_{4,3}]_{s,s}`$ and $`[\mathbf{Q}_{4,4}]_{s,s}`$.

We formally characterize this growth behavior within this substage. At
the beginning of each sub-stage, we establish an induction hypothesis
that we expect to hold throughout. Subsequently, we analyze the dynamics
under this hypothesis within the substage, aiming to prove its validity
by the end of sub-stage. Due to the symmetry of
$`[\mathbf{Q}_{4,3}]_{s,s}`$ and $`[\mathbf{Q}_{4,4}]_{s,s}`$ across
$`s\in\tau(\mathcal{X})`$, we may, without loss of generality, focus on
a particular $`s\in\tau(\mathcal{X})`$.

###### Induction D.1.

Given $`s\in\tau(\mathcal{X})`$, let $`T_{2,1,s}`$ denote the first time
that $`[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}]_{s,s}`$ reaches
$`\Omega\Big(\frac{1}{\log d}\Big)`$. For all iterations
$`t<T_{2,1,s}`$, we have the following holds

1.  1.
    $`[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}]_{s,s}`$ monotonically increases;
2.  2.
    $`\big|[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,4}]_{s,s}\big|\leq[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}]_{s,s}`$
    and
    $`[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}]_{s,s}-[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,4}]_{s,s}=\Theta\Big([\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}]_{s,s}\Big)`$;
3.  3.
    for $`p\in\{3,4\}`$, for $`s^{\prime}\in\tau(\mathcal{X})\not=s`$,
    $`|[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,p}]_{s,s^{\prime}}|\leq O\Big(\frac{[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,p}]_{s,s}}{d}\Big)`$.

#### D.3.1 Attention and Logit Preliminaries

We first introduce several properties of the attention scores and logits
if
[Induction D.1](#A4.Thminduction1 "Induction D.1. ‣ D.3 Stage 2.1: Initial Growth of Gap ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
holds.

###### Lemma D.10.

If
[Induction D.1](#A4.Thminduction1 "Induction D.1. ‣ D.3 Stage 2.1: Initial Growth of Gap ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
holds for all iterations $`<t`$, given input $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1}`$,
then we have

1.  1.
    for $`\ell=1`$,

    1.  (a)
        $`\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},0\to\mathsf{pred},1}\in\Big[\frac{1}{3},\frac{1}{3}+O\big(\frac{1}{\log d}\big)\Big]`$;
    2.  (b)
        $`\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},0\to\mathsf{pred},2},\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},0\to\mathsf{ans},0}\leq\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},0\to\mathsf{pred},1}`$;
    3.  (c)
        $`\big|\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},0\to\mathbf{k}}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},0\to\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}\big|\leq O(\frac{1}{\log d})`$
        for
        $`\mathbf{k}\not=\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\in\mathcal{I}^{(2,0)}`$.
2.  2.
    for $`\ell=2`$,

    1.  (a)
        $`\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}\in\Big[\frac{1}{4},\frac{1}{4}+O\big(\frac{1}{\log d}\big)\Big]`$;
    2.  (b)
        $`\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathbf{k}}\leq\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}`$
        for $`\mathbf{k}\not=(\mathsf{pred},2)`$;
    3.  (c)
        for
        $`\mathbf{k}\in\big\{(\mathsf{ans},0),(\mathsf{pred},1)\big\}`$,
        $`\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\in\big\{(\mathsf{ans},1),(\mathsf{pred},2)\big\}`$,

        |  |  |  |
        |----|----|----|
        |  |
        ``` math
        \big|\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathbf{k}}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}\big|\leq O\Big(\frac{1}{\log d}\Big);
        ``` |  |
    4.  (d)
        $`\big|\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},0}\big|\leq O\big(\frac{1}{d}\big)`$.

###### Proof.

For $`\ell=1`$, given $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1}`$, according to
[Assumption 3.5](#S3.Thmassumption5 "Assumption 3.5 (Block-sparse attention matrix). ‣ Additional Technical Assumptions. ‣ 3.3 LEGO Task via CoT Reasoning and Training Objective ‣ 3 Problem Formulation ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},0\to\mathsf{pred},1}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\frac{e^{[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}]_{\tau(x_{0}),\tau(x_{0})}}}{e^{[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}]_{\tau(x_{0}),\tau(x_{0})}}+e^{[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}]_{\tau(x_{0}),\tau(x_{1})}}+e^{[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,4}]_{\tau(x_{0}),\tau(x_{0})}}}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\frac{1}{1+e^{[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}]_{\tau(x_{0}),\tau(x_{1})}-[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}]_{\tau(x_{0}),\tau(x_{0})}}+e^{[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,4}]_{\tau(x_{0}),\tau(x_{0})}-[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}]_{\tau(x_{0}),\tau(x_{0})}}}.`$ |  |

Thus, by
[Induction D.1](#A4.Thminduction1 "Induction D.1. ‣ D.3 Stage 2.1: Initial Growth of Gap ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
-O\Big(\frac{1}{\log d}\Big)\leq[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}]_{\tau(x_{0}),\tau(x_{1})}-[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}]_{\tau(x_{0}),\tau(x_{0})},[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,4}]_{\tau(x_{0}),\tau(x_{0})}-[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}]_{\tau(x_{0}),\tau(x_{0})}\leq 0,
``` |  |

which implies that
$`0\leq\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},0\to\mathsf{pred},1}\leq\frac{1}{3}+O\big(\frac{1}{\log d}\big)`$.
(b) is straightforward since

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}]_{\tau(x_{0}),\tau(x_{1})},[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,4}]_{\tau(x_{0}),\tau(x_{0})}\leq[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}]_{\tau(x_{0}),\tau(x_{0})}.
``` |  |

(c) is a direct consequence of (a) and (b).

For $`\ell=2`$, given $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1}`$, (a)- (c) are very
similar to the above analysis, and then for (d), we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},0}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~=\frac{e^{[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}]_{\tau(x_{1}),\tau(x_{0})}}-e^{[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,4}]_{\tau(x_{1}),\tau(x_{0})}}}{e^{[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}]_{\tau(x_{1}),\tau(x_{0})}}+e^{[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}]_{\tau(x_{1}),\tau(x_{1})}}+e^{[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,4}]_{\tau(x_{1}),\tau(x_{0})}}+e^{[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,4}]_{\tau(x_{1}),\tau(x_{1})}}}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(i)}}{{\leq}}O\Big([\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}]_{\tau(x_{1}),\tau(x_{0})}-[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,4}]_{\tau(x_{1}),\tau(x_{0})}\Big)\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(ii)}}{{\leq}}O(\frac{1}{d}),`$ |  |

where (i) is due to the fact that $`|e^{x}-e^{y}|\leq O(|x-y|)`$ when
$`x,y`$ are small, and (ii) is due to
[Induction D.1](#A4.Thminduction1 "Induction D.1. ‣ D.3 Stage 2.1: Initial Growth of Gap ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
(b). ∎

###### Lemma D.11.

If
[Induction D.1](#A4.Thminduction1 "Induction D.1. ‣ D.3 Stage 2.1: Initial Growth of Gap ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
holds for all iterations $`<t`$, given input $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1}`$,
then we have

1.  1.
    for $`\ell=1`$, if $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1}\in\mathcal{E}_{1}`$, then

    1.  (a)
        for $`j=j_{1}`$, $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{1},r}\ll-\varrho`$ for
        $`r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j_{1}}\setminus\{r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}\}`$;
    2.  (b)
        for $`j=j^{\prime}_{1}\triangleq\tau\big(g_{2}(y_{0})\big)`$,
        $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{1},r}\ll-\varrho`$ for
        $`r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j^{\prime}_{1}}\setminus\{r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}\}`$;
    3.  (c)
        for other $`j\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})`$, $`r`$ is not activated for
        all $`r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}`$, i.e.,
        $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r}\ll-\varrho`$.
2.  2.
    $`\ell=2`$, if $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1}\in\mathcal{E}_{2}`$, then

    1.  (a)
        for
        $`j\in\{\tau(g_{\ell}(y_{\ell^{\prime}}))\}_{\ell\in[2],\ell^{\prime}\in\{0,1\}}`$,
        only the corresponding $`r_{g_{\ell}\cdot y_{\ell^{\prime}}}`$
        may be activated, with
        $`|\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r_{g_{\ell}\cdot y_{\ell^{\prime}}}}|\leq O(1)`$,
        while all other $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r}\ll-\varrho`$ for
        $`r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}\setminus\{r_{g_{\ell}\cdot y_{\ell^{\prime}}}\}`$;
    2.  (b)
        for other $`j\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})`$, $`r`$ is not activated for
        all $`r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}`$, i.e.,
        $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r}\ll-\varrho`$.

###### Proof.

We only prove (a) for $`\ell=2`$ since the other cases are
straightforward. $`j=\tau(g_{\ell}(y_{\ell^{\prime}}))`$, by
[Lemma D.6](#A4.Thmlemma6 "Lemma D.6 (Characterizations of Lambda). ‣ Notations for activated neurons. ‣ D.2 Some Useful Bounds for Gradients ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\Lambda_{5,j,r_{g_{\ell}\cdot y_{\ell^{\prime}}}}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},1}V_{j,r_{g_{\ell}\cdot y_{\ell^{\prime}}}}(g_{1})+\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}V_{j,r_{g_{\ell}\cdot y_{\ell^{\prime}}}}(g_{2})`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},0}V_{j,r_{g_{\ell}\cdot y_{\ell^{\prime}}}}(y_{0})+\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},1}V_{j,r_{g_{\ell}\cdot y_{\ell^{\prime}}}}(y_{1})\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0}).`$ |  |

Notice that since $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1}\in\mathcal{E}_{2}`$, we have
two $`V`$ terms are positive and two are negative with magnitude
$`B\pm O(\delta)`$. Therefore,
$`|\Lambda_{5,j,r_{g_{\ell}\cdot y_{\ell^{\prime}}}}|\leq O\Big(\frac{1}{\log d}\Big)\cdot B=O(1)`$.
∎

###### Lemma D.12.

If
[Induction D.1](#A4.Thminduction1 "Induction D.1. ‣ D.3 Stage 2.1: Initial Growth of Gap ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
holds for all iterations $`<t`$, given input
$`\mathbf{Z}^{2,0}\in\mathcal{E}_{1}`$, then we have

1.  1.
    $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{1},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}\geq\frac{1}{3}B-O(1)`$;
2.  2.
    $`-O(\delta)\leq\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{1},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{1},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}\leq O(1)`$;

###### Proof.

By
[Lemma D.6](#A4.Thmlemma6 "Lemma D.6 (Characterizations of Lambda). ‣ Notations for activated neurons. ‣ D.2 Some Useful Bounds for Gradients ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\Lambda_{5,j_{1},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}=`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},0\to\mathsf{pred},1}V_{j_{1},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}(g_{1})+\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},0\to\mathsf{pred},2}V_{j_{1},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}(g_{2})+\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},0\to\mathsf{ans},0}V_{j,r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}(y_{0})\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0}).`$ |  |

By
[Lemma D.10](#A4.Thmlemma10 "Lemma D.10. ‣ D.3.1 Attention and Logit Preliminaries ‣ D.3 Stage 2.1: Initial Growth of Gap ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
and the cancellation in
([44](#A4.E44 "Equation 44 ‣ Lemma D.3 (Properties of target feature magnitude). ‣ Notations for activated neurons. ‣ D.2 Some Useful Bounds for Gradients ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")),
we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},0\to\mathsf{pred},2}V_{j_{1},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}(g_{2})+\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},0\to\mathsf{ans},0}V_{j_{1},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}(y_{0})`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~\geq\Big(\frac{1}{3}-O\Big(\frac{1}{\log d}\Big)\Big)\cdot O(\delta)-O\Big(\frac{1}{\log d}\Big)\cdot B.`$ |  |

Putting it back, and using the fact that
$`\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},0\to\mathsf{pred},1}V_{j_{1},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}(g_{1})\geq\frac{1}{3}\cdot(B-O(\delta))`$,
we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\Lambda_{5,j_{1},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}`$ | $`\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{3}\cdot(B-O(\delta))+\Big(\frac{1}{3}-O\Big(\frac{1}{\log d}\Big)\Big)\cdot O(\delta)-O\Big(\frac{1}{\log d}\Big)\cdot B\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{3}B-O(1).`$ |  |

Moving on to (b), we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\Lambda_{5,j_{1},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}-\Lambda_{5,j^{\prime}_{1},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}=\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},0\to\mathsf{pred},1}(V_{j_{1},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}(g_{1})-V_{j^{\prime}_{1},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}(g_{1}))\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},0\to\mathsf{pred},2}(V_{j_{1},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}(g_{2})-V_{j^{\prime}_{1},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}(g_{2}))+\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},0\to\mathsf{ans},0}(V_{j_{1},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}(y_{0})-V_{j^{\prime}_{1},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}(y_{0}))`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},0\to\mathsf{pred},1}\cdot(2B+O(\delta))-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},0\to\mathsf{pred},2}\cdot(2B-O(\delta))+\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},0\to\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot O(\delta)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq(2B-O(\delta))\cdot O\Big(\frac{1}{\log d}\Big)+O(\delta)\leq O(1).`$ |  |

Similarly, we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\Lambda_{5,j_{1},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}-\Lambda_{5,j^{\prime}_{1},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},0\to\mathsf{pred},1}\cdot(2B-O(\delta))-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},0\to\mathsf{pred},2}\cdot(2B+O(\delta))-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},0\to\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot O(\delta)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},0\to\mathsf{pred},2}\cdot O(\delta)-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},0\to\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot O(\delta)\geq-O(\delta).`$ |  |

∎

###### Lemma D.13.

If
[Induction D.1](#A4.Thminduction1 "Induction D.1. ‣ D.3 Stage 2.1: Initial Growth of Gap ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
holds for all iterations $`<t`$, given input $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1}`$,
then we have

1.  1.
    for $`\ell=1`$, if $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1}\in\mathcal{E}_{1}`$,
    $`\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j}=\Omega(1)`$ for
    $`j\in\{j_{1},j_{1}^{\prime}\}`$,
    $`1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{1}}-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{1}^{\prime}}=\frac{1}{\mathsf{poly}d}`$.
2.  2.
    for $`\ell=2`$, if $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1}\in\mathcal{E}_{2}`$,
    $`\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j}=O(\frac{1}{d})`$ for all $`j`$.

###### Proof.

- •
  For $`\ell=1`$, by
  [Lemma D.11](#A4.Thmlemma11 "Lemma D.11. ‣ D.3.1 Attention and Logit Preliminaries ‣ D.3 Stage 2.1: Initial Growth of Gap ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
  and
  [Lemma D.6](#A4.Thmlemma6 "Lemma D.6 (Characterizations of Lambda). ‣ Notations for activated neurons. ‣ D.2 Some Useful Bounds for Gradients ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
  we have

  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle F^{(t)}_{5,j_{1}}(\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1})=\sum_{r\in[m]}\mathbf{sReLU}\big(\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{1},r}\big)=\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{1},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}+\varrho(m/q-1)=\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{1},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}+O\Big(\frac{1}{\mathrm{polylog}d}\Big),`$ |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle F^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{1}}(\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1})=\sum_{r\in[m]}\mathbf{sReLU}\big(\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{1},r}\big)=\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{1},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}+\varrho(m/q-1),`$ |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle F^{(t)}_{5,j}(\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1})=\sum_{r\in[m]}\mathbf{sReLU}\big(\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r}\big)\leq O\Big(\frac{1}{\mathrm{polylog}d}\Big)\text{ for }j\not=j_{1},j^{\prime}_{1}\in\tau(\mathcal{Y}),`$ |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle F^{(t)}_{5,j}(\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1})\leq m\cdot\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0}^{q})\text{ for }j\notin\tau(\mathcal{Y}).`$ |  |

  Putting it together, we obtain

  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|
  |  | $`\displaystyle\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{1}}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\frac{1}{1+e^{F^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{1}}-F^{(t)}_{5,j_{1}}}+\Big(\sum_{j\not=j_{1},j^{\prime}_{1}\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})}e^{F^{(t)}_{5,j}}+\sum_{j\notin\tau(\mathcal{Y})}e^{F^{(t)}_{5,j}}\Big)\cdot e^{-F^{(t)}_{5,j_{1}}}}`$ |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle=\frac{1}{1+e^{\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{1},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{1},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}}+\Big(O(\log d)\cdot e^{O(\frac{1}{\mathrm{polylog}d})}+O(d)\cdot e^{\widetilde{O}(m\sigma_{0}^{q})}\Big)\cdot e^{-F^{(t)}_{5,j_{1}}}}.`$ |  |

  Thus, by
  [Lemma D.12](#A4.Thmlemma12 "Lemma D.12. ‣ D.3.1 Attention and Logit Preliminaries ‣ D.3 Stage 2.1: Initial Growth of Gap ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
  and the fact that $`B=C_{B}\log d`$ for some sufficiently large
  constant $`C_{B}>0`$, we have
  $`\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{1}}=\frac{1}{1+e^{-O(\delta)}+O(1)\cdot e^{-(C_{B}/3-1)\log d}}=\Omega(1)`$.
  Similarly, we have

  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|
  |  | $`\displaystyle\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{1}}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\frac{1}{1+e^{-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{1},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}+\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{1},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}}+\Big(O(\log d)\cdot e^{O(\frac{1}{\mathrm{polylog}d})}+O(d)\cdot e^{\widetilde{O}(m\sigma_{0}^{q})}\Big)\cdot e^{-F^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{1}}}}`$ |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle=\frac{1}{1+e^{O(1)}+O(1)\cdot e^{-(C_{B}/3-1)\log d}}=\Omega(1).`$ |  |

  From the above analysis, it is easy to see that
  $`1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{1}}-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{1}}\leq O\Big(\frac{1}{e^{(C_{B}/3-1)\log d}}\Big)=O(\frac{1}{\mathsf{poly}d})`$.
- •
  For $`\ell=2`$, by
  [Lemma D.11](#A4.Thmlemma11 "Lemma D.11. ‣ D.3.1 Attention and Logit Preliminaries ‣ D.3 Stage 2.1: Initial Growth of Gap ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
  and
  [Lemma D.6](#A4.Thmlemma6 "Lemma D.6 (Characterizations of Lambda). ‣ Notations for activated neurons. ‣ D.2 Some Useful Bounds for Gradients ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
  we have

  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle F^{(t)}_{5,j}(\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1})=\sum_{r\in[m]}\mathbf{sReLU}\big(\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{1},r}\big)\in[\varrho m/q,O(1)+\varrho(m/q-1)]\text{ for }j\in\{\tau(g_{\ell}(y_{\ell^{\prime}}))\}_{\ell\in[2],\ell^{\prime}\in\{0,1\}}`$ |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle F^{(t)}_{5,j}(\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1})=\sum_{r\in[m]}\mathbf{sReLU}\big(\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r}\big)\leq O\Big(\frac{1}{\mathrm{polylog}d}\Big)\text{ for }j\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})\setminus\{\tau(g_{\ell}(y_{\ell^{\prime}}))\}_{\ell\in[2],\ell^{\prime}\in\{0,1\}}`$ |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle F^{(t)}_{5,j}(\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1})\leq m\cdot\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0}^{q})\text{ for }j\notin\tau(\mathcal{Y}).`$ |  |

  Therefore, for any $`j`$, we have
  $`\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{j}=O\big(\frac{1}{d}\big)`$ since
  $`F^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}}\leq O(1)`$ for all $`j^{\prime}`$.

∎

In the following, we illustrate the activations on the non-high
probability event.

###### Lemma D.14.

If
[Induction D.1](#A4.Thminduction1 "Induction D.1. ‣ D.3 Stage 2.1: Initial Growth of Gap ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
holds for all iterations $`<t`$, given input $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1}`$,
then we have

1.  1.
    for $`\ell=1`$, if $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1}\notin\mathcal{E}_{1}`$,
    then

    1.  (a)
        for $`j=j_{1}`$,
        $`\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j_{1}}=\{r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}\}`$,
        $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{1},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}=B\pm O(\delta)`$;
    2.  (b)
        for $`j\neq j_{1}\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})`$, assuming
        $`j=\tau(g_{1}(y))`$, then
        $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r_{g_{1}\cdot y}}=\frac{1}{3}B\pm O(1)`$
        and $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r}\ll-\varrho`$ for
        $`r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}\setminus\{r_{g_{1}\cdot y}\}`$.
2.  2.
    $`\ell=2`$, if $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1}\notin\mathcal{E}_{2}`$, then

    1.  (a)
        if $`g_{1}=g_{2}\wedge y_{0}\neq y_{1}`$,

        1.  i.
            for $`j=j_{2}`$,
            $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}=\frac{1}{2}B\pm O(1)`$
            and $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r}\ll-\varrho`$ for
            $`r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j_{2}}\setminus\{r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}\}`$;
        2.  ii.
            for $`j=\tau(g_{2}(y_{0}))`$,
            $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}=\frac{1}{2}B\pm O(1)`$
            and $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r}\ll-\varrho`$ for
            $`r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}\setminus\{r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}\}`$;
        3.  iii.
            for other $`j\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})`$, assuming
            $`j=\tau(g_{2}(y))`$,
            $`|\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r_{g_{2}\cdot y}}|\leq O(1)`$ and
            $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r}\ll-\varrho`$ for
            $`r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}\setminus\{r_{g_{2}\cdot y}\}`$.
    2.  (b)
        if $`g_{1}\neq g_{2}\wedge y_{0}=y_{1}`$,

        1.  i.
            for $`j=j_{2}`$,
            $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}=\frac{1}{2}B\pm O(1)`$
            and $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r}\ll-\varrho`$ for
            $`r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j_{2}}\setminus\{r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}\}`$;
        2.  ii.
            for $`j=\tau(g_{1}(y_{1}))`$,
            $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{1}}}=\frac{1}{2}B\pm O(1)`$
            and $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r}\ll-\varrho`$ for
            $`r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}\setminus\{r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{1}}\}`$;
        3.  iii.
            for other $`j\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})`$, assuming
            $`j=\tau(g(y_{1}))`$,
            $`|\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r_{g\cdot y_{1}}}|\leq O(1)`$ and
            $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r}\ll-\varrho`$ for
            $`r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}\setminus\{r_{g\cdot y_{1}}\}`$.
    3.  (c)
        if $`g_{1}=g_{2}\wedge y_{0}=y_{1}`$,

        1.  i.
            for $`j=j_{2}`$,
            $`\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j_{2}}=\{r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}\}`$,
            $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}=B\pm O(\delta)`$
            ;
        2.  ii.
            for $`j\neq j_{2}\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})`$,
            $`|\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r}|\leq O(1)`$ for all
            $`r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}`$.
    4.  (d)
        $`g_{1}\neq g_{2}\wedge y_{0}\neq y_{1}\wedge\Big(g_{1}(y_{0})=g_{2}(y_{1})\vee g_{2}(y_{0})=g_{1}(y_{1})\Big)`$,
        $`|\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r}|\leq O(1)`$ for all
        $`r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}`$.

With the characterization of activated neurons, we can derive the
following logits for the non-high probability event.

###### Lemma D.15.

If
[Induction D.1](#A4.Thminduction1 "Induction D.1. ‣ D.3 Stage 2.1: Initial Growth of Gap ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
holds for all iterations $`<t`$, given input $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1}`$,
then we have

1.  1.
    for $`\ell=1`$, if $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1}\notin\mathcal{E}_{1}`$,
    then
    $`1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{1}}=O\Big(\frac{1}{\mathsf{poly}d}\Big)`$.
2.  2.
    $`\ell=2`$, if $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1}\notin\mathcal{E}_{2}`$, then

    1.  (a)
        if $`g_{1}=g_{2}\wedge y_{0}\neq y_{1}`$,
        $`\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j}=\Omega(1)`$ for
        $`j\in\{j_{2},\tau(g_{2}(y_{0}))\}`$,
        $`1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}}-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{2}(y_{0}))}=\frac{1}{\mathsf{poly}d}`$.
    2.  (b)
        if $`g_{1}\neq g_{2}\wedge y_{0}=y_{1}`$,
        $`\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j}=\Omega(1)`$ for
        $`j\in\{j_{2},\tau(g_{1}(y_{1}))\}`$,
        $`1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}}-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{1}(y_{1}))}=\frac{1}{\mathsf{poly}d}`$.
    3.  (c)
        if $`g_{1}=g_{2}\wedge y_{0}=y_{1}`$,
        $`1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}}=O\Big(\frac{1}{\mathsf{poly}d}\Big)`$.
    4.  (d)
        $`g_{1}\neq g_{2}\wedge y_{0}\neq y_{1}\wedge\Big(g_{1}(y_{0})=g_{2}(y_{1})\vee g_{2}(y_{0})=g_{1}(y_{1})\Big)`$,
        $`\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}}=O(\frac{1}{d})`$ for all $`j`$.

#### D.3.2 Gradient Lemma

###### Lemma D.16.

If
[Induction D.1](#A4.Thminduction1 "Induction D.1. ‣ D.3 Stage 2.1: Initial Growth of Gap ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
holds for all iterations $`<t`$, given $`s\in\tau(\mathcal{X})`$, for
$`[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}]_{s,s}`$, we have

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=1}^{2}\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}}\mathsf{Loss}^{2,\ell}_{5}\Big]_{s,s}=\Theta\Big(\frac{\log d}{d}\Big).`$ |  |

###### Proof.

By gradient decompositions, we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=1}^{2}\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}}\mathsf{Loss}^{2,\ell}_{5}\Big]_{s,s}=\sum_{\ell\in[2]}\sum_{\kappa\in\{i,ii,iii\}}\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,3,\ell,\kappa}.`$ |  |

By
[Lemma D.13](#A4.Thmlemma13 "Lemma D.13. ‣ D.3.1 Attention and Logit Preliminaries ‣ D.3 Stage 2.1: Initial Growth of Gap ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
and
[Lemma D.15](#A4.Thmlemma15 "Lemma D.15. ‣ D.3.1 Attention and Logit Preliminaries ‣ D.3 Stage 2.1: Initial Growth of Gap ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
it is straightforward to see that $`|\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,3,1,iii}|`$,
$`|\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,3,2,iii}|=O(\frac{1}{\mathsf{poly}d})`$, and
thus we can focus on other terms.

By
[Lemma D.11](#A4.Thmlemma11 "Lemma D.11. ‣ D.3.1 Attention and Logit Preliminaries ‣ D.3 Stage 2.1: Initial Growth of Gap ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
and
[Lemma D.14](#A4.Thmlemma14 "Lemma D.14. ‣ D.3.1 Attention and Logit Preliminaries ‣ D.3 Stage 2.1: Initial Growth of Gap ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,3,1,i}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},0}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}\cdot(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{1}})\cdot`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~\Big(\Big(V_{j_{1},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}(g_{1})-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{1},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{0})=s\}\cap\mathcal{E}_{1}}\Bigg]`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},0}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}\cdot(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{1}})\cdot`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~\Big(\Big(V_{j_{1},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}(g_{1})-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{1},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{0})=s\}\cap\mathcal{E}^{c}_{1}}\Bigg]`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(a)}}{{=}}\Theta(\frac{1}{d})\cdot\Omega(1)\cdot\Theta(B)+\Theta(\frac{1}{d})\cdot O(\frac{1}{\mathsf{poly}d})\cdot\Theta(B)\cdot O(\frac{1}{\log d})`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\Theta\Big(\frac{\log d}{d}\Big),`$ |  |

where (a) follows from
[Lemma D.12](#A4.Thmlemma12 "Lemma D.12. ‣ D.3.1 Attention and Logit Preliminaries ‣ D.3 Stage 2.1: Initial Growth of Gap ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
[Lemma D.13](#A4.Thmlemma13 "Lemma D.13. ‣ D.3.1 Attention and Logit Preliminaries ‣ D.3 Stage 2.1: Initial Growth of Gap ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
[Lemma D.14](#A4.Thmlemma14 "Lemma D.14. ‣ D.3.1 Attention and Logit Preliminaries ‣ D.3 Stage 2.1: Initial Growth of Gap ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
and
[Lemma D.15](#A4.Thmlemma15 "Lemma D.15. ‣ D.3.1 Attention and Logit Preliminaries ‣ D.3 Stage 2.1: Initial Growth of Gap ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
and the fact that $`\tau(x_{0})=s`$ holds with probability
$`\frac{1}{d}`$. $`\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,3,2,i}`$ can be upper bounded
similarly.

Moving to $`\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,3,1,ii}`$, noticing that
$`V_{j^{\prime}_{1},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}(g_{1})=-B+O(\delta)`$ on
$`\mathcal{E}_{1}`$, we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,3,1,ii}`$ | $`\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},0}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}\cdot\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{1}}\cdot`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~\Big(\Big(V_{j^{\prime}_{1},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}(g_{1})-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{1},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{0})=s\}\cap\mathcal{E}_{1}}\Bigg]`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle-\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},0}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}\cdot\sum_{y\neq y_{0}\in\mathcal{Y}}\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{1}(y))}\cdot`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~\Big(\Big(V_{\tau(g_{1}(y)),r_{g_{1}\cdot y}}(g_{1})-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{1}(y)),r_{g_{1}\cdot y}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{0})=s\}\cap\mathcal{E}^{c}_{1}}\Bigg]`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\Theta(\frac{1}{d})\cdot\Omega(1)\cdot\Theta(B)-\Theta(\frac{1}{d})\cdot O(\frac{1}{\mathsf{poly}d})\cdot\Theta(B)\cdot O(\frac{1}{\log d})`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\Theta\Big(\frac{\log d}{d}\Big).`$ |  |

For $`\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,3,2,ii}`$, we only need to control the
negative gradient, since the positive part can be easily upper bounded
by $`O\Big(\frac{\log d}{d}\Big).`$

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,3,2,ii}`$ | $`\displaystyle\geq-\Theta(\frac{1}{d})\cdot O(\frac{1}{d})\cdot\Theta(B)-\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\cdot\sum_{y\neq y_{1}\in\mathcal{Y}}\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{2}(y))}\cdot`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~\Big(\Big(V_{\tau(g_{2}(y)),r_{g_{2}\cdot y}}(g_{2})-\Lambda_{5,j,r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{0})=s,g_{1}=g_{2},y_{0}\neq y_{1}\}}\Bigg]`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(a)}}{{\geq}}-\Theta(\frac{1}{d})\cdot O(\frac{1}{d})\cdot\Theta(B)-\Theta(\frac{1}{d})\cdot O(1)\cdot\Theta(B)\cdot O(\frac{1}{\log d})`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq-O\Big(\frac{1}{d}\Big),`$ |  |

where (a) is due to the fact that
$`\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{2}(y_{0}))}=\Omega(1)`$ and
$`\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{2}(y))}=O(\frac{1}{d})`$ for other
$`y\neq y_{1},y_{0}`$. Putting everything together, we complete the
proof. ∎

###### Lemma D.17 (Negative gradient).

If
[Induction D.1](#A4.Thminduction1 "Induction D.1. ‣ D.3 Stage 2.1: Initial Growth of Gap ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
holds for all iterations $`<t`$, given $`s\in\tau(\mathcal{X})`$, we
have

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=1}^{2}\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,4}}\mathsf{Loss}^{2,\ell}_{5}\Big]_{s,s}\geq-O\Big(\frac{1}{\log d}\Big)\sum_{\ell=1}^{2}\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}}\mathsf{Loss}^{2,\ell}_{5}\Big]_{s,s}.`$ |  |

###### Proof.

By gradient decompositions, we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=1}^{2}\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,4}}\mathsf{Loss}^{2,\ell}_{5}\Big]_{s,s}=\sum_{\ell\in[2]}\sum_{\kappa\in\{i,ii,iii\}}\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,4,\ell,\kappa}.`$ |  |

Similarly as
[Lemma D.16](#A4.Thmlemma16 "Lemma D.16. ‣ D.3.2 Gradient Lemma ‣ D.3 Stage 2.1: Initial Growth of Gap ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
$`|\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,4,1,iii}|`$,
$`|\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,4,2,iii}|=O(\frac{1}{\mathsf{poly}d})`$, and
thus we can focus on other terms. By
[Lemma D.11](#A4.Thmlemma11 "Lemma D.11. ‣ D.3.1 Attention and Logit Preliminaries ‣ D.3 Stage 2.1: Initial Growth of Gap ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
[Lemma D.12](#A4.Thmlemma12 "Lemma D.12. ‣ D.3.1 Attention and Logit Preliminaries ‣ D.3 Stage 2.1: Initial Growth of Gap ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
and
[Lemma D.14](#A4.Thmlemma14 "Lemma D.14. ‣ D.3.1 Attention and Logit Preliminaries ‣ D.3 Stage 2.1: Initial Growth of Gap ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
we have

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,4,1,i}+\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,4,1,ii}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},0}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{1}})\cdot`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~\Big(\Big(V_{j_{1},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}(y_{0})-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{1},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{0})=s\}\cap\mathcal{E}_{1}}\Bigg]`$ |  | (64) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},0}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{1}})\cdot`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~\Big(\Big(V_{j_{1},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}(y_{0})-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{1},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{0})=s\}\cap\mathcal{E}^{c}_{1}}\Bigg]`$ |  | (65) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle-\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},0}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{1}}\cdot`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~\Big(\Big(V_{j^{\prime}_{1},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}(y_{0})-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{1},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{0})=s\}\cap\mathcal{E}_{1}}\Bigg]`$ |  | (66) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle-\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},0}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot\sum_{g\neq g_{1}\in\mathcal{G}}\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g(y_{0}))}\cdot`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~\Big(\Big(V_{\tau(g(y_{0})),r_{g\cdot y_{0}}}(y_{0})-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g(y_{0})),r_{g\cdot y_{0}}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{0})=s\}\cap\mathcal{E}^{c}_{1}}\Bigg].`$ |  | (67) |

First, considering the event $`\{\tau(x_{0})=s\}\cap\mathcal{E}_{1}`$,
we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\eqref{eq-s21-gd2-1}+\eqref{eq-s21-gd2-3}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},0}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot\bigg((1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{1}})\cdot\Big(V_{j_{1},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{1}}}(y_{0})-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{1},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{1}^{\prime}}\cdot\Big(V_{j^{\prime}_{1},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}(y_{0})-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{1}^{\prime},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\bigg)\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{0})=s\}\cap\mathcal{E}_{1}}\Bigg]`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(a)}}{{=}}\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},0}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot\bigg((1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{1}})\cdot\Big(V_{j_{1},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{1}}}(y_{0})-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{1},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~-(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{1}}-\frac{1}{\mathsf{poly}d})\cdot\Big(V_{j^{\prime}_{1},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}(y_{0})-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{1}^{\prime},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\bigg)\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{0})=s\}\cap\mathcal{E}_{1}}\Bigg]`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq-\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},0}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot\bigg((1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{1}})\cdot`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~\Big(V_{j_{1},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{1}}}(y_{0})-V_{j^{\prime}_{1},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}(y_{0})+\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{1}^{\prime},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{1},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\bigg)\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{0})=s\}\cap\mathcal{E}_{1}}\Bigg]`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(b)}}{{\geq}}-\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},0}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot\bigg((1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{1}})\cdot\Big(O(\delta)+O(1)\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\bigg)\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{0})=s\}\cap\mathcal{E}_{1}}\Bigg]`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq-O\Big(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,3,1,i}}{\log d}\Big),`$ |  |

where (a) follows from
[Lemma D.13](#A4.Thmlemma13 "Lemma D.13. ‣ D.3.1 Attention and Logit Preliminaries ‣ D.3 Stage 2.1: Initial Growth of Gap ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization");
(b) follows from
[Lemma D.12](#A4.Thmlemma12 "Lemma D.12. ‣ D.3.1 Attention and Logit Preliminaries ‣ D.3 Stage 2.1: Initial Growth of Gap ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
and
[Lemma D.3](#A4.Thmlemma3 "Lemma D.3 (Properties of target feature magnitude). ‣ Notations for activated neurons. ‣ D.2 Some Useful Bounds for Gradients ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization").

Notice that $`\eqref{eq-s21-gd2-2}\geq 0`$ since
$`V_{j_{1},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}(y_{0})-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{1},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}\geq\Omega(B)`$,
thus we just need to consider the possible negative gradient from
([67](#A4.E67 "Equation 67 ‣ Proof. ‣ D.3.2 Gradient Lemma ‣ D.3 Stage 2.1: Initial Growth of Gap ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")).
By
[Lemma D.15](#A4.Thmlemma15 "Lemma D.15. ‣ D.3.1 Attention and Logit Preliminaries ‣ D.3 Stage 2.1: Initial Growth of Gap ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
we have
$`\sum_{g\neq g_{1}\in\mathcal{G}}\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g(y_{0}))}\leq O(\frac{1}{\mathsf{poly}d})`$
on $`\{\tau(x_{0})=s\}\cap\mathcal{E}^{c}_{1}`$, and hence
$`\eqref{eq-s21-gd2-4}\ll\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,3,1,i}`$.

Moving to the gradient from $`\ell=2`$, it is straightforward to see
that $`\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,4,2,i}`$ is non-negative, and thus we can
focus on the possible negative gradient from
$`\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,4,2,ii}`$.

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,4,2,ii}`$ | $`\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}\cdot\sum_{j\neq j_{2}\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})}\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j}\cdot`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~\Big(\sum_{r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}}\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r})\cdot\Big(V_{j,r}(y_{1})-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{1})=s\}\cap\mathcal{E}_{2}}\Bigg]`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle-\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}\cdot\sum_{j\neq j_{2}\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})}\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j}\cdot`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~\Big(\sum_{r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}}\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r})\cdot\Big(V_{j,r}(y_{1})-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{1})=s\}\cap\mathcal{E}^{c}_{2}}\Bigg]`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq-\Theta(\frac{1}{d})\cdot O(\frac{1}{d})\cdot\Theta(B)-\Theta(\frac{1}{d})\cdot O(1)\cdot\Theta(B)\cdot O(\frac{1}{\log d})\geq-O\Big(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,3,1,i}}{\log d}\Big).`$ |  |

Putting everything together, and combining with the fact that
$`\sum_{\ell=1}^{2}\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}}\mathsf{Loss}^{2,\ell}_{5}\Big]_{s,s}=\Theta(\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,3,1,i})`$
from
[Lemma D.16](#A4.Thmlemma16 "Lemma D.16. ‣ D.3.2 Gradient Lemma ‣ D.3 Stage 2.1: Initial Growth of Gap ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
we complete the proof. ∎

###### Lemma D.18 (Growth of gap).

If
[Induction D.1](#A4.Thminduction1 "Induction D.1. ‣ D.3 Stage 2.1: Initial Growth of Gap ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
holds for all iterations $`<t`$, given $`s\in\tau(\mathcal{X})`$, we
have

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=1}^{2}\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}}\mathsf{Loss}^{2,\ell}_{5}\Big]_{s,s}+\sum_{\ell=1}^{2}\Big[\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,4}}\mathsf{Loss}^{2,\ell}_{5}\Big]_{s,s}\geq\Omega\bigg(\sum_{\ell=1}^{2}\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}}\mathsf{Loss}^{2,\ell}_{5}\Big]_{s,s}\bigg).`$ |  |

###### Proof.

By gradient decompositions in
([50](#A4.E50 "Equation 50 ‣ 1st item ‣ 1st item ‣ Notations for gradient decompositions. ‣ D.2 Some Useful Bounds for Gradients ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"))-([61](#A4.E61 "Equation 61 ‣ 2nd item ‣ 2nd item ‣ Notations for gradient decompositions. ‣ D.2 Some Useful Bounds for Gradients ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")),
we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=1}^{2}\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}}\mathsf{Loss}^{2,\ell}_{5}\Big]_{s,s}+\sum_{\ell=1}^{2}\Big[\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,4}}\mathsf{Loss}^{2,\ell}_{5}\Big]_{s,s}=\sum_{\ell\in[2]}\sum_{\kappa\in\{i,ii,iii\}}\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,3,\ell,\kappa}-\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,4,\ell,\kappa}.`$ |  |

Due to
[Lemma D.13](#A4.Thmlemma13 "Lemma D.13. ‣ D.3.1 Attention and Logit Preliminaries ‣ D.3 Stage 2.1: Initial Growth of Gap ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
and
[Lemma D.15](#A4.Thmlemma15 "Lemma D.15. ‣ D.3.1 Attention and Logit Preliminaries ‣ D.3 Stage 2.1: Initial Growth of Gap ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
$`|\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,p,\ell,iii}|=O(\frac{1}{\mathsf{poly}d})`$ for
$`p\in\{3,4\}`$ and $`\ell\in[2]`$, we can focus on the gradient
difference between $`\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}`$ and
$`\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,4}`$ contributed by other terms.

By
[Lemma D.10](#A4.Thmlemma10 "Lemma D.10. ‣ D.3.1 Attention and Logit Preliminaries ‣ D.3 Stage 2.1: Initial Growth of Gap ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
we have

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},0}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\leq\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},0}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1},\quad\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}\leq\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}.`$ |  |

Hence, for $`\ell\in[2]`$, we have

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,3,\ell,i}-\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,4,\ell,i}\geq-O(\delta)\cdot O(\frac{1}{d})\cdot\Theta(1)\geq-O(\delta/d).`$ |  |

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,3,1,ii}-\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,4,1,ii}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},0}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}\cdot\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{1}}\cdot`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~\Big(\Big(V_{j^{\prime}_{1},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}(g_{1})-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{1},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{0})=s\}\cap\mathcal{E}_{1}}\Bigg]`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},0}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{1}}\cdot`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~\Big(\Big(V_{j^{\prime}_{1},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}(y_{0})-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{1},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{0})=s\}\cap\mathcal{E}_{1}}\Bigg]`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle-\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},0}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}\cdot\sum_{y\neq y_{0}\in\mathcal{Y}}\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{1}(y))}\cdot`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~\Big(\Big(V_{\tau(g_{1}(y)),r_{g_{1}\cdot y}}(g_{1})-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{1}(y)),r_{g_{1}\cdot y}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{0})=s\}\cap\mathcal{E}^{c}_{1}}\Bigg]`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},0}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot\sum_{y\neq y_{0}\in\mathcal{Y}}\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{1}(y))}\cdot`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~\Big(\Big(V_{\tau(g_{1}(y)),r_{g_{1}\cdot y}}(y_{0})-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{1}(y)),r_{g_{1}\cdot y}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{0})=s\}\cap\mathcal{E}^{c}_{1}}\Bigg]`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(a)}}{{\geq}}\Theta(\frac{1}{d})\cdot\Omega(1)\cdot\Theta(B)-\Theta(\frac{1}{d})\cdot O(\frac{1}{\mathsf{poly}d})\cdot O(B)\cdot O(\frac{1}{\log d})\geq\Omega\Big(\frac{\log d}{d}\Big),`$ |  |

where (a) follows from
[Lemma D.3](#A4.Thmlemma3 "Lemma D.3 (Properties of target feature magnitude). ‣ Notations for activated neurons. ‣ D.2 Some Useful Bounds for Gradients ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
that
$`V_{j^{\prime}_{1},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}(g_{1})-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{1},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}\leq-\Omega(B)`$,
and
$`V_{j^{\prime}_{1},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}(y_{0})-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{1},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}\geq\Omega(B)`$.

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,3,2,ii}-\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,4,2,ii}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\cdot\sum_{j\neq j_{2}\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})}\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j}\cdot`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~\Big(\sum_{r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}}\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r})\cdot\Big(V_{j,r}(g_{2})-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{1})=s\}\cap\mathcal{E}_{2}}\Bigg]`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}\cdot\sum_{j\neq j_{2}\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})}\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j}\cdot`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~\Big(\sum_{r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}}\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r})\cdot\Big(V_{j,r}(y_{1})-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{1})=s\}\cap\mathcal{E}_{2}}\Bigg]`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle-\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\cdot\sum_{j\neq j_{2}\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})}\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j}\cdot`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~\Big(\sum_{r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}}\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r})\cdot\Big(V_{j,r}(g_{2})-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{1})=s\}\cap\mathcal{E}^{c}_{2}}\Bigg]`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}\cdot\sum_{j\neq j_{2}\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})}\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j}\cdot`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~\Big(\sum_{r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}}\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r})\cdot\Big(V_{j,r}(y_{1})-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{1})=s\}\cap\mathcal{E}^{c}_{2}}\Bigg]`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(a)}}{{\geq}}-\Theta\Big(\frac{1}{d}\Big)\cdot O\Big(\frac{1}{d}\Big)\cdot\Theta(B)-\Theta\Big(\frac{1}{d}\Big)\cdot O(1)\cdot\Theta(B)\cdot O\Big(\frac{1}{\log d}\Big)\geq-O\Big(\frac{1}{d}\Big),`$ |  |

where (a) follows from
[Lemma D.11](#A4.Thmlemma11 "Lemma D.11. ‣ D.3.1 Attention and Logit Preliminaries ‣ D.3 Stage 2.1: Initial Growth of Gap ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
and
[Lemma D.13](#A4.Thmlemma13 "Lemma D.13. ‣ D.3.1 Attention and Logit Preliminaries ‣ D.3 Stage 2.1: Initial Growth of Gap ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
which together imply that on the event $`\mathcal{E}_{2}`$, only a
constant number of neurons are activated and
$`\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j}\leq O\left(\frac{1}{d}\right)`$ for all
$`j\neq j_{2}`$; and from
[Lemma D.15](#A4.Thmlemma15 "Lemma D.15. ‣ D.3.1 Attention and Logit Preliminaries ‣ D.3 Stage 2.1: Initial Growth of Gap ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
which implies that on the complement event $`\mathcal{E}_{2}^{c}`$,
occurring with probability at most $`O\left(\frac{1}{\log d}\right)`$,
there exists at most one $`j\neq j_{2}`$ such that
$`\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j}\geq\Omega(1)`$ while
$`\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j}\leq O\Big(\frac{1}{\mathsf{poly}d}\Big)`$
for other $`j\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})`$.

Putting it all together, we finish the proof. ∎

###### Lemma D.19.

If
[Induction D.1](#A4.Thminduction1 "Induction D.1. ‣ D.3 Stage 2.1: Initial Growth of Gap ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
holds for all iterations $`<t`$, given $`s\in\tau(\mathcal{X})`$, for
$`[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}]_{s,s}\geq\Omega(\frac{\varrho}{\log d})`$, we
have

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=1}^{2}\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,4}}\mathsf{Loss}^{2,\ell}_{5}\Big]_{s,s}\geq\Omega\bigg(\sum_{\ell=1}^{2}\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}}\mathsf{Loss}^{2,\ell}_{5}\Big]_{s,s}\bigg).`$ |  |

###### Proof.

Notice that by
[Lemma D.17](#A4.Thmlemma17 "Lemma D.17 (Negative gradient). ‣ D.3.2 Gradient Lemma ‣ D.3 Stage 2.1: Initial Growth of Gap ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
when
$`[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}]_{s,s}\geq\Omega(\frac{\varrho}{\log d})`$, we
have
$`[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,4}]_{s,s}\geq-O(\frac{\varrho}{\log^{2}d})`$.
Hence

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}+\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},0}\geq\Omega\Big(\frac{\varrho}{\log d}\Big),`$ |  |

which implies
$`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}(\mathbf{Z}^{2,1})\geq\Omega(\frac{\varrho}{\log d})\cdot B\pm O(\delta)\geq\varrho`$
already lies in the linear regime for
$`\mathbf{Z}^{2,1}\in\mathcal{E}_{2}`$. Then we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,4,2,i}`$ | $`\displaystyle\geq\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}\cdot\bigg((1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}})\cdot`$ |  |
````
