````math
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~\Big(V_{j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\bigg)\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{1})=s\}\cap\mathcal{E}_{2}}\Bigg]`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq\Omega(1)\cdot\Theta(B)\cdot\Theta\Big(\frac{1}{d}\Big)\geq\Omega\Big(\frac{\log d}{d}\Big).`$ |  |

Moreover, from
[Lemma D.17](#A4.Thmlemma17 "Lemma D.17 (Negative gradient). ‣ D.3.2 Gradient Lemma ‣ D.3 Stage 2.1: Initial Growth of Gap ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
the magnitude of negative gradient from other $`\mathcal{N}`$ terms can
be upper bounded by
$`O\Big(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,3,1,i}}{\log d}\Big)`$. Therefore,
combining with the fact that
$`\sum_{\ell=1}^{2}\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}}\mathsf{Loss}^{2,\ell}_{5}\Big]_{s,s}=\Theta(\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,3,1,i})`$
from
[Lemma D.16](#A4.Thmlemma16 "Lemma D.16. ‣ D.3.2 Gradient Lemma ‣ D.3 Stage 2.1: Initial Growth of Gap ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
we complete the proof. ∎

###### Lemma D.20.

If
[Induction D.1](#A4.Thminduction1 "Induction D.1. ‣ D.3 Stage 2.1: Initial Growth of Gap ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
holds for all iterations $`<t`$, given
$`s\neq s^{\prime}\in\tau(\mathcal{X})`$, for $`p\in\{3,4\}`$, we have

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\bigg|\sum_{\ell=1}^{2}\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,p}}\mathsf{Loss}^{2,\ell}_{5}\Big]_{s,s^{\prime}}\bigg|\leq O\Big(\frac{\log d}{d^{2}}\Big)=O(\frac{1}{d})\cdot\bigg|\sum_{\ell=1}^{2}\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,p}}\mathsf{Loss}^{2,\ell}_{5}\Big]_{s,s}\bigg|.`$ |  |

###### Proof.

The proof follows directly by combining the expressions from
[Lemma D.8](#A4.Thmlemma8 "Lemma D.8 (Refined expression for the gradient of 𝐐_{4,3}). ‣ Notations for activated neurons. ‣ D.2 Some Useful Bounds for Gradients ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
[Lemma D.9](#A4.Thmlemma9 "Lemma D.9 (Refined expression for the gradient of 𝐐_{4,4}). ‣ Notations for activated neurons. ‣ D.2 Some Useful Bounds for Gradients ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
and
[Lemma D.16](#A4.Thmlemma16 "Lemma D.16. ‣ D.3.2 Gradient Lemma ‣ D.3 Stage 2.1: Initial Growth of Gap ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
along with the fact that the event
$`\{\tau(x_{0})=s,\tau(x_{1})=s^{\prime}\}`$ occurs with probability
$`O\big(\tfrac{1}{d^{2}}\big)`$. ∎

#### D.3.3 At the End of Stage 2.1

Putting gradient lemmas together, we can directly prove that
[Induction D.1](#A4.Thminduction1 "Induction D.1. ‣ D.3 Stage 2.1: Initial Growth of Gap ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
holds for all iterations $`t`$ until the end of stage 2.1, where we can
conclude the following:

###### Lemma D.21 (End of stage 2.1).

Given $`s\in\tau(\mathcal{X})`$,
[Induction D.1](#A4.Thminduction1 "Induction D.1. ‣ D.3 Stage 2.1: Initial Growth of Gap ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
holds for all iterations
$`t<T_{2,1,s}=O\Big(\frac{d}{\eta\log^{2}d}\Big)`$, then at the end of
stage 2.1, we have

1.  1.
    $`[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,p}]_{s,s}=\Omega(\frac{1}{\log d})`$ for
    $`p\in\{3,4\}`$;
2.  2.
    $`[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}]_{s,s}-[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,4}]_{s,s}\geq\Omega\Big(\frac{1}{\log d}\Big)`$;
3.  3.
    $`\Big|[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,p}]_{s,s^{\prime}}\Big|\leq O\Big(\frac{[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,p}]_{s,s}}{d}\Big)`$
    for $`s^{\prime}\in\tau(\mathcal{X})\not=s`$ for $`p\in\{3,4\}`$;
    otherwise, $`[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,p}]_{s,s^{\prime}}=0`$.

### D.4 Stage 2.2: Continual Growth of Diagonal Entries

In Stage 2.2, the diagonal entries $`[\mathbf{Q}_{4,3}]_{s,s}`$ and
$`[\mathbf{Q}_{4,4}]_{s,s}`$ continue to grow until they reach a certain
threshold. The analysis in this stage parallels that of Stage 2.1, but
our focus now shifts to the gradients contributed by $`\ell=2`$, as the
logit at $`\ell=1`$ is already near-optimal and thus contributes
negligibly to the growth of $`\mathbf{Q}_{4,3}`$ and
$`\mathbf{Q}_{4,4}`$.

###### Induction D.2.

Given $`s\in\tau(\mathcal{X})`$, let $`T_{2,2,s}`$ denote the first time
that $`[\mathbf{Q}_{4,3}]_{s,s}`$ reaches $`0.0001`$. For all iterations
$`T_{2,1,s}\leq t<T_{2,2,s}`$, we have the following holds

1.  1.
    $`[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}]_{s,s},[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,4}]_{s,s}\leq O(1)`$
    monotonically increases;
2.  2.
    $`[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}]_{s,s}-[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,4}]_{s,s}\in\Big[\Omega\big(\frac{1}{\log d}\big),O(1)\Big]`$;
3.  3.
    for $`(p,q)\in\{(4,3),(4,4)\}`$,
    $`\Big|[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{p,q}]_{s,s^{\prime}}\Big|\leq O\Big(\frac{[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{p,q}]_{s,s}}{d}\Big)`$
    for $`s^{\prime}\in\tau(\mathcal{X})\not=s`$; other
    $`[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{p,q}]_{s,s^{\prime}}=0`$.

Throughout the following analysis, instead of $`\mathcal{E}_{2}`$
defined in
([63](#A4.E63 "Equation 63 ‣ Probabilistic Events. ‣ D.2 Some Useful Bounds for Gradients ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")),
we consider a renewed event $`\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{2}`$ for
$`\ell=2`$:

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{2}\triangleq\Big\{g_{1}\neq g_{2}\wedge y_{0}\neq y_{1}\}.`$ |  | (68) |

#### D.4.1 Attention and Logit Preliminaries

The proof in this part proceeds analogously to the arguments in
[Section D.3.1](#A4.SS3.SSS1 "D.3.1 Attention and Logit Preliminaries ‣ D.3 Stage 2.1: Initial Growth of Gap ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
with the induction hypothesis from
[Induction D.2](#A4.Thminduction2 "Induction D.2. ‣ D.4 Stage 2.2: Continual Growth of Diagonal Entries ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
incorporated. Hence, we omit the details here.

###### Lemma D.22.

If
[Induction D.2](#A4.Thminduction2 "Induction D.2. ‣ D.4 Stage 2.2: Continual Growth of Diagonal Entries ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
holds for all iterations $`\in[T_{2,1,s},t)`$, given input
$`\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1}`$, then we have

1.  1.
    for $`\ell=1`$,

    1.  (a)
        $`\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},0\to\mathsf{pred},1}\in\Big[\frac{1}{3}+\Omega\big(\frac{1}{\log d}\big),\frac{1}{3}+c_{1}\Big]`$,
        where $`c_{1}>0`$ is a small constant;
    2.  (b)
        $`\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},0\to\mathsf{pred},2}\in\Big[\frac{1}{3}-c_{2},\frac{1}{3}-\Omega\big(\frac{1}{\log d}\big)\Big]`$,
        where $`c_{2}>0`$ is a small constant;
    3.  (c)
        $`\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},0\to\mathsf{pred},2}+\Omega\big(\frac{1}{\log d}\big)\leq\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},0\to\mathsf{ans},0}`$;
    4.  (d)
        $`\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},0\to\mathsf{pred},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},0\to\mathsf{ans},0}\in\Big[\Omega\big(\frac{1}{\log d}\big),c_{3}\Big]`$.
2.  2.
    for $`\ell=2`$,

    1.  (a)
        $`\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}\in\Big[\frac{1}{4}+\Omega\big(\frac{1}{\log d}\big),\frac{1}{4}+c_{4}\Big]`$,
        where $`c_{4}>0`$ is a small constant;
    2.  (b)
        $`\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},1},\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},0}\in\Big[\frac{1}{4}-c_{5},\frac{1}{4}-\Omega\big(\frac{1}{\log d}\big)\Big]`$,
        moreover,
        $`\big|\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},0}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},1}\big|\leq O\Big(\frac{1}{d}\Big)`$,
        where $`c_{5}>0`$ is a small constant;
    3.  (c)
        $`\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},1},\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},0}\leq\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},1}-\Omega\Big(\frac{1}{\log d}\Big)`$;
    4.  (d)
        $`\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},1}\in\Big[\Omega\big(\frac{1}{\log d}\big),c_{6}\Big]`$,
        where $`c_{6}>0`$ is a small constant.

Notice that the constant $`c_{1}-c_{6}`$ depends on the threshold
$`0.0001`$ in
[Induction D.2](#A4.Thminduction2 "Induction D.2. ‣ D.4 Stage 2.2: Continual Growth of Diagonal Entries ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization").
We choose the threshold $`0.0001`$ small enough to ensure
$`2C_{B}(c_{1}+c_{2})<1-c_{6}C_{B}`$ and $`1-4c_{5}C_{B}>0`$.

###### Lemma D.23.

If
[Induction D.2](#A4.Thminduction2 "Induction D.2. ‣ D.4 Stage 2.2: Continual Growth of Diagonal Entries ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
holds for all iterations $`\in[T_{2,1,s},t)`$, given input
$`\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1}`$, then we have

1.  1.
    for $`\ell=1`$, if $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1}\in\mathcal{E}_{1}`$, then

    1.  (a)
        for $`j=j_{1}`$, $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{1},r}\ll-\varrho`$ for
        $`r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j_{1}}\setminus\{r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}\}`$;
    2.  (b)
        for $`j=j^{\prime}_{1}\triangleq\tau\big(g_{2}(y_{0})\big)`$,
        $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{1},r}\ll-\varrho`$ for
        $`r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j^{\prime}_{1}}\setminus\{r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}\}`$;
    3.  (c)
        for other $`j\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})`$, $`r`$ is not activated for
        all $`r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}`$, i.e.,
        $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r}\ll-\varrho`$.
2.  2.
    $`\ell=2`$, if
    $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1}\in\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{2}`$, then

    1.  (a)
        for $`j=j_{2}`$, $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r}\ll-\varrho`$ for
        $`r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j_{2}}\setminus\{r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}\}`$;
    2.  (b)
        for $`j=j^{\prime}_{2}\triangleq\tau\big(g_{2}(y_{0})\big)`$,
        $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{2},r}\ll-\varrho`$ for
        $`r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j^{\prime}_{2}}\setminus\{r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}\}`$;
    3.  (c)
        for other $`j\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})`$, $`r`$ is not activated for
        all $`r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}`$, i.e.,
        $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r}\ll-\varrho`$.

###### Lemma D.24.

If
[Induction D.2](#A4.Thminduction2 "Induction D.2. ‣ D.4 Stage 2.2: Continual Growth of Diagonal Entries ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
holds for all iterations $`\in[T_{2,1,s},t)`$, given input
$`\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell}`$, then we have

1.  1.
    $`\ell=1`$, for $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1}\in\mathcal{E}_{1}`$,

    1.  (a)
        $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{1},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}\geq\Big(\frac{1}{3}+\Omega\big(\frac{1}{\log d}\big)\Big)B`$;
    2.  (b)
        $`\Omega(1)\leq\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{1},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{1},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}\leq 2(c_{1}+c_{2})B`$.
2.  2.
    $`\ell=2`$, for
    $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1}\in\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{2}`$,

    1.  (a)
        $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}\in\Big[\Omega(1),4c_{5}B\Big]`$;
    2.  (b)
        $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}\in\big[\Omega(1),c_{6}B\big]`$
        and
        $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}\geq\Omega(1)`$.

###### Lemma D.25.

If
[Induction D.2](#A4.Thminduction2 "Induction D.2. ‣ D.4 Stage 2.2: Continual Growth of Diagonal Entries ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
holds for all iterations $`\in[T_{2,1,s},t)`$, given input
$`\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1}`$, then we have

1.  1.
    for $`\ell=1`$, if $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1}\in\mathcal{E}_{1}`$,
    $`\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{1}^{\prime}}\geq\Omega\Big(\frac{1}{d^{2C_{B}(c_{1}+c_{2})}}\Big)`$;
2.  2.
    for $`\ell=2`$, if
    $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1}\in\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{2}`$,
    $`1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}}=\Omega(1)`$,
    $`\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}^{\prime}}=O\big(\frac{1}{d^{1-c_{6}C_{B}}}\big)`$.

###### Proof.

- •
  For $`\ell=1`$, we have

  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|
  |  | $`\displaystyle\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{1}}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\frac{1}{1+e^{\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{1},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{1},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}}+O(d)\cdot e^{-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{1},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}}}`$ |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(a)}}{{\geq}}\frac{1}{1+e^{2(c_{1}+c_{2})B}+O(d)\cdot e^{-\big(\frac{1}{3}-2c_{1}-2c_{2}\big)B}}\geq\Omega\Big(\frac{1}{d^{2C_{B}(c_{1}+c_{2})}}\Big),`$ |  |

  where the inequality (a) follows from
  [Lemma D.24](#A4.Thmlemma24 "Lemma D.24. ‣ D.4.1 Attention and Logit Preliminaries ‣ D.4 Stage 2.2: Continual Growth of Diagonal Entries ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
  and the last inequality is due to the fact that $`(c_{1}+c_{2})`$ is
  some sufficiently small constant s.t.,
  $`e^{-\big(\frac{1}{3}-2c_{1}-2c_{2}\big)B}=1/\mathsf{poly}d.`$
- •
  For $`\ell=2`$, we have

  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|
  |  | $`\displaystyle\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{j_{2}}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\frac{1}{1+e^{-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}+\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}}+O(d)\cdot e^{-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}}}`$ |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(a)}}{{\leq}}\frac{1}{1+O(d)\cdot e^{-4c_{5}B}}=O\bigg(\frac{1}{d^{1-4c_{5}C_{B}}}\bigg),`$ |  |

  where the inequality (a) follows from
  [Lemma D.24](#A4.Thmlemma24 "Lemma D.24. ‣ D.4.1 Attention and Logit Preliminaries ‣ D.4 Stage 2.2: Continual Growth of Diagonal Entries ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization").
  Similarly, we have

  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|
  |  | $`\displaystyle\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{j^{\prime}_{2}}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\frac{1}{1+e^{\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}}+O(d)\cdot e^{-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}}}`$ |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{1+e^{\Omega(1)}+O(d)\cdot e^{-c_{6}B}}=O\bigg(\frac{1}{d^{1-c_{6}C_{B}}}\bigg).`$ |  |

∎

###### Lemma D.26.

If
[Induction D.2](#A4.Thminduction2 "Induction D.2. ‣ D.4 Stage 2.2: Continual Growth of Diagonal Entries ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
holds for all iterations $`\in[T_{2,1,s},t)`$, given input
$`\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1}`$, then we have

1.  1.
    for $`\ell=1`$, if $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1}\notin\mathcal{E}_{1}`$,
    then

    1.  (a)
        for $`j=j_{1}`$,
        $`\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j_{1}}=\{r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}\}`$,
        $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{1},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}=B\pm O(\delta)`$;
    2.  (b)
        for $`j\neq j_{1}\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})`$, assuming
        $`j=\tau(g_{1}(y))`$, then
        $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r_{g_{1}\cdot y}}\in\bigg[\Big(\frac{1}{3}-c_{2}\Big)B,\Big(\frac{1}{3}+c_{1}\Big)B\bigg]`$
        and $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r}\ll-\varrho`$ for
        $`r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}\setminus\{r_{g_{1}\cdot y}\}`$.
2.  2.
    $`\ell=2`$, if
    $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1}\notin\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{2}`$, then

    1.  (a)
        if $`g_{1}=g_{2}\wedge y_{0}\neq y_{1}`$,

        1.  i.
            for $`j=j_{2}`$,
            $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}\in\Big[\frac{1}{2}B+\Omega(1),\big(\frac{1}{2}+2c_{5}\big)B\Big]`$
            and $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r}\ll-\varrho`$ for
            $`r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j_{2}}\setminus\{r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}\}`$;
        2.  ii.
            for $`j=\tau(g_{2}(y_{0}))`$,
            $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}\geq\Omega(1)`$
            and $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r}\ll-\varrho`$ for
            $`r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}\setminus\{r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}\}`$;
        3.  iii.
            for other $`j\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})`$, assuming
            $`j=\tau(g_{2}(y))`$,
            $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r_{g_{2}\cdot y}}\in[\Omega(1),c_{6}B]`$
            and $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r}\ll-\varrho`$ for
            $`r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}\setminus\{r_{g_{2}\cdot y}\}`$.
    2.  (b)
        if $`g_{1}\neq g_{2}\wedge y_{0}=y_{1}`$,

        1.  i.
            for $`j=j_{2}`$,
            $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}\in\Big[\frac{1}{2}B+\Omega(1),\big(\frac{1}{2}+2c_{5}\big)B\Big]`$
            and $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r}\ll-\varrho`$ for
            $`r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j_{2}}\setminus\{r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}\}`$;
        2.  ii.
            for $`j=\tau(g_{1}(y_{1}))`$,
            $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{1}}}\geq\Omega(1)`$
            and $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r}\ll-\varrho`$ for
            $`r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}\setminus\{r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{1}}\}`$;
        3.  iii.
            for other $`j\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})`$, $`r`$ is not activted
            for all $`r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}`$, i.e.,
            $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r}\ll-\varrho`$.
    3.  (c)
        if $`g_{1}=g_{2}\wedge y_{0}=y_{1}`$,

        1.  i.
            for $`j=j_{2}`$,
            $`\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j_{2}}=\{r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}\}`$,
            $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}=B\pm O(\delta)`$
            ;
        2.  ii.
            for other $`j\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})`$, assuming
            $`j=\tau(g_{2}(y))`$,
            $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r_{g_{2}\cdot y}}\in[\Omega(1),c_{6}B]`$
            and $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r}\ll-\varrho`$ for
            $`r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}\setminus\{r_{g_{2}\cdot y}\}`$.

###### Lemma D.27.

If
[Induction D.2](#A4.Thminduction2 "Induction D.2. ‣ D.4 Stage 2.2: Continual Growth of Diagonal Entries ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
holds for all iterations $`\in[T_{2,1,s},t)`$, given input
$`\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1}`$, then we have

1.  1.
    for $`\ell=1`$, if $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1}\notin\mathcal{E}_{1}`$,
    then
    $`1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{1}}=O\Big(\frac{1}{\mathsf{poly}d}\Big)`$.
2.  2.
    $`\ell=2`$, if
    $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1}\notin\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{2}`$, then

    1.  (a)
        if $`g_{1}=g_{2}\wedge y_{0}\neq y_{1}`$,
        $`\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}}=\Omega(1)`$,
        $`1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}}-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{2}(y_{0}))}=\frac{1}{\mathsf{poly}d}`$.
    2.  (b)
        if $`g_{1}\neq g_{2}\wedge y_{0}=y_{1}`$,
        $`\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}}=\Omega(1)`$,
        $`1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}}-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{1}(y_{1}))}=\frac{1}{\mathsf{poly}d}`$.
    3.  (c)
        if $`g_{1}=g_{2}\wedge y_{0}=y_{1}`$,
        $`1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}}=O\Big(\frac{1}{\mathsf{poly}d}\Big)`$.

#### D.4.2 Gradient Lemma

###### Lemma D.28.

If
[Induction D.2](#A4.Thminduction2 "Induction D.2. ‣ D.4 Stage 2.2: Continual Growth of Diagonal Entries ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
holds for all iterations $`\in[T_{2,1,s},t)`$, given
$`s\in\tau(\mathcal{X})`$, for $`[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}]_{s,s}`$, we
have

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=1}^{2}\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}}\mathsf{Loss}^{2,\ell}_{5}\Big]_{s,s}=\Theta\Big(\frac{\log d}{d}\Big).`$ |  |

###### Proof.

The proof is similar to
[Lemma D.16](#A4.Thmlemma16 "Lemma D.16. ‣ D.3.2 Gradient Lemma ‣ D.3 Stage 2.1: Initial Growth of Gap ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
but we need to shift our focus to
$`\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}}\mathsf{Loss}^{2,2}_{5}\Big]_{s,s}`$.
By
[Lemma D.23](#A4.Thmlemma23 "Lemma D.23. ‣ D.4.1 Attention and Logit Preliminaries ‣ D.4 Stage 2.2: Continual Growth of Diagonal Entries ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
and
[Lemma D.26](#A4.Thmlemma26 "Lemma D.26. ‣ D.4.1 Attention and Logit Preliminaries ‣ D.4 Stage 2.2: Continual Growth of Diagonal Entries ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,3,2,i}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\cdot(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}})\cdot`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~\Big(\Big(V_{j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}(g_{2})-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{1})=s\}\cap\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{2}}\Bigg]`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\cdot(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}})\cdot`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~\Big(\Big(V_{j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}(g_{2})-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{1})=s\}\cap\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}^{c}_{2}}\Bigg]`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(a)}}{{=}}\Theta\big(\frac{1}{d}\big)\cdot\Omega(1)\cdot\Theta(B)+\Theta(\frac{1}{d})\cdot O(1)\cdot\Theta(B)\cdot O(\frac{1}{\log d})`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\Theta\Big(\frac{\log d}{d}\Big),`$ |  |

where the inequality (a) follows from
[Lemma D.25](#A4.Thmlemma25 "Lemma D.25. ‣ D.4.1 Attention and Logit Preliminaries ‣ D.4 Stage 2.2: Continual Growth of Diagonal Entries ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
and
[Lemma D.26](#A4.Thmlemma26 "Lemma D.26. ‣ D.4.1 Attention and Logit Preliminaries ‣ D.4 Stage 2.2: Continual Growth of Diagonal Entries ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization").

Furthermore, for $`\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,3,2,ii}`$, we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\Big|\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,3,2,ii}\Big|`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\Bigg|\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\cdot\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{2}}\cdot`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~\Big(\Big(V_{j^{\prime}_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}(g_{2})-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{1})=s\}\cap\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{2}}\bigg]\Bigg|`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\Bigg|\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\cdot\sum_{j\neq j_{2}\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})}\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j}\cdot`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~\Big(\sum_{r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}}\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r})\cdot\Big(V_{j,r}(g_{2})-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{1})=s\}\cap\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}^{c}_{2}}\bigg]\Bigg|`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\Theta\big(\frac{1}{d}\big)\cdot O\Big(\frac{1}{d^{1-c_{6}C_{B}}}\Big)\cdot\Theta(B)+\Theta\big(\frac{1}{d}\big)\cdot O(1)\cdot\Theta(B)\cdot O\Big(\frac{1}{\log d}\Big)\leq O(\frac{1}{d}).`$ |  |

$`|\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,3,1,i}|`$ and $`|\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,3,2,i}|`$
can be upper bounded by $`O\big(\frac{\log d}{d}\big)`$ as
[Lemma D.16](#A4.Thmlemma16 "Lemma D.16. ‣ D.3.2 Gradient Lemma ‣ D.3 Stage 2.1: Initial Growth of Gap ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization").
Thus, we complete the proof. ∎

###### Lemma D.29.

If
[Induction D.2](#A4.Thminduction2 "Induction D.2. ‣ D.4 Stage 2.2: Continual Growth of Diagonal Entries ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
holds for all iterations $`\in[T_{2,1,s},t)`$, given
$`s\in\tau(\mathcal{X})`$, we have

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=1}^{2}\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,4}}\mathsf{Loss}^{2,\ell}_{5}\Big]_{s,s}=\Theta\Big(\frac{\log d}{d}\Big).`$ |  |

###### Proof.

The proof follows a similar analysis to
[Lemma D.19](#A4.Thmlemma19 "Lemma D.19. ‣ D.3.2 Gradient Lemma ‣ D.3 Stage 2.1: Initial Growth of Gap ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
and we thus omit the details here. ∎

###### Lemma D.30.

If
[Induction D.2](#A4.Thminduction2 "Induction D.2. ‣ D.4 Stage 2.2: Continual Growth of Diagonal Entries ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
holds for all iterations $`\in[T_{2,1,s},t)`$, given
$`s\in\tau(\mathcal{X})`$, we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\sum^{t}_{t^{\prime}=T_{2,1,s}}\bigg(\sum_{\ell=1}^{2}\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(t^{\prime})}{4,3}}\mathsf{Loss}^{2,\ell}_{5}\Big]_{s,s}-\sum_{\ell=1}^{2}\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(t^{\prime})}_{4,4}}\mathsf{Loss}^{2,\ell}_{5}\Big]_{s,s}\bigg)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~\geq-O\big(\frac{1}{\log d}\big)\cdot\bigg(\sum^{t}_{t^{\prime}=T_{2,1,s}}\sum_{\ell=1}^{2}\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(t^{\prime})}_{4,4}}\mathsf{Loss}^{2,\ell}_{5}\Big]_{s,s}\bigg).`$ |  |

###### Proof.

Following the analogous analysis as
[Lemma D.18](#A4.Thmlemma18 "Lemma D.18 (Growth of gap). ‣ D.3.2 Gradient Lemma ‣ D.3 Stage 2.1: Initial Growth of Gap ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=1}^{2}\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}}\mathsf{Loss}^{2,\ell}_{5}\Big]_{s,s}+\sum_{\ell=1}^{2}\Big[\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,4}}\mathsf{Loss}^{2,\ell}_{5}\Big]_{s,s}=\sum_{\ell\in[2]}\sum_{\kappa\in\{i,ii,iii\}}\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,3,\ell,\kappa}-\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,4,\ell,\kappa}.`$ |  |

Meanwhile
$`|\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,p,\ell,iii}|=O(\frac{1}{\mathsf{poly}d})`$ for
$`p\in\{3,4\}`$ and $`\ell\in[2]`$, we can focus on the gradient
difference between $`\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}`$ and
$`\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,4}`$ contributed by other terms.

For $`\ell=1`$, since
$`\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},0}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}\geq\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},0}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}`$,
and thus it is straightforward to see that
$`\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,3,1,i}-\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,4,1,i}\geq 0`$.
Furthermore, we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,3,1,ii}-\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,4,1,ii}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},0}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}\cdot\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{1}}\cdot`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~\Big(\Big(V_{j^{\prime}_{1},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}(g_{1})-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{1},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{0})=s\}\cap\mathcal{E}_{1}}\Bigg]`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},0}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{1}}\cdot`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~\Big(\Big(V_{j^{\prime}_{1},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}(y_{0})-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{1},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{0})=s\}\cap\mathcal{E}_{1}}\Bigg]`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle-\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},0}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}\cdot\sum_{y\neq y_{0}\in\mathcal{Y}}\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{1}(y))}\cdot`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~\Big(\Big(V_{\tau(g_{1}(y)),r_{g_{1}\cdot y}}(g_{1})-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{1}(y)),r_{g_{1}\cdot y}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{0})=s\}\cap\mathcal{E}^{c}_{1}}\Bigg]`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},0}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot\sum_{y\neq y_{0}\in\mathcal{Y}}\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{1}(y))}\cdot`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~\Big(\Big(V_{\tau(g_{1}(y)),r_{g_{1}\cdot y}}(y_{0})-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{1}(y)),r_{g_{1}\cdot y}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{0})=s\}\cap\mathcal{E}^{c}_{1}}\Bigg]`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(a)}}{{\geq}}\Theta(\frac{1}{d})\cdot\Omega\Big(\frac{1}{d^{2C_{B}(c_{1}+c_{2})}}\Big)\cdot\Theta(B)-\Theta(\frac{1}{d})\cdot O(\frac{1}{\mathsf{poly}d})\cdot O(B)\cdot O(\frac{1}{\log d})\geq\Omega\Big(\frac{\log d}{d^{1+2C_{B}(c_{1}+c_{2})}}\Big).`$ |  |

where the inequality (a) is due to
[Lemma D.25](#A4.Thmlemma25 "Lemma D.25. ‣ D.4.1 Attention and Logit Preliminaries ‣ D.4 Stage 2.2: Continual Growth of Diagonal Entries ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
and
[Lemma D.27](#A4.Thmlemma27 "Lemma D.27. ‣ D.4.1 Attention and Logit Preliminaries ‣ D.4 Stage 2.2: Continual Growth of Diagonal Entries ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization").

For $`\ell=2`$, since
$`\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},\mathsf{pred},2}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}\geq\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}`$,
and thus it is straightforward to see that
$`\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,3,2,i}-\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,4,2,i}\geq 0`$.
Moreover, we have

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,3,2,ii}-\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,4,2,ii}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\cdot\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{2}}\cdot`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~\Big(\Big(V_{j^{\prime}_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}(g_{2})-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{1})=s\}\cap\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{2}}\Bigg]`$ |  | (69) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}\cdot\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{2}}\cdot`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~\Big(\Big(V_{j^{\prime}_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}(y_{1})-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{1})=s\}\cap\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{2}}\Bigg]`$ |  | (70) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle-\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\cdot\sum_{j\neq j_{2}\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})}\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j}\cdot`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~\Big(\sum_{r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}}\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r})\cdot\Big(V_{j,r}(g_{2})-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{1})=s\}\cap\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}^{c}_{2}}\Bigg]`$ |  | (71) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}\cdot\sum_{j\neq j_{2}\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})}\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j}\cdot`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~\Big(\sum_{r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}}\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r})\cdot\Big(V_{j,r}(y_{1})-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{1})=s\}\cap\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}^{c}_{2}}\Bigg].`$ |  | (72) |

By
[Lemma D.25](#A4.Thmlemma25 "Lemma D.25. ‣ D.4.1 Attention and Logit Preliminaries ‣ D.4 Stage 2.2: Continual Growth of Diagonal Entries ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
we obtain that

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\eqref{eq-s22-cyc-gd1}+\eqref{eq-s22-cyc-gd2}\geq-O\big(\frac{1}{d}\big)\cdot O\Big(\frac{1}{d^{1-c_{6}C_{B}}}\Big)\cdot\Theta(B)\geq-O\Big(\frac{\log d}{d^{2-c_{6}C_{B}}}\Big).`$ |  |

By
[Lemma D.26](#A4.Thmlemma26 "Lemma D.26. ‣ D.4.1 Attention and Logit Preliminaries ‣ D.4 Stage 2.2: Continual Growth of Diagonal Entries ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
for $`\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}^{c}_{2}`$, we only need to consider the
case that $`g_{1}=g_{2}\wedge y_{0}\neq y_{1}`$, and we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\eqref{eq-s22-cyc-gd3}+\eqref{eq-s22-cyc-gd4}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq-\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\cdot\sum_{y\neq y_{1}\in\mathcal{Y}}\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{2}(y))}\cdot`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~\Big(\Big(V_{\tau(g_{2}(y)),r_{g_{2}\cdot y}}(g_{2})-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{2}(y)),r_{g_{2}\cdot y}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{1})=s\}\cap\{g_{1}=g_{2}\wedge y_{0}\neq y_{1}\}}\Bigg]`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}\cdot\sum_{y\neq y_{1}\in\mathcal{Y}}\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{2}(y))}\cdot`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~\Big(\Big(V_{\tau(g_{2}(y)),r_{g_{2}\cdot y}}(y_{1})-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{2}(y)),r_{g_{2}\cdot y}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{1})=s\}\cap\{g_{1}=g_{2}\wedge y_{0}\neq y_{1}\}}\Bigg]`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq-\Theta\Big(\frac{1}{d}\Big)\cdot O(1)\cdot\Theta(B)\cdot O\Big(\frac{1}{\log d}\Big)\geq-O\Big(\frac{1}{d}\Big).`$ |  |

Putting it all together, combining with the fact that $`c_{6}`$ and
$`(c_{1}+c_{2})`$ are sufficiently small, we finish the proof. ∎

###### Lemma D.31 (Lower bound of gap).

If
[Induction D.2](#A4.Thminduction2 "Induction D.2. ‣ D.4 Stage 2.2: Continual Growth of Diagonal Entries ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
holds for all iterations $`\in[T_{2,1,s},t)`$, given
$`s\in\tau(\mathcal{X})`$, we have
$`[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}]_{s,s}-[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,4}]_{s,s}\geq\Omega\big(\frac{1}{\log d}\big)`$.

###### Proof.

Letting $`\widetilde{T}`$ denote the first time that
$`[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}]_{s,s}-[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,4}]_{s,s}\leq\frac{1}{2}\Big(\mathbf{Q}^{(T_{2,1,s})}_{4,3}]_{s,s}-[\mathbf{Q}^{(T_{2,1,s})}_{4,4}]_{s,s}\Big)`$,
which implies that

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\sum^{\widetilde{T}}_{t^{\prime}=T_{2,1,s}}\bigg(\sum_{\ell=1}^{2}\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(t^{\prime})}{4,3}}\mathsf{Loss}^{2,\ell}_{5}\Big]_{s,s}-\sum_{\ell=1}^{2}\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(t^{\prime})}_{4,4}}\mathsf{Loss}^{2,\ell}_{5}\Big]_{s,s}\bigg).
``` |  |

Hence, by
[Lemma D.30](#A4.Thmlemma30 "Lemma D.30. ‣ D.4.2 Gradient Lemma ‣ D.4 Stage 2.2: Continual Growth of Diagonal Entries ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
we have $`[\mathbf{Q}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{4,3}]_{s,s}`$ and
$`[\mathbf{Q}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{4,3}]_{s,s}`$ reaches $`\Omega(1)`$.
Thus, we can have a refined lower bound for
$`\eqref{eq-s22-cyc-gd3}+\eqref{eq-s22-cyc-gd4}`$ in
[Lemma D.30](#A4.Thmlemma30 "Lemma D.30. ‣ D.4.2 Gradient Lemma ‣ D.4 Stage 2.2: Continual Growth of Diagonal Entries ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
and obtain:

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | ([71](#A4.E71 "Equation 71 ‣ Proof. ‣ D.4.2 Gradient Lemma ‣ D.4 Stage 2.2: Continual Growth of Diagonal Entries ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")) | $`\displaystyle+\eqref{eq-s22-cyc-gd4}\geq-O(\frac{1}{\mathsf{poly}d})`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle-\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\cdot\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{2}(y_{0}))}\cdot`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~\Big(\Big(V_{\tau(g_{2}(y_{0})),r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}(g_{2})-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{2}(y_{0})),r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{1})=s\}\cap\{g_{1}=g_{2}\wedge y_{0}\neq y_{1}\}}\Bigg]`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}\cdot\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{2}(y_{0}))}\cdot`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~\Big(\Big(V_{\tau(g_{2}(y_{0})),r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}(y_{1})-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{2}(y_{0})),r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{1})=s\}\cap\{g_{1}=g_{2}\wedge y_{0}\neq y_{1}\}}\Bigg]`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(a)}}{{\geq}}-O\Big(\frac{1}{\mathsf{poly}d}\Big)-\Theta\Big(\frac{1}{d}\Big)\cdot O\Big(\frac{1}{d^{\Omega(1)}}\Big)\cdot\Theta(B)\cdot O\Big(\frac{1}{\log d}\Big)\geq-O\Big(\frac{1}{d^{1+\Omega(1)}}\Big),`$ |  |

where (a) follows from the fact that on the event
$`\{g_{1}=g_{2}\wedge y_{0}\neq y_{1}\}`$, we have
$`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}\geq\Omega(B)`$
once $`[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}]_{s,s}`$ and
$`[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,4}]_{s,s}`$ reach constant magnitude, and
consequently, the logit satisfies
$`\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{2}(y_{0}))}\leq O\left(\frac{1}{d^{\Omega(1)}}\right).`$

Therefore,

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\sum^{t}_{t^{\prime}=\widetilde{T}+1}\bigg(\sum_{\ell=1}^{2}\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(t^{\prime})}{4,3}}\mathsf{Loss}^{2,\ell}_{5}\Big]_{s,s}-\sum_{\ell=1}^{2}\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(t^{\prime})}_{4,4}}\mathsf{Loss}^{2,\ell}_{5}\Big]_{s,s}\bigg)\geq-O\Big(\frac{1}{\log d\cdot d^{\Omega(1)}}\Big)\cdot O(1),
``` |  |

which means that
$`[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}]_{s,s}-[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,4}]_{s,s}\geq\Omega\Big(\frac{1}{\log d}\Big)-O\Big(\frac{1}{\log d\cdot d^{\Omega(1)}}\Big)\geq\Omega\Big(\frac{1}{\log d}\Big).`$
∎

###### Lemma D.32.

If
[Induction D.2](#A4.Thminduction2 "Induction D.2. ‣ D.4 Stage 2.2: Continual Growth of Diagonal Entries ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
holds for all iterations $`\in[T_{2,1,s},t)`$, given
$`s^{\prime}\not=s\in\tau(\mathcal{X})`$, for $`p\in\{3,4\}`$, we have

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\bigg|\sum_{\ell=1}^{2}\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,p}}\mathsf{Loss}^{2,\ell}_{5}\Big]_{s,s^{\prime}}\bigg|\leq O\Big(\frac{\log d}{d^{2}}\Big)=O\Big(\frac{1}{d}\Big)\cdot\bigg|\sum_{\ell=1}^{2}\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,p}}\mathsf{Loss}^{2,\ell}_{5}\Big]_{s,s}\bigg|.`$ |  |

#### D.4.3 At the End of Stage 2.2

Putting gradient lemmas together, we can directly prove that
[Induction D.2](#A4.Thminduction2 "Induction D.2. ‣ D.4 Stage 2.2: Continual Growth of Diagonal Entries ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
holds for all iterations $`t`$ until the end of stage 2.2, where we can
conclude the following:

###### Lemma D.33 (End of Stage 2.2).

Given $`s\in\tau(\mathcal{X})`$,
[Induction D.2](#A4.Thminduction2 "Induction D.2. ‣ D.4 Stage 2.2: Continual Growth of Diagonal Entries ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
holds for all iterations
$`T_{2,1,s}\leq t<T_{2,2,s}=O\Big(\frac{d}{\eta\log d}\Big)`$, then at
the end of stage 2.2, we have

1.  1.
    $`[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,p}]_{s,s}=\Omega(1)`$ for $`p\in\{3,4\}`$;
2.  2.
    $`[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}]_{s,s}-[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,4}]_{s,s}\in\Big[\Omega\big(\frac{1}{\log d}\big),O(1)\Big]`$;
3.  3.
    $`|[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,p}]_{s,s^{\prime}}|\leq O\Big(\frac{[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,p}]_{s,s}}{d}\Big)`$
    for $`s^{\prime}\in\tau(\mathcal{X})\not=s`$, and other
    $`[\mathbf{Q}_{4,p}]_{s,s^{\prime}}=0`$.

### D.5 Stage 2.3: Decrease of Gap and Convergence

After rapid growth of diagonal entries in stage 2.2, we now focus on the
convergence of the attention and logit matrices, and the decrease of the
gap between $`[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}]_{s,s}`$ and
$`[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,4}]_{s,s}`$. Recall that

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\epsilon^{L,\ell}_{\mathsf{attn}}\big(\mathbf{Z}^{L,\ell-1}\big)`$ | $`\displaystyle=1-\mathbf{Attn}_{\mathsf{ans},\ell-1\to\mathsf{pred},\ell}\big(\mathbf{Z}^{L,\ell-1}\big)-\mathbf{Attn}_{\mathsf{ans},\ell-1\to\mathsf{ans},\ell-1}\big(\mathbf{Z}^{L,\ell-1}\big),`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\Delta^{L,\ell}\big(\mathbf{Z}^{L,\ell-1}\big)`$ | $`\displaystyle=\mathbf{Attn}_{\mathsf{ans},\ell-1\to\mathsf{pred},\ell}\big(\mathbf{Z}^{L,\ell-1}\big)-\mathbf{Attn}_{\mathsf{ans},\ell-1\to\mathsf{ans},\ell-1}\big(\mathbf{Z}^{L,\ell-1}\big).`$ |  |

Throught stage 2.3, we will focus on the attention gap
$`\Delta^{L,\ell}\big(\mathbf{Z}^{L,\ell-1}\big)`$ instead of the gap of
the attention matrices. We abbreviate.
$`\epsilon^{L,\ell}_{\mathsf{attn}}\big(\mathbf{Z}^{L,\ell-1}\big)`$ and
$`\Delta^{L,\ell}\big(\mathbf{Z}^{L,\ell-1}\big)`$ as
$`\epsilon^{L,\ell}_{\mathsf{attn}}`$ and $`\Delta^{L,\ell}`$ for
simplicity.

###### Induction D.3.

Given $`\epsilon\geq\widetilde{\Omega}(\sigma_{0})`$, for
$`s\in\tau(\mathcal{X})`$, let $`T_{2,3,s}`$ denote the first time that
$`\mathbb{E}\big[\epsilon^{2,2}_{\mathsf{attn}}\mid\tau(x_{1})=s\big]\leq\epsilon`$.
For all iterations $`T_{2,2,s}\leq t<T_{2,3,s}`$, we have the following
holds:

1.  1.
    $`[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}]_{s,s}`$ and
    $`[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,4}]_{s,s}`$ monotonically increases
    $`\leq\widetilde{O}(1)`$;
2.  2.
    $`{\Delta^{2,\ell}}\geq 0`$ for any $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell}`$ with
    $`\ell\in\{1,2\}`$;
3.  3.
    $`\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}\leq 0.5+\widetilde{c}_{1}`$
    for some small constant $`\widetilde{c}_{1}>0`$;
4.  4.
    for $`(p,q)\in\{(4,3),(4,4)\}`$,
    $`\Big|[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{p,q}]_{s,s^{\prime}}\Big|\leq O\Big(\frac{[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{p,q}]_{s,s}}{d}\Big)`$
    for $`s^{\prime}\in\tau(\mathcal{X})\not=s`$; other
    $`[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{p,q}]_{s,s^{\prime}}=0`$.

#### D.5.1 Attention and Logit Preliminaries

###### Lemma D.34.

If
[Induction D.3](#A4.Thminduction3 "Induction D.3. ‣ D.5 Stage 2.3: Decrease of Gap and Convergence ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
holds for all iterations $`\in[T_{2,2,s},t)`$, given
$`\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1}`$ then we have

1.  1.
    for $`\ell=1`$,

    1.  (a)
        $`\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},0\to\mathsf{ans},0}\geq\Omega(1)`$,
        and
        $`\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},0\to\mathsf{ans},0}>\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},0\to\mathsf{pred},2}`$;
2.  2.
    for $`\ell=2`$,

    1.  (a)
        $`\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},1}\geq\Omega(1)`$,
        $`\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},1},\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},0}\leq\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},1}`$;
    2.  (b)
        $`\Big|\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},0}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},1}\Big|\leq\widetilde{O}\big(\frac{1}{d}\big)`$.

Moreover, given $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,1}`$ and corresponding
$`\mathbf{Z}^{2,0}`$,
$`\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},0\to\mathsf{pred},1}\big(\mathbf{Z}^{2,0}\big)\geq\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}\big(\mathbf{Z}^{2,1}\big)`$.

###### Lemma D.35.

If
[Induction D.3](#A4.Thminduction3 "Induction D.3. ‣ D.5 Stage 2.3: Decrease of Gap and Convergence ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
holds for all iterations $`\in[T_{2,2,s},t)`$, given input
$`\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1}`$, then we have

1.  1.
    for $`\ell=1`$, if $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1}\in\mathcal{E}_{1}`$, then

    1.  (a)
        for $`j=j_{1}`$, $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{1},r}\ll-\varrho`$ for
        $`r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j_{1}}\setminus\{r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}\}`$;
    2.  (b)
        for
        $`j=j^{\prime}_{1}\triangleq\tau\big(g_{2}(y_{0})\big)=\tau(g_{1}(\widetilde{y}))`$,
        $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{1},r}\ll-\varrho`$ for
        $`r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j^{\prime}_{1}}\setminus\{r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}},r_{g_{1}\cdot\widetilde{y}}\}`$;
    3.  (c)
        for other $`j\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})`$, assuming
        $`j=\tau(g_{1}(y))`$ for some $`y\neq y_{0}`$, then
        $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r}\ll-\varrho`$ for
        $`r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}\setminus\{r_{g_{1}\cdot y}\}`$.
2.  2.
    $`\ell=2`$, if
    $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1}\in\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{2}`$, then

    1.  (a)
        for $`j=j_{2}`$, $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r}\ll-\varrho`$ for
        $`r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j_{2}}\setminus\{r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}\}`$;
    2.  (b)
        for $`j=j^{\prime}_{2}\triangleq\tau\big(g_{2}(y_{0})\big)`$,
        $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{2},r}\ll-\varrho`$ for
        $`r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j^{\prime}_{2}}\setminus\{r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}\}`$;
    3.  (c)
        for other $`j\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})`$, if $`j=\tau(g_{2}(y))`$ for
        some $`y\in\mathcal{Y}`$, then
        $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r}\ll-\varrho`$ for
        $`r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}\setminus\{r_{g_{2}\cdot y}\}`$.

###### Lemma D.36.

If
[Induction D.3](#A4.Thminduction3 "Induction D.3. ‣ D.5 Stage 2.3: Decrease of Gap and Convergence ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
holds for all iterations $`\in[T_{2,2,s},t)`$, given input
$`\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1}`$, then we have

1.  1.
    $`\ell=1`$, for $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1}\in\mathcal{E}_{1}`$,

    1.  (a)
        $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{1},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}=\big(1-2\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{2,1}\big)B\pm O(\delta)\geq\Big(\frac{1}{3}+c_{1}\Big)B`$;
    2.  (b)
        $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{1},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{1},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}=2\big(\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},0\to\mathsf{pred},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},0\to\mathsf{pred},2}\big)B\pm O(\delta)\geq 2(c_{1}+c_{2})B`$;
    3.  (c)
        for $`y\neq y_{0}`$,
        $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{1}(y)),r_{g_{1}\cdot y}}=\Big(2\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},0\to\mathsf{pred},1}-1\Big)B\pm O(\delta)`$,
        which is only activated if
        $`\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},0\to\mathsf{pred},1}>\frac{1}{2}`$
2.  2.
    $`\ell=2`$, for
    $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1}\in\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{2}`$,

    1.  (a)
        $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}=\big(1-2\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{2,2}\big)B\pm O(\delta)\geq 4c_{5}B`$;
    2.  (b)
        $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}=\Delta^{2,2}\cdot B\pm O(\delta)`$,
        and

        |  |  |  |
        |----|----|----|
        |  |
        ``` math
        \Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}=2\big(\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},0}\big)B\pm O(\delta);
        ``` |  |
    3.  (c)
        for $`y\neq y_{0},y_{1}`$,
        $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{2}(y)),r_{g_{2}\cdot y}}=\Big(2\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}-1\Big)B\pm O(\delta)`$,
        which is only activated if
        $`\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}>\frac{1}{2}`$.

###### Lemma D.37.

If
[Induction D.3](#A4.Thminduction3 "Induction D.3. ‣ D.5 Stage 2.3: Decrease of Gap and Convergence ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
holds for all iterations $`\in[T_{2,2,s},t)`$, given input
$`\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1}`$, then we have

1.  1.
    for $`\ell=1`$, if $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1}\in\mathcal{E}_{1}`$,

    |  |  |  |
    |----|----|----|
    |  |
    ``` math
    1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{1}}=\Theta(1)\cdot\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{1}^{\prime}}=\Theta\Bigg(\frac{1}{d^{2\big(\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},0\to\mathsf{pred},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},0\to\mathsf{pred},2}\big)C_{B}}}\Bigg).
    ``` |  |
2.  2.
    for $`\ell=2`$, if
    $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1}\in\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{2}`$,

    |  |  |  |
    |----|----|----|
    |  |
    ``` math
    \mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{2}}=\Theta\Bigg(\frac{1}{d^{2\big(\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},0}\big)C_{B}}+d^{1-\Delta^{2,2}C_{B}}}\Bigg),
    ``` |  |

    moreover,

    |  |  |  |
    |----|----|----|
    |  |
    ``` math
    1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}}\geq\min\bigg\{\Omega(1),\Omega\Big(\frac{1}{d^{C_{B}\cdot(1-2\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{2,2})-1}}\Big)\bigg\}.
    ``` |  |

###### Lemma D.38.

If
[Induction D.3](#A4.Thminduction3 "Induction D.3. ‣ D.5 Stage 2.3: Decrease of Gap and Convergence ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
holds for all iterations $`\in[T_{2,2,s},t)`$, given input
$`\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1}`$, then we have

1.  1.
    for $`\ell=1`$, if $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1}\notin\mathcal{E}_{1}`$,
    then

    1.  (a)
        for $`j=j_{1}`$,
        $`\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j_{1}}=\{r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}\}`$,
        $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{1},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}=B\pm O(\delta)`$;
    2.  (b)
        for $`j\neq j_{1}\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})`$, assuming
        $`j=\tau(g_{1}(y))`$ for $`y\neq y_{0}`$, then
        $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{1},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r_{g_{1}\cdot y}}\geq 2\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},0\to\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot B\geq\Omega(B)`$
        and $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r}\ll-\varrho`$ for
        $`r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}\setminus\{r_{g_{1}\cdot y}\}`$.
2.  2.
    $`\ell=2`$, if
    $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1}\notin\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{2}`$, then

    1.  (a)
        if $`g_{1}=g_{2}\wedge y_{0}\neq y_{1}`$,

        1.  i.
            for $`j=j_{2}`$,
            $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}=(1-\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{2,2})\cdot B\pm O(\delta)`$
            and $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r}\ll-\varrho`$ for
            $`r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j_{2}}\setminus\{r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}\}`$;
        2.  ii.
            for $`j=\tau(g_{2}(y_{0}))`$,
            $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}=2(\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},0})B\pm O(\delta)`$
            and $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r}\ll-\varrho`$ for
            $`r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}\setminus\{r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}\}`$;
        3.  iii.
            for other $`j\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})`$, assuming
            $`j=\tau(g_{2}(y))`$ for some $`y\neq y_{0},y_{1}`$,
            $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r_{g_{2}\cdot y}}=2\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},1}B\pm O(\delta)`$
            and $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r}\ll-\varrho`$ for
            $`r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}\setminus\{r_{g_{2}\cdot y}\}`$.
    2.  (b)
        if $`g_{1}\neq g_{2}\wedge y_{0}=y_{1}`$,

        1.  i.
            for $`j=j_{2}`$,
            $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}=(1-\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{2,2})\cdot B\pm O(\delta)`$
            and $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r}\ll-\varrho`$ for
            $`r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j_{2}}\setminus\{r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}\}`$;
        2.  ii.
            for $`j=\tau(g_{1}(y_{1}))=\tau(g_{2}(\widetilde{y}))`$,

            |  |  |  |  |
            |----|----|----|----|
            |  |  | $`\displaystyle\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{1}}}=2(\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},1})B\pm O(\delta)`$ |  |
            |  |  | $`\displaystyle\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r_{g_{2}\cdot\widetilde{y}}}=2(\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},0}+\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},1})B\pm O(\delta),`$ |  |

            where $`r_{g_{2}\cdot\widetilde{y}}`$ is only activated if
            $`\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}>\frac{1}{2}`$.
            $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r}\ll-\varrho`$ for
            $`r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}\setminus\{r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{1}},r_{g_{2}\cdot\widetilde{y}}\}`$;
        3.  iii.
            for other $`j\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})`$, assuming
            $`j=\tau(g_{2}(y))`$ for $`y\neq y_{1},\widetilde{y}`$, then
            $`r_{g_{2}\cdot{y}}`$ is only activated if
            $`\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}>\frac{1}{2}`$
            and

            |  |  |  |
            |----|----|----|
            |  |
            ``` math
            \Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y}}=2(\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},0}+\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},1})B\pm O(\delta).
            ``` |  |

            $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r}\ll-\varrho`$ for
            $`r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}\setminus\{r_{g_{2}\cdot y}\}`$.
    3.  (c)
        if $`g_{1}=g_{2}\wedge y_{0}=y_{1}`$,

        1.  i.
            for $`j=j_{2}`$,
            $`\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j_{2}}=\{r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}\}`$,
            $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}=B\pm O(\delta)`$
            ;
        2.  ii.
            for other $`j\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})`$, assuming
            $`j=\tau(g_{2}(y))`$,

            |  |  |  |
            |----|----|----|
            |  |
            ``` math
            \Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y}}=2(\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},0}+\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},1})B\pm O(\delta).
            ``` |  |

            and $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r}\ll-\varrho`$ for
            $`r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}\setminus\{r_{g_{2}\cdot y}\}`$.

###### Lemma D.39.

If
[Induction D.3](#A4.Thminduction3 "Induction D.3. ‣ D.5 Stage 2.3: Decrease of Gap and Convergence ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
holds for all iterations $`\in[T_{2,2,s},t)`$, given input
$`\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1}`$, then we have

1.  1.
    for $`\ell=1`$, if $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1}\notin\mathcal{E}_{1}`$,
    then
    $`1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{1}}=O\bigg(\frac{1}{d^{2\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},0\to\mathsf{ans},0}C_{B}}}\bigg)=O\Big(\frac{1}{\mathsf{poly}d}\Big)`$.
2.  2.
    $`\ell=2`$, if
    $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell-1}\notin\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{2}`$, then

    1.  (a)
        if $`g_{1}=g_{2}\wedge y_{0}\neq y_{1}`$,
        $`\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{2}(y_{0}))}=O\bigg(\frac{1}{d^{2\big(\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},0}\big)C_{B}}}\bigg)`$.
    2.  (b)
        if $`g_{1}\neq g_{2}\wedge y_{0}=y_{1}`$,
        $`\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{1}(y_{1}))}=O\bigg(\frac{1}{d^{2\big(\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},1}\big)C_{B}}}\bigg)`$.
    3.  (c)
        if $`g_{1}=g_{2}\wedge y_{0}=y_{1}`$,
        $`1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}}=O\bigg(\frac{1}{d^{2\big(\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},0}+\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},1}\big)C_{B}}}\bigg)=\frac{1}{\mathsf{poly}d}`$.

#### D.5.2 Gradient Lemma

###### Lemma D.40.

If
[Induction D.3](#A4.Thminduction3 "Induction D.3. ‣ D.5 Stage 2.3: Decrease of Gap and Convergence ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
holds for all iterations $`\in[T_{2,2,s},t)`$, given
$`s\in\tau(\mathcal{X})`$, for $`[\mathbf{Q}_{4,4}]_{s,s}`$, we have

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=1}^{2}\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,4}}{\mathsf{Loss}^{2,\ell}_{5}}\Big]_{s,s}\geq\Omega\Big(\frac{\epsilon\log d}{d^{(1-2\epsilon)C_{B}}}\Big).`$ |  |

###### Proof.

By gradient decomposition, we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=1}^{2}\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,4}}\mathsf{Loss}^{2,\ell}_{5}\Big]_{s,s}=\sum_{\ell\in[2]}\sum_{\kappa\in\{i,ii,iii\}}\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,4,\ell,\kappa}.`$ |  |

Firstly, for $`\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,4,2,i}`$, by
[Lemma D.35](#A4.Thmlemma35 "Lemma D.35. ‣ D.5.1 Attention and Logit Preliminaries ‣ D.5 Stage 2.3: Decrease of Gap and Convergence ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
and
[Lemma D.38](#A4.Thmlemma38 "Lemma D.38. ‣ D.5.1 Attention and Logit Preliminaries ‣ D.5 Stage 2.3: Decrease of Gap and Convergence ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
we have

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,4,2,i}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}\cdot(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}})\cdot`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~\Big(\Big(V_{j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}(y_{1})-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{1})=s\}\cap\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{2}}\Bigg]`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}\cdot(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}})\cdot`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~\Big(\Big(V_{j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}(y_{1})-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{1})=s\}\cap\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}^{c}_{2}}\Bigg]`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(a)}}{{\geq}}\Theta\Big(\frac{1}{d}\Big)\cdot\mathbb{E}\bigg[\min\Big\{\Omega(1),\Omega\Big(\frac{1}{d^{C_{B}\cdot(1-2\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{2,2})-1}}\Big)\Big\}\cdot 2\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{2,2}\cdot B\mid\tau(x_{1})=s\bigg]`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq\Omega\Big(\frac{\epsilon\log d}{d^{(1-2\epsilon)C_{B}}}\Big),`$ |  | (73) |

where (a) follows from
[Lemma D.37](#A4.Thmlemma37 "Lemma D.37. ‣ D.5.1 Attention and Logit Preliminaries ‣ D.5 Stage 2.3: Decrease of Gap and Convergence ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization").
Noticing that $`\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,4,1,i}>0`$ and
$`|\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,4,\ell,iii}|`$ for $`\ell\in[2]`$ is
sufficiently small, to provide a lower bound for
$`\sum_{\ell=1}^{2}\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,4}}\mathsf{Loss}^{2,\ell}_{5}\Big]_{s,s}`$,
we only need to focus on the negative gradient from
$`\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,4,1,ii}`$ and $`\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,4,2,ii}`$.

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,4,2,ii}=`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle-\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}\cdot\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{2}}\cdot`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~\Big(\Big(V_{j^{\prime}_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}(y_{1})-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{1})=s\}\cap\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{2}}\Bigg]`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle-\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}\cdot\sum_{j\neq j_{2}\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})}\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j}\cdot`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~\Big(\sum_{r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}}\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r})\cdot\Big(V_{j,r}(y_{1})-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{1})=s\}\cap\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}^{c}_{2}}\Bigg]`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq-\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}\cdot\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{1}(y_{1}))}\cdot\Big(\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{1}(y_{1})),r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{1}}})\cdot`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~\Big(V_{\tau(g_{1}(y_{1})),r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{1}}}(y_{1})-\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{1}(y_{1})),r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{1}}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{1})=s\}\cap\{g_{1}\neq g_{2},y_{0}=y_{1}\}}\Bigg].`$ |  | (74) |

When
$`\mathbb{E}\Big[C_{B}\cdot(1-2\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{2,2})\mid\tau(x_{1})=s\Big]<1`$,
by
[Lemma D.37](#A4.Thmlemma37 "Lemma D.37. ‣ D.5.1 Attention and Logit Preliminaries ‣ D.5 Stage 2.3: Decrease of Gap and Convergence ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
and
[Lemma D.39](#A4.Thmlemma39 "Lemma D.39. ‣ D.5.1 Attention and Logit Preliminaries ‣ D.5 Stage 2.3: Decrease of Gap and Convergence ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{1}(y_{1}))}\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{1})=s\}\cap\{g_{1}\neq g_{2},y_{0}=y_{1}\}}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq O\bigg(\frac{1}{d^{2\big(\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},1}\big)C_{B}}}\bigg)\big(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}}\big)\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{1})=s\}\cap\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{2}}.`$ |  |

During this time, $`\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{2,2}\geq\Omega(1)`$, thus
we can lower bound $`\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,4,2,ii}`$ by
$`\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,4,2,ii}\geq-O\bigg(\frac{1}{d^{\Omega(1)}\cdot\log d}\bigg)\cdot\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,4,2,i}`$,
which implies that the negative gradient from
$`\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,4,2,ii}`$ is dominated by the positive gradient
from $`\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,4,2,i}`$.

When
$`\mathbb{E}\Big[C_{B}\cdot(1-2\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{2,2})\mid\tau(x_{1})=s\Big]\geq 1`$,
since
$`2\big(\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},1}\big)C_{B}\geq(1-2\eta^{2,2})C_{B}`$,
we obtain

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{\tau(g_{1}(y_{1}))}\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{1})=s\}\cap\{g_{1}\neq g_{2},y_{0}=y_{1}\}}\leq O(\frac{1}{d})\cdot(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{j_{2}})\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{1})=s\}\cap\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{2}}.`$ |  | (75) |

For the event $`\{g_{1}\neq g_{2},y_{0}=y_{1}\}`$, if
$`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{1}(y_{1})),r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{1}}}`$ is still
in the linear regime, we have

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{1}(y_{1})),r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{1}}}=\big(1-2\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}\big)\cdot B\pm O(\delta)\geq\varrho,`$ |  |

which implies

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{2,2}\geq 1-2\mathbf{Attn}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}\geq\Omega\Big(\frac{\varrho}{B}\Big).`$ |  | (76) |

Hence, putting
([76](#A4.E76 "Equation 76 ‣ Proof. ‣ D.5.2 Gradient Lemma ‣ D.5 Stage 2.3: Decrease of Gap and Convergence ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"))
back to
([74](#A4.E74 "Equation 74 ‣ Proof. ‣ D.5.2 Gradient Lemma ‣ D.5 Stage 2.3: Decrease of Gap and Convergence ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")),
and putting
([75](#A4.E75 "Equation 75 ‣ Proof. ‣ D.5.2 Gradient Lemma ‣ D.5 Stage 2.3: Decrease of Gap and Convergence ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"))
back to
([74](#A4.E74 "Equation 74 ‣ Proof. ‣ D.5.2 Gradient Lemma ‣ D.5 Stage 2.3: Decrease of Gap and Convergence ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")),
we can lower bound $`\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,4,2,ii}`$ as follows
$`\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,4,2,ii}\geq-O\big(\frac{1}{d}\big)\cdot\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,4,2,i}`$.
If $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{1}(y_{1})),r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{1}}}`$ falls
into the smoothed regime, we can upper bound
$`\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{1}(y_{1})),r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{1}}})`$
by $`O(\frac{\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{2,2}B}{\varrho})`$, and then
similarly, we can obatian
$`\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,4,2,ii}\geq-O\big(\frac{1}{d}\big)\cdot\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,4,2,i}`$.

Following the analogous analysis, the negative gradient from
$`\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,4,1,ii}`$ can also be dominated by the positive
gradient from $`\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,4,2,i}`$. Hence, we complete the
proof. ∎

###### Lemma D.41.

If
[Induction D.3](#A4.Thminduction3 "Induction D.3. ‣ D.5 Stage 2.3: Decrease of Gap and Convergence ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
holds for all iterations $`\in[T_{2,2,s},t)`$, given
$`s\in\tau(\mathcal{X})`$, for $`[\mathbf{Q}_{4,p}]_{s,s^{\prime}}`$,
$`p\in\{3,4\}`$, $`s^{\prime}\not=s\in\tau(\mathcal{X})`$,
$`\ell\in[2]`$, we have

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\bigg|\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,p}}{\mathsf{Loss}^{2,\ell}_{5}}\Big]_{s,s^{\prime}}\bigg|\leq O\Big(\frac{1}{d}\Big)\bigg|\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,p}}{\mathsf{Loss}^{2,\ell}_{5}}\Big]_{s,s}\bigg|.`$ |  |

#### D.5.3 Non-negative Gap

###### Lemma D.42.

If
[Induction D.3](#A4.Thminduction3 "Induction D.3. ‣ D.5 Stage 2.3: Decrease of Gap and Convergence ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
holds for all iterations $`\in[T_{2,2,s},t)`$, then at time $`t`$, we
have $`{\Delta^{2,\ell}}\geq 0`$ for any $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,\ell}`$ with
$`\ell\in\{1,2\}`$.

###### Proof.

Let $`\widetilde{T}`$ denote the first time that
$`\mathbb{E}[\Delta^{2,2}\mid\tau(x_{1})=s]<\alpha`$, where
$`\alpha=\frac{\epsilon^{\frac{1}{\alpha-2}}}{\mathsf{poly}d}`$.

Following the analogous analysis as
[Lemma D.30](#A4.Thmlemma30 "Lemma D.30. ‣ D.4.2 Gradient Lemma ‣ D.4 Stage 2.2: Continual Growth of Diagonal Entries ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=1}^{2}\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{4,3}}\mathsf{Loss}^{2,\ell}_{5}\Big]_{s,s}+\sum_{\ell=1}^{2}\Big[\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{4,4}}\mathsf{Loss}^{2,\ell}_{5}\Big]_{s,s}=\sum_{\ell\in[2]}\sum_{\kappa\in\{i,ii,iii\}}\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,3,\ell,\kappa}-\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,4,\ell,\kappa}.`$ |  |

We can ignore the negligible difference introduced by
$`\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,p,\ell,iii}`$ for $`p\in\{3,4\}`$ and
$`\ell\in[2]`$.

For $`\ell=1`$, since
$`\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{{\mathsf{ans},0}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}\geq\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{{\mathsf{ans},0}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}`$,
and thus it is straightforward to see that
$`\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,3,1,i}-\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,4,1,i}\geq 0`$.
Furthermore, we have

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,3,1,ii}-\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,4,1,ii}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{{\mathsf{ans},0}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}\cdot\mathbf{logit}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j^{\prime}_{1}}\cdot\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}\big(\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{1},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}\big)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~\Big(\Big(V_{j^{\prime}_{1},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}(g_{1})-\Lambda^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j^{\prime}_{1},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{0})=s\}\cap\mathcal{E}_{1}}\Bigg]`$ |  | (77) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{{\mathsf{ans},0}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot\mathbf{logit}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j^{\prime}_{1}}\cdot\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}\big(\Lambda^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j^{\prime}_{1},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}\big)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~\Big(\Big(V_{j^{\prime}_{1},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}(y_{0})-\Lambda^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j^{\prime}_{1},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{0})=s\}\cap\mathcal{E}_{1}}\Bigg]`$ |  | (78) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle-\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{{\mathsf{ans},0}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}\cdot\sum_{y\neq y_{0}\in\mathcal{Y}}\mathbf{logit}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,\tau(g_{1}(y))}\cdot\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}\big(\Lambda^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,\tau(g_{1}(y)),r_{g_{1}\cdot y}}\big)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~\Big(\Big(V_{\tau(g_{1}(y)),r_{g_{1}\cdot y}}(g_{1})-\Lambda^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,\tau(g_{1}(y)),r_{g_{1}\cdot y}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{0})=s\}\cap\mathcal{E}^{c}_{1}}\Bigg]`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{{\mathsf{ans},0}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot\sum_{y\neq y_{0}\in\mathcal{Y}}\mathbf{logit}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,\tau(g_{1}(y))}\cdot\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}\big(\Lambda^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,\tau(g_{1}(y)),r_{g_{1}\cdot y}}\big)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~\Big(\Big(V_{\tau(g_{1}(y)),r_{g_{1}\cdot y}}(y_{0})-\Lambda^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,\tau(g_{1}(y)),r_{g_{1}\cdot y}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{0})=s\}\cap\mathcal{E}^{c}_{1}}\Bigg].`$ |  |  |

By
[Lemma D.39](#A4.Thmlemma39 "Lemma D.39. ‣ D.5.1 Attention and Logit Preliminaries ‣ D.5 Stage 2.3: Decrease of Gap and Convergence ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
we obtain that
$`\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,3,1,ii}-\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,4,1,ii}`$
is dominated by $`\eqref{eq-s23-gd-gap-1}+\eqref{eq-s23-gd-gap-2}`$.
Moreover, by
[Lemma D.37](#A4.Thmlemma37 "Lemma D.37. ‣ D.5.1 Attention and Logit Preliminaries ‣ D.5 Stage 2.3: Decrease of Gap and Convergence ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
we have

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathbf{logit}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,\tau(g_{1}(y))}(\mathbf{Z}^{2,0})`$ | $`\displaystyle\geq\Omega\Bigg(\frac{1}{d^{2\big(\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{\mathsf{ans},0\to\mathsf{pred},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{\mathsf{ans},0\to\mathsf{pred},2}\big)C_{B}}}\Bigg)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq\Omega\Bigg(\frac{1}{d^{2\big(\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},1}\big)C_{B}}}\Bigg).`$ |  | (79) |

For $`\ell=2`$, we have

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,3,2,i}-\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,4,2,i}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\cdot(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j_{2}})\cdot`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~\Big(\Big(V_{j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}(g_{2})-\Lambda^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{1})=s\}\cap\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{2}}\Bigg]`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle-\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}\cdot(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j_{2}})\cdot`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~\Big(\Big(V_{j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}(y_{1})-\Lambda^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{1})=s\}\cap\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{2}}\Bigg]`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq\Omega\big(\alpha\epsilon\log d\big)\mathbb{E}\bigg[(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j_{2}})\cdot\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s}\bigg],`$ |  | (80) |

where the last inequality follows from the definition of
$`\widetilde{T}`$.

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,3,2,ii}-\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,4,2,ii}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\cdot\mathbf{logit}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j^{\prime}_{2}}\cdot\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}\big(\Lambda^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j^{\prime}_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}\big)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~\Big(\Big(V_{j^{\prime}_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}(g_{2})-\Lambda^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j^{\prime}_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{1})=s\}\cap\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{2}}\Bigg]`$ |  | (81) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}\cdot\mathbf{logit}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j^{\prime}_{2}}\cdot\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}\big(\Lambda^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j^{\prime}_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}\big)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~\Big(\Big(V_{j^{\prime}_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}(y_{1})-\Lambda^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j^{\prime}_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{1})=s\}\cap\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{2}}\Bigg]`$ |  | (82) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle-\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\cdot\sum_{j\neq j_{2}\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})}\mathbf{logit}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j}\cdot`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~\Big(\sum_{r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}}\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j,r})\cdot\Big(V_{j,r}(g_{2})-\Lambda^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j,r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{1})=s\}\cap\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}^{c}_{2}}\Bigg]`$ |  | (83) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}\cdot\sum_{j\neq j_{2}\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})}\mathbf{logit}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j}\cdot`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~\Big(\sum_{r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}}\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j,r})\cdot\Big(V_{j,r}(y_{1})-\Lambda^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j,r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\{\tau(x_{1})=s\}\cap\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}^{c}_{2}}\Bigg].`$ |  | (84) |

Notice that by
[Lemma D.36](#A4.Thmlemma36 "Lemma D.36. ‣ D.5.1 Attention and Logit Preliminaries ‣ D.5 Stage 2.3: Decrease of Gap and Convergence ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
and the definition of $`\widetilde{T}`$, we have

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle|\eqref{eq-s23-cyc-gd1}+\eqref{eq-s23-cyc-gd2}|\leq O\big((\alpha\log d)^{q-1}\big)\cdot\mathbb{E}\bigg[\Big(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j_{2}}\Big)\cdot\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s}\bigg],`$ |  |

which is dominated by
([80](#A4.E80 "Equation 80 ‣ Proof. ‣ D.5.3 Non-negative Gap ‣ D.5 Stage 2.3: Decrease of Gap and Convergence ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"))
due to the choice of $`\alpha`$.

Furthermore, by
[Lemma D.38](#A4.Thmlemma38 "Lemma D.38. ‣ D.5.1 Attention and Logit Preliminaries ‣ D.5 Stage 2.3: Decrease of Gap and Convergence ‣ Appendix D Learning the Attention Layer: Simply Transitive Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
````
