````math
        |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~+\big(1-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\big)\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{1}}\bigg]`$ |  |  |
        |  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq\Omega\Big(\frac{B}{d}\Big)\cdot\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}})\big|\tau(x_{1})=s,\mathcal{E}_{1}\bigg].`$ |  | (109) |

        On the other hand,

        |  |  |  |  |
        |----|----|----|----|
        |  |  | $`\displaystyle-\mathbb{E}\bigg[(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}})\cdot\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}})\cdot`$ |  |
        |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~\Big(\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot V_{j_{2},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}(y_{0})+\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}\cdot V_{j_{2},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}(g_{1})\Big)`$ |  |
        |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{1}}\mathds{1}_{g_{1}(y_{0})=g_{2}(y_{1})}\bigg]`$ |  |
        |  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq-O\big(\frac{B}{d\cdot n_{y}}\big)\cdot\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}})\big|\tau(x_{1})=s,\mathcal{E}_{1}\bigg],`$ |  |

        which implies that
        $`\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,2,i}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{1}}\bigg]\geq\Omega\Big(\frac{B}{d}\Big)\cdot\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}})\big|\tau(x_{1})=s,\mathcal{E}_{1}\bigg]\geq 0`$.
        Moving to $`\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,2,ii}`$, by
        [Lemma E.10](#A5.Thmlemma10 "Lemma E.10 (Gradient sum of 𝐐_{4,3} and 𝐐_{4,4}). ‣ Notations for activated neurons. ‣ E.2 Some Useful Bounds for Gradients ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
        and
        [Lemma E.13](#A5.Thmlemma13 "Lemma E.13. ‣ E.3.1 Attention and Lambda Preliminaries ‣ E.3 Stage 1.2.1: Initial Growth of 𝐐 ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
        we have

        |  |  |  |  |
        |----|----|----|----|
        |  |  | $`\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,2,ii}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{1}}\bigg]`$ |  |
        |  |  | $`\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\bigg[\sum_{j\neq j_{2}\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})}\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j}\cdot\Big(\sum_{r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}}\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda_{5,j,r})\cdot`$ |  |
        |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~\Big(\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot V_{j,r}(y_{0})+\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}\cdot V_{j,r}(g_{1})`$ |  |
        |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~-\big(1-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\big)\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{1}}\bigg]`$ |  |
        |  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq-{O}\Big(\frac{n_{y}\cdot B}{d^{2}}\Big).`$ |  |
    2.  (b)
        For $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,1}\in\mathcal{E}_{2}`$, by
        [Induction E.1](#A5.Thminduction1 "Induction E.1. ‣ E.3 Stage 1.2.1: Initial Growth of 𝐐 ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
        and
        [Lemma E.14](#A5.Thmlemma14 "Lemma E.14. ‣ E.3.1 Attention and Lambda Preliminaries ‣ E.3 Stage 1.2.1: Initial Growth of 𝐐 ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
        we can also obtain a crude bound of logit:
        $`\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j}\leq O\big(\frac{1}{d}\big)`$ for
        $`j\neq j^{\prime}_{2}`$. Then $`\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,2,i}`$ can
        be bounded in the same way as
        ([109](#A5.E109 "Equation 109 ‣ Item 1a ‣ Item 1 ‣ Proof. ‣ E.3.2 Gradient Lemma ‣ E.3 Stage 1.2.1: Initial Growth of 𝐐 ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")),
        and we have

        |  |  |  |
        |----|----|----|
        |  | $`\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,2,i}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{2}}\bigg]\geq\Omega\Big(\frac{B}{d\cdot n_{y}}\Big)\cdot\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}})\big|\tau(x_{1})=s,\mathcal{E}_{2}\bigg]\geq 0.`$ |  |

        Moving to $`\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,2,ii}`$,

        - •
          for $`j=\tau(g_{1}(y_{1}))`$, we have

          |  |  |  |  |
          |----|----|----|----|
          |  |  | $`\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbf{logit}_{5,j}^{(t)}\Big(\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda_{5,j,r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{1}}})\cdot`$ |  |
          |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~\Big(\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot V_{j,r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{1}}}(y_{0})+\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}\cdot V_{j,r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{1}}}(g_{1})`$ |  |
          |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~-\big(1-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\big)\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{2}}\bigg]`$ |  |
          |  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq-{O}\Big(\frac{B}{n^{2}_{y}\cdot d^{2}}\Big).`$ |  |

          where the last inequality is due to the cancellation of the
          term
          $`\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot V_{j,r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{1}}}(y_{0})+\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}\cdot V_{j,r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{1}}}(g_{1})`$
          and the fact that

          |  |  |  |
          |----|----|----|
          |  | $`\displaystyle\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{1}(y_{1})),r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{1}}}=\Big(\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}\Big)\cdot\frac{2B}{n_{y}}+O(\delta)\leq O\big(\frac{B}{n_{y}}\big).`$ |  |
        - •
          for $`j=\tau(g_{2}(y))`$ if there exists $`y\not=y_{0},y_{1}`$
          s.t., $`g_{1}(y)=g_{2}(y)`$

          - –
            when
            $`\mathbb{E}\big[\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\mid\tau(x_{1})=s\big]\leq 0.01`$,
            by
            [Induction E.1](#A5.Thminduction1 "Induction E.1. ‣ E.3 Stage 1.2.1: Initial Growth of 𝐐 ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
            and
            [Lemma E.14](#A5.Thmlemma14 "Lemma E.14. ‣ E.3.1 Attention and Lambda Preliminaries ‣ E.3 Stage 1.2.1: Initial Growth of 𝐐 ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
            we have
            $`\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{2}(y))}\ll O\big(\frac{1}{d}\big)\cdot\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}^{\prime}}`$
            and $`\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}^{\prime}}=\Omega(1)`$.
            Then

            |  |  |  |  |
            |----|----|----|----|
            |  |  | $`\displaystyle\bigg|\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j}\cdot\Big(\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r_{g_{1}\cdot y}})\cdot`$ |  |
            |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~\Big(\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot V_{j,r_{g_{1}\cdot y}}(y_{0})+\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}\cdot V_{j,r_{g_{1}\cdot y}}(g_{1})`$ |  |
            |  |  | $`\displaystyle-\big(1-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\big)\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r_{g_{1}\cdot y}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{2}}\bigg]\bigg|`$ |  |
            |  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq{O}\Big(\frac{B}{n_{y}\cdot d^{2}}\Big).`$ |  |

            On the other hand,

            |  |  |  |  |
            |----|----|----|----|
            |  |  | $`\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{2}}\cdot\Big(\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{2},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}})\cdot`$ |  |
            |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~\Big(\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot V_{j^{\prime}_{2},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}(y_{0})+\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}\cdot V_{j^{\prime}_{2},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}(g_{1})`$ |  |
            |  |  | $`\displaystyle-\big(1-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\big)\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{2},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)`$ |  |
            |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{1}}\bigg]`$ |  |
            |  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq{\Omega}\Big(\frac{B}{n_{y}\cdot d}\Big),`$ |  |

            where the last inequality follows from the fact that

            |  |  |  |  |
            |----|----|----|----|
            |  |  | $`\displaystyle\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot V_{j^{\prime}_{2},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}(y_{0})+\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}\cdot V_{j^{\prime}_{2},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}(g_{1})`$ |  |
            |  |  | $`\displaystyle-\big(1-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\big)\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{2},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}`$ |  |
            |  |  | $`\displaystyle=\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}\cdot\Big(2B-2\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{2},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}\Big)\pm O(\delta)\geq\Omega(B).`$ |  |
          - –
            when
            $`\mathbb{E}\big[\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\mid\tau(x_{1})=s\big]\geq 0.01`$,
            by
            [Lemma E.14](#A5.Thmlemma14 "Lemma E.14. ‣ E.3.1 Attention and Lambda Preliminaries ‣ E.3 Stage 1.2.1: Initial Growth of 𝐐 ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
            we have $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r_{g_{1}\cdot y}}`$ cannot be
            activated. Furthermore,

            |  |  |  |  |
            |----|----|----|----|
            |  |  | $`\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{2}}\cdot\Big(\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{2},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}})\cdot`$ |  |
            |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~\Big(\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot V_{j^{\prime}_{2},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}(y_{0})+\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}\cdot V_{j^{\prime}_{2},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}(g_{1})`$ |  |
            |  |  | $`\displaystyle-\big(1-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\big)\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{2},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{1}}\bigg]`$ |  |
            |  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq 0.`$ |  |

        Putting the above discussion together, we have

        |  |  |  |
        |----|----|----|
        |  | $`\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,2,ii}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{2}}\bigg]\geq-O\Big(\frac{B}{n^{2}_{y}\cdot d^{2}}\Big).`$ |  |
    3.  (c)
        For $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,1}\in\mathcal{E}_{3}`$, by
        [Induction E.1](#A5.Thminduction1 "Induction E.1. ‣ E.3 Stage 1.2.1: Initial Growth of 𝐐 ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
        and
        [Lemma E.15](#A5.Thmlemma15 "Lemma E.15. ‣ E.3.1 Attention and Lambda Preliminaries ‣ E.3 Stage 1.2.1: Initial Growth of 𝐐 ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
        we can first have some facts of logit:

        |  |  |  |  |
        |----|----|----|----|
        |  | $`\displaystyle 1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}}=\Omega(1),\quad`$ | $`\displaystyle\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{1}(y_{1}))}=\Omega(1)`$ |  |
        |  | $`\displaystyle\mathbf{logit}_{5,j}^{(t)}\leq\frac{1}{\mathsf{poly}d}`$ | $`\displaystyle\text{ for other }j.`$ |  |

        Therefore, we have

        |  |  |  |  |
        |----|----|----|----|
        |  |  | $`\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,2,i}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{3}}\bigg]`$ |  |
        |  |  | $`\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\bigg[(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}})\cdot\Big(-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot V_{j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}(y_{1})-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}\cdot V_{j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}(g_{1})`$ |  |
        |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~+\big(1-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\big)\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{3}}\bigg]`$ |  |
        |  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq\Omega\Big(\frac{B}{n_{y}\cdot d}\Big).`$ |  |

        Moving to $`\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,2,ii}`$, we have

        |  |  |  |  |
        |----|----|----|----|
        |  |  | $`\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,2,ii}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{3}}\bigg]`$ |  |
        |  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{1}(y_{1}))}\cdot`$ |  |
        |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~\Big(\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot V_{\tau(g_{1}(y_{1})),r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{1}}}(y_{1})+\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}\cdot V_{\tau(g_{1}(y_{1})),r}(g_{1})`$ |  |
        |  |  | $`\displaystyle-\big(1-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\big)\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{1}(y_{1})),r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{1}}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})`$ |  |
        |  |  | $`\displaystyle\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{3}}\bigg]-{O}\Big(\frac{n_{y}\cdot B}{d\cdot\mathsf{poly}d}\Big)`$ |  |
        |  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq\Omega\Big(\frac{B}{n_{y}\cdot d}\Big)-{O}\Big(\frac{B}{d\cdot\mathsf{poly}d}\Big)=\Omega\Big(\frac{B}{n_{y}\cdot d}\Big).`$ |  |
    4.  (d)
        For $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,1}\in\mathcal{E}_{4}`$, by comparing
        [Lemma E.16](#A5.Thmlemma16 "Lemma E.16. ‣ E.3.1 Attention and Lambda Preliminaries ‣ E.3 Stage 1.2.1: Initial Growth of 𝐐 ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
        with
        [Lemma E.14](#A5.Thmlemma14 "Lemma E.14. ‣ E.3.1 Attention and Lambda Preliminaries ‣ E.3 Stage 1.2.1: Initial Growth of 𝐐 ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
        we can directly bound $`\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,2,ii}`$ in the same
        way as $`\mathcal{E}_{2}`$, where the only difference is that we
        do not need to consider $`\tau(g_{1}(y_{1}))`$ for
        $`\mathcal{E}_{4}`$. Thus
        $`\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,2,ii}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{4}}\bigg]\geq 0`$.
        Moreover, it is clear that

        |  |  |  |  |
        |----|----|----|----|
        |  |  | $`\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,2,i}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{4}}\bigg]`$ |  |
        |  |  | $`\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\bigg[(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}})\cdot\Big(\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}})\cdot`$ |  |
        |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~\Big(-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot V_{j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}(y_{0})-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}\cdot V_{j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}(g_{1})`$ |  |
        |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~+\big(1-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\big)\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{4}}\bigg]`$ |  |
        |  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq 0.`$ |  |

        where the last inequality is due to the cancellation of
        $`\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot V_{j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}(y_{0})+\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}\cdot V_{j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}(g_{1})`$,
        and the fact that

        |  |  |  |
        |----|----|----|
        |  | $`\displaystyle\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}=\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}\cdot 2B+O(\delta)\geq\Omega(B).`$ |  |
    5.  (e)
        For $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,1}\in\mathcal{E}_{5}\cup\mathcal{E}_{6}`$,
        by
        [Induction E.1](#A5.Thminduction1 "Induction E.1. ‣ E.3 Stage 1.2.1: Initial Growth of 𝐐 ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
        [Lemma E.17](#A5.Thmlemma17 "Lemma E.17. ‣ E.3.1 Attention and Lambda Preliminaries ‣ E.3 Stage 1.2.1: Initial Growth of 𝐐 ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
        and
        [Lemma E.18](#A5.Thmlemma18 "Lemma E.18. ‣ E.3.1 Attention and Lambda Preliminaries ‣ E.3 Stage 1.2.1: Initial Growth of 𝐐 ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
        we can derive the following logit condition:
        $`1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}},\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j}\leq\frac{1}{\mathsf{poly}d}`$
        for $`j\neq j_{2}`$. Hence, we can simply bound
        $`\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,2,i}`$ and $`\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,2,ii}`$
        as follows:

        |  |  |  |
        |----|----|----|
        |  | $`\displaystyle\bigg|\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,2,i}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{m}}\bigg]\bigg|,\bigg|\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,2,ii}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{m}}\bigg]\bigg|\leq O\Big(\frac{B}{dn_{y}}\cdot\frac{1}{\mathsf{poly}d}\Big)\text{ for }m\in\{5,6\}.`$ |  |

    Putting it all together, we have for the regmie (1),

    |  |  |  |
    |----|----|----|
    |  | $`\displaystyle\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}}{\mathsf{Loss}^{2,2}_{5}}\Big]_{s,s}+\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,4}}{\mathsf{Loss}^{2,2}_{5}}\Big]_{s,s}=\sum_{\kappa\in\{i,ii,iii\}}\sum_{i\in[6]}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,2,\kappa}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}\bigg]\geq\Omega(\frac{B}{d\cdot n_{y}}).`$ |  |
2.  2.
    In regime (ii), the analysis follows a structure analogous to that
    of regime (i). The primary distinction lies in the fact that, under
    the main event $`\mathcal{E}_{1}`$, the term
    $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}`$ is guaranteed to
    remain within the linear regime, thereby serving as the dominant
    component driving the overall gradient.

    1.  (a)
        For $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,1}\in\mathcal{E}_{1}`$, by
        [Induction E.1](#A5.Thminduction1 "Induction E.1. ‣ E.3 Stage 1.2.1: Initial Growth of 𝐐 ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
        and
        [Lemma E.13](#A5.Thmlemma13 "Lemma E.13. ‣ E.3.1 Attention and Lambda Preliminaries ‣ E.3 Stage 1.2.1: Initial Growth of 𝐐 ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
        we have

        |  |  |  |  |
        |----|----|----|----|
        |  |  | $`\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,2,i}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{1}}\bigg]`$ |  |
        |  |  | $`\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\bigg[(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}})\cdot\Big(\sum_{r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j_{2}}}\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r})\cdot`$ |  |
        |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~\Big(-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot V_{j_{2},r}(y_{0})-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}\cdot V_{j_{2},r}(g_{1})`$ |  |
        |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~+\big(1-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\big)\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{1}}\bigg].`$ |  |

        By
        [Lemma E.13](#A5.Thmlemma13 "Lemma E.13. ‣ E.3.1 Attention and Lambda Preliminaries ‣ E.3 Stage 1.2.1: Initial Growth of 𝐐 ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
        we can obtain

        |  |  |  |  |  |
        |----|----|----|----|----|
        |  |  | $`\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}})\cdot\Big(\Big(-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot V_{j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}(y_{0})-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}\cdot V_{j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}(g_{1})`$ |  |  |
        |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~+\big(1-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\big)\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{1}}\bigg]`$ |  | (110) |
        |  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq\Omega\Big(\frac{B}{d}\Big)\cdot\mathbb{E}\bigg[(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}})\big|\tau(x_{1})=s,\mathcal{E}_{1}\bigg].`$ |  |  |

        On the other hand,

        |  |  |  |  |
        |----|----|----|----|
        |  |  | $`\displaystyle-\mathbb{E}\bigg[(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}})\cdot\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}})\cdot`$ |  |
        |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~\Big(\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot V_{j_{2},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}(y_{0})+\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}\cdot V_{j_{2},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}(g_{1})\Big)`$ |  |
        |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{1}}\mathds{1}_{g_{1}(y_{0})=g_{2}(y_{1})}\bigg]`$ |  |
        |  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq-O\big(\frac{B}{d\cdot n_{y}}\big)\cdot\mathbb{E}\bigg[(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}})\big|\tau(x_{1})=s,\mathcal{E}_{1}\bigg],`$ |  |

        which implies that
        $`\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,2,i}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{1}}\bigg]\geq\Omega\Big(\frac{B}{d}\Big)\cdot\mathbb{E}\bigg[(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}})\big|\tau(x_{1})=s,\mathcal{E}_{1}\bigg]\geq 0`$.
        Moving to $`\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,2,ii}`$, by
        [Lemma E.13](#A5.Thmlemma13 "Lemma E.13. ‣ E.3.1 Attention and Lambda Preliminaries ‣ E.3 Stage 1.2.1: Initial Growth of 𝐐 ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
        we have

        - •
          for $`j=\tau(g_{1}(y_{0}))`$, $`r=r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}`$

          |  |  |  |  |
          |----|----|----|----|
          |  |  | $`\displaystyle\Big(\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot V_{j,r}(y_{0})+\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}\cdot V_{j,r}(g_{1})`$ |  |
          |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~-\big(1-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\big)\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\geq 0.`$ |  |
        - •
          for $`j=\tau(g_{1}(y_{1}))`$, $`r=r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{1}}`$, due
          to the cancellation of
          $`\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot V_{j,r}(y_{0})+\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}\cdot V_{j,r}(g_{1})`$,
          we have

          |  |  |  |  |
          |----|----|----|----|
          |  |  | $`\displaystyle\Big(\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot V_{j,r}(y_{0})+\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}\cdot V_{j,r}(g_{1})`$ |  |
          |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~-\big(1-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\big)\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)`$ |  |
          |  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq-\big(1-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\big)\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r}.`$ |  |
        - •
          for $`j=\tau(g_{2}(y_{0}))`$, $`r=r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}`$

          |  |  |  |  |
          |----|----|----|----|
          |  |  | $`\displaystyle\Big(\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot V_{j,r}(y_{0})+\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}\cdot V_{j,r}(g_{1})`$ |  |
          |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~-\big(1-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\big)\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)`$ |  |
          |  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq\big(1-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\big)\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r}.`$ |  |
        - •
          for $`j=g_{1}(y)`$, where $`\exists y\neq y_{0},y_{1}`$, s.t.,
          $`g_{2}(y)=g_{1}(y)`$, we have

          |  |  |  |  |
          |----|----|----|----|
          |  |  | $`\displaystyle\Big(\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot V_{j,r}(y_{0})+\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}\cdot V_{j,r}(g_{1})`$ |  |
          |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~-\big(1-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\big)\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)`$ |  |
          |  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq-\big(1-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\big)\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r}.`$ |  |

        Putting them together, and upper bound
        $`\sum_{j\neq j_{2}\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})}\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j}`$
        by $`1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}}`$, we have

        |  |  |  |  |
        |----|----|----|----|
        |  |  | $`\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,2,ii}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{1}}\bigg]`$ |  |
        |  |  | $`\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\bigg[\sum_{j\neq j_{2}\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})}\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j}\cdot\Big(\sum_{r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}}\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda_{5,j,r})\cdot`$ |  |
        |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~\Big(\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot V_{j,r}(y_{0})+\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}\cdot V_{j,r}(g_{1})`$ |  |
        |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~-\big(1-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\big)\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{1}}\bigg]`$ |  |
        |  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq-\mathbb{E}\bigg[\Big(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}}\Big)\cdot\big(1-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\big)`$ |  |
        |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~\cdot\max_{y\neq y_{0},y_{1}}\Big\{\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{1}(y_{1})),r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{1}}},\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{2}(y_{0})),r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}\Big\}\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{1}}\bigg],`$ |  |

        where the last inequality is due to the fact that
        $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{2}(y_{0})),r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}=\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{2}(y)),r_{g_{2}\cdot y}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\frac{B}{d\cdot n_{y}})`$
        for $`y\neq y_{0},y_{1}`$ s.t., $`g_{1}(y)=g_{2}(y)`$. Notice
        that

        |  |  |  |
        |----|----|----|
        |  | $`\displaystyle\max_{y\neq y_{0},y_{1}}\Big\{\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{1}(y_{1})),r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{1}}},\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{2}(y_{0})),r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}\Big\}\leq`$ |  |
        |  | $`\displaystyle\max\bigg\{\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1},\Theta(\frac{1}{n_{y}})\bigg\}2B,`$ |  |

        while

        |  |  |  |  |
        |----|----|----|----|
        |  | ([110](#A5.E110 "Equation 110 ‣ Item 2a ‣ Item 2 ‣ Proof. ‣ E.3.2 Gradient Lemma ‣ E.3 Stage 1.2.1: Initial Growth of 𝐐 ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")) | $`\displaystyle\geq\mathbb{E}\bigg[\Big(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}}\Big)\cdot\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot 2B\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{1}}\bigg]`$ |  |
        |  |  | $`\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\Big(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}}\Big)\cdot\big(1-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\big)2B\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{1}}\bigg].`$ |  |

        Since by
        [Induction E.1](#A5.Thminduction1 "Induction E.1. ‣ E.3 Stage 1.2.1: Initial Growth of 𝐐 ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
        $`\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}\leq c_{1}\ll\frac{1}{2}`$,
        thus we have

        |  |  |  |
        |----|----|----|
        |  | $`\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,2,i}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{1}}\bigg]+\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,2,ii}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{1}}\bigg]\geq\Omega\Big(\frac{B}{d}\Big)\cdot\mathbb{E}\bigg[(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}})\big|\tau(x_{1})=s,\mathcal{E}_{1}\bigg].`$ |  |
    2.  (b)
        For $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,1}\in\mathcal{E}_{2}`$,
        $`\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,2,i}`$ can be bounded in the same way as
        ([110](#A5.E110 "Equation 110 ‣ Item 2a ‣ Item 2 ‣ Proof. ‣ E.3.2 Gradient Lemma ‣ E.3 Stage 1.2.1: Initial Growth of 𝐐 ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")),
        and we have

        |  |  |  |
        |----|----|----|
        |  | $`\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,2,i}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{2}}\bigg]\geq\Omega\Big(\frac{B}{d\cdot n_{y}}\Big)\cdot\mathbb{E}\bigg[(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}})\big|\tau(x_{1})=s,\mathcal{E}_{2}\bigg]\geq 0.`$ |  |

        Moving to $`\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,2,ii}`$,

        - •
          for $`j=\tau(g_{1}(y_{1}))`$, we have

          |  |  |  |  |  |
          |----|----|----|----|----|
          |  |  | $`\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbf{logit}_{5,j}^{(t)}\Big(\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda_{5,j,r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{1}}})\cdot`$ |  |  |
          |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~\Big(\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot V_{j,r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{1}}}(y_{0})+\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}\cdot V_{j,r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{1}}}(g_{1})`$ |  | (111) |
          |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~-\big(1-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\big)\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{2}}\bigg]`$ |  |  |
          |  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq-{O}\Big(\frac{B}{n^{2}_{y}\cdot d}\Big)\cdot\mathbb{E}\bigg[(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}})\big|\tau(x_{1})=s,\mathcal{E}_{2}\bigg].`$ |  |  |

          where the last inequality is due to the cancellation of the
          term
          $`\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot V_{j,r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{1}}}(y_{0})+\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}\cdot V_{j,r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{1}}}(g_{1})`$
          and the fact that

          |  |  |  |
          |----|----|----|
          |  | $`\displaystyle\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{1}(y_{1})),r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{1}}}=\Big(\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}\Big)\cdot\frac{2B}{n_{y}}+O(\delta)\leq O\big(\frac{B}{n_{y}}\big).`$ |  |
        - •
          for $`j^{\prime}_{2}=\tau(g_{2}(y_{0}))=\tau(g_{1}(y_{0}))`$,
          clearly, we have

          |  |  |  |  |
          |----|----|----|----|
          |  |  | $`\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{2}}\cdot\Big(\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{2},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}})\cdot`$ |  |
          |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~\Big(\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot V_{j^{\prime}_{2},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}(y_{0})+\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}\cdot V_{j^{\prime}_{2},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}(g_{1})`$ |  |
          |  |  | $`\displaystyle-\big(1-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\big)\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{2},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)`$ |  |
          |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{2}}\bigg]\geq 0,`$ |  |

          where the last inequality is due to the fact that

          |  |  |  |  |
          |----|----|----|----|
          |  |  | $`\displaystyle\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}\cdot V_{j^{\prime}_{2},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}(g_{1})-\big(1-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}`$ |  |
          |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\big)\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{2},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{0}}}`$ |  |
          |  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}`$ |  |
          |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~\cdot\bigg(1-2\Big(\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}+\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}\Big)\bigg)2B`$ |  |
          |  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}\bigg(1-2\Big(c_{1}+0.25\Big)\bigg)2B\geq 0.`$ |  |
        - •
          for $`j=\tau(g_{2}(y))`$ if there exists $`y\not=y_{0},y_{1}`$
          s.t., $`g_{1}(y)=g_{2}(y)`$

          - –
            when
            $`\mathbb{E}\big[\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\mid\tau(x_{1})=s\big]\leq 0.01`$,
            by
            [Induction E.1](#A5.Thminduction1 "Induction E.1. ‣ E.3 Stage 1.2.1: Initial Growth of 𝐐 ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
            and
            [Lemma E.14](#A5.Thmlemma14 "Lemma E.14. ‣ E.3.1 Attention and Lambda Preliminaries ‣ E.3 Stage 1.2.1: Initial Growth of 𝐐 ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
            we have
            $`\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{2}(y))}\ll O\big(\frac{1}{d}\big)\cdot\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}^{\prime}}`$.
            Then

            |  |  |  |  |
            |----|----|----|----|
            |  |  | $`\displaystyle\bigg|\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j}\cdot\Big(\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r_{g_{1}\cdot y}})\cdot`$ |  |
            |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~\Big(\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot V_{j,r_{g_{1}\cdot y}}(y_{0})+\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}\cdot V_{j,r_{g_{1}\cdot y}}(g_{1})`$ |  |
            |  |  | $`\displaystyle-\big(1-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\big)\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r_{g_{1}\cdot y}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{2}}\bigg]\bigg|`$ |  |
            |  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq{O}\Big(\frac{1}{d}\Big)\cdot\eqref{eq-s21-gd1-non-3}.`$ |  |
          - –
            when
            $`\mathbb{E}\big[\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\mid\tau(x_{1})=s\big]\geq 0.01`$,
            by
            [Lemma E.14](#A5.Thmlemma14 "Lemma E.14. ‣ E.3.1 Attention and Lambda Preliminaries ‣ E.3 Stage 1.2.1: Initial Growth of 𝐐 ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
            we have $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r_{g_{1}\cdot y}}`$ cannot be
            activated.

        Putting the above discussion together, we have

        |  |  |  |
        |----|----|----|
        |  | $`\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,2,ii}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{1}}\bigg]+\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,2,ii}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{2}}\bigg]\geq\Omega\Big(\frac{B}{d\cdot n_{y}}\Big)\cdot\mathbb{E}\bigg[(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}})\big|\tau(x_{1})=s,\mathcal{E}_{2}\bigg].`$ |  |
    3.  (c)
        For $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,1}\in\mathcal{E}_{3}`$, by
        [Induction E.1](#A5.Thminduction1 "Induction E.1. ‣ E.3 Stage 1.2.1: Initial Growth of 𝐐 ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
        and
        [Lemma E.15](#A5.Thmlemma15 "Lemma E.15. ‣ E.3.1 Attention and Lambda Preliminaries ‣ E.3 Stage 1.2.1: Initial Growth of 𝐐 ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
        we can first have the following logit bound:

        |  |  |  |  |
        |----|----|----|----|
        |  | $`\displaystyle\mathbf{logit}_{5,j}^{(t)}\leq\frac{1}{\mathsf{poly}d}\cdot\Big(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}}\Big)`$ | $`\displaystyle\text{ for }j\neq j_{2},\tau\big(g_{1}(y_{1})\big).`$ |  |

        Therefore, we have

        |  |  |  |  |
        |----|----|----|----|
        |  |  | $`\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,2,i}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{3}}\bigg]`$ |  |
        |  |  | $`\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\bigg[(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}})\cdot\Big(-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot V_{j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}(y_{1})-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}\cdot V_{j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}(g_{1})`$ |  |
        |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~+\big(1-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\big)\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{3}}\bigg]`$ |  |
        |  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq\Omega\Big(\frac{B}{n_{y}\cdot d}\Big)\cdot\mathbb{E}\bigg[(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}})\big|\tau(x_{1})=s,\mathcal{E}_{3}\bigg].`$ |  |

        Moving to $`\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,2,ii}`$, we have

        |  |  |  |  |
        |----|----|----|----|
        |  |  | $`\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,2,ii}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{3}}\bigg]`$ |  |
        |  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{1}(y_{1}))}\cdot`$ |  |
        |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~\Big(\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot V_{\tau(g_{1}(y_{1})),r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{1}}}(y_{1})+\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}\cdot V_{\tau(g_{1}(y_{1})),r}(g_{1})`$ |  |
        |  |  | $`\displaystyle-\big(1-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\big)\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{1}(y_{1})),r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{1}}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)`$ |  |
        |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{3}}\bigg]`$ |  |
        |  |  | $`\displaystyle-{O}\Big(\frac{n_{y}\cdot B}{n_{y}\cdot d\cdot\mathsf{poly}d}\Big)\cdot\mathbb{E}\bigg[(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}})\big|\tau(x_{1})=s,\mathcal{E}_{3}\bigg].`$ |  |

        Thus,

        |  |  |  |
        |----|----|----|
        |  | $`\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,2,ii}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{3}}\bigg]+\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,2,ii}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{3}}\bigg]\geq\Omega\Big(\frac{B}{n_{y}\cdot d}\Big)\cdot\mathbb{E}\bigg[(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}})\big|\tau(x_{1})=s,\mathcal{E}_{3}\bigg].`$ |  |
    4.  (d)
        For $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,1}\in\mathcal{E}_{4}`$, we can bound the
        gradient in a manner similar to regime (i), and obtain

        |  |  |  |
        |----|----|----|
        |  | $`\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,2,ii}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{4}}\bigg]+\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,2,ii}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{4}}\bigg]\geq 0.`$ |  |
    5.  (e)
        For $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,1}\in\mathcal{E}_{5}\cup\mathcal{E}_{6}`$,
        by
        [Induction E.1](#A5.Thminduction1 "Induction E.1. ‣ E.3 Stage 1.2.1: Initial Growth of 𝐐 ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
        [Lemma E.17](#A5.Thmlemma17 "Lemma E.17. ‣ E.3.1 Attention and Lambda Preliminaries ‣ E.3 Stage 1.2.1: Initial Growth of 𝐐 ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
        and
        [Lemma E.18](#A5.Thmlemma18 "Lemma E.18. ‣ E.3.1 Attention and Lambda Preliminaries ‣ E.3 Stage 1.2.1: Initial Growth of 𝐐 ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
        we can derive the following logit condition:
        $`1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}},\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j}\leq\frac{1}{\mathsf{poly}d}\cdot\mathbb{E}\bigg[(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}})\big|\tau(x_{1})=s,\mathcal{E}_{1}\bigg]`$
        for $`j\neq j_{2}`$. Hence, we can simply bound
        $`\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,2,i}`$ and $`\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,2,ii}`$
        as follows:

        |  |  |  |  |
        |----|----|----|----|
        |  |  | $`\displaystyle\bigg|\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,2,i}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{m}}\bigg]\bigg|,\bigg|\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,2,ii}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{m}}\bigg]\bigg|`$ |  |
        |  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq O\Big(\frac{B}{dn_{y}}\cdot\frac{1}{\mathsf{poly}d}\Big)\cdot\mathbb{E}\bigg[(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}})\big|\tau(x_{1})=s,\mathcal{E}_{1}\bigg],\text{ for }m\in\{5,6\}.`$ |  |

    Putting everything together, we have for the regmie (ii),

    |  |  |  |
    |----|----|----|
    |  | $`\displaystyle\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}}{\mathsf{Loss}^{2,2}_{5}}\Big]_{s,s}+\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,4}}{\mathsf{Loss}^{2,2}_{5}}\Big]_{s,s}=\sum_{\kappa\in\{i,ii,iii\}}\sum_{i\in[6]}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,2,\kappa}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}\bigg]`$ |  |
    |  | $`\displaystyle\geq\Omega(\frac{B}{d})\cdot\mathbb{E}\bigg[(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}})\big|\tau(x_{1})=s,\mathcal{E}_{1}\bigg].`$ |  |

∎

###### Lemma E.20.

If
[Induction E.1](#A5.Thminduction1 "Induction E.1. ‣ E.3 Stage 1.2.1: Initial Growth of 𝐐 ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
holds for all iterations $`<t`$, given $`s\in\tau(\mathcal{X})`$, for
$`[\mathbf{Q}_{4,p}]_{s,s^{\prime}}`$, $`p\in\{3,4\}`$,
$`s^{\prime}\not=s\in\tau(\mathcal{X})`$, we have

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\bigg|\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,p}}{\mathsf{Loss}^{2,2}_{5}}\Big]_{s,s^{\prime}}\bigg|\leq O\Big(\frac{1}{d}\Big)\bigg|\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,p}}{\mathsf{Loss}^{2,2}_{5}}\Big]_{s,s}\bigg|.`$ |  |

#### E.3.3 Bounded Decrease of Attention to Related Context Clause

###### Lemma E.21.

If
[Induction E.1](#A5.Thminduction1 "Induction E.1. ‣ E.3 Stage 1.2.1: Initial Growth of 𝐐 ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
holds for all iterations $`<t`$, then for any sample
$`\mathbf{Z}^{2,1}`$, we have

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\geq-O\big(\frac{\log d\log d}{\log d}\big).`$ |  |

###### Proof.

Denote the first time that
$`\mathbb{E}\Big[\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\big|\tau(x_{1})=s\Big]\leq-\Omega\big(\frac{\log d\log d}{\log d}\big)`$
as $`\widetilde{T}`$. Notice that
$`\left|\left[\mathbf{Q}_{4,p}^{(t)}\right]_{s,s^{\prime}}\right|\leq O\left(\frac{\left[\mathbf{Q}_{4,p}^{(t)}\right]_{s,s}}{d}\right)`$
for $`p\in\{3,4\}`$, thus for any sample $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,1}`$ satisfying
$`\tau(x_{1})=s`$, at time $`\widetilde{T}`$, we have

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\leq-\Omega\Big(\frac{\log d\log d}{\log d}\Big).`$ |  |

Based on the the gradient compositions from
[Lemma E.11](#A5.Thmlemma11 "Lemma E.11. ‣ Notations for gradient decompositions. ‣ E.2 Some Useful Bounds for Gradients ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
we have
$`\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}_{4,3}}\operatorname{Loss}_{5}^{2,2}\Big]_{s,s}=\mathbb{E}\Big[\mathcal{N}_{s,3,2,i}+\mathcal{N}_{s,3,2,ii}+\mathcal{N}_{s,3,2,iii}\Big]`$,
and we will discuss $`\mathcal{N}_{s,3,2,\kappa}`$ for
$`\kappa\in\{i,ii,iii\}`$ on different samples $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,1}`$.
Following the similar argument as
[Lemma E.19](#A5.Thmlemma19 "Lemma E.19. ‣ E.3.2 Gradient Lemma ‣ E.3 Stage 1.2.1: Initial Growth of 𝐐 ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
we can first directly bound the term
$`\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,3,2,iii}`$ as follows:

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,3,2,iii}\bigg]\leq\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0}^{q})=\frac{1}{\mathsf{poly}d}.`$ |  |

- •
  for $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,1}\in\mathcal{E}_{1}`$, at time $`\widetilde{T}`$,
  since the neurons for predicting $`j_{2}`$ cannot be activated, and
  $`\mathbf{logit}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j}\leq O(\frac{1}{d})`$ for
  $`j\neq j_{2}`$, thus we can naively bound the gradient on the event
  $`\mathcal{E}_{1}`$ as follows:

  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|
  |  | $`\displaystyle\Big|\sum_{\kappa\in\{i,ii\}}\mathbb{E}\big[\mathcal{N}_{s,3,2,\kappa}^{(\widetilde{T})}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{1}}\big]\Big|\leq O\Big(\frac{n_{y}B}{d^{2}}\Big)+\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q}).`$ |  |
- •
  for $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,1}\in\mathcal{E}_{2}`$, similarly,
  $`\Lambda_{5,j_{2},r}^{(t)}`$ is not activated, and thus we can focus
  on the term $`\mathcal{N}_{s,3,2,ii}`$, and specifically, the
  prediction of $`j_{2}^{{}^{\prime}}=\tau(g_{2}(y_{0}))`$. By
  ([98](#A5.E98 "Equation 98 ‣ Item 1 ‣ Lemma E.11. ‣ Notations for gradient decompositions. ‣ E.2 Some Useful Bounds for Gradients ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"))
  and
  [Lemma E.14](#A5.Thmlemma14 "Lemma E.14. ‣ E.3.1 Attention and Lambda Preliminaries ‣ E.3 Stage 1.2.1: Initial Growth of 𝐐 ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
  we have

  |  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|----|
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,3,2,ii}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{2}}\bigg]\geq-\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}`$ |  |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle\cdot\Big(1+\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},1}\Big)\mid\tau(x_{1})=s\bigg]`$ |  |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle\cdot\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbf{logit}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j^{\prime}_{2}}\mid\tau(x_{1})=s,\mathcal{E}_{2}\bigg]\frac{2B}{n_{y}\cdot d}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0}^{q}).`$ |  | (112) |
- •
  for $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,1}\in\mathcal{E}_{3}`$, by
  [Lemma E.15](#A5.Thmlemma15 "Lemma E.15. ‣ E.3.1 Attention and Lambda Preliminaries ‣ E.3 Stage 1.2.1: Initial Growth of 𝐐 ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
  we can mainly focus on the term
  $`\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,3,2,i}`$, and the prediction of
  $`\tau(g_{1}(y_{1}))`$ in $`\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,3,2,ii}`$
  since
  $`\mathbf{logit}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,\tau(g_{1}(y_{1}))}=1-O\big(\frac{1}{\log d}\big)`$.
  Hence, we have

  |  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|----|
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\Big(\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,3,2,ii}+\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,3,2,ii}\Big)\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{3}}\bigg]\geq\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}`$ |  | (113) |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle\cdot\Big(1+\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}\Big)\mid\tau(x_{1})=s\bigg]\cdot\Big(1-O\big(\frac{1}{\log d}\big)\Big)\frac{2B}{n_{y}\cdot d}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0}^{q}).`$ |  |  |
- •
  for $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,1}\in\mathcal{E}_{4}`$, the negative gradient can
  be bounded in the same way as
  ([112](#A5.E112 "Equation 112 ‣ 2nd item ‣ Proof. ‣ E.3.3 Bounded Decrease of Attention to Related Context Clause ‣ E.3 Stage 1.2.1: Initial Growth of 𝐐 ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")),
  however, the probability of $`\mathcal{E}_{4}`$ is order-wise smaller
  than $`\mathcal{E}_{2}`$ and $`\mathcal{E}_{3}`$, which can be
  neglected. Moreover, for for
  $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,1}\in\mathcal{E}_{5}\cup\mathcal{E}_{6}`$, the overall
  gradient is also negeligble since $`\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}}`$
  is very close to $`1`$.

Putting it all together, we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{4,3}}{\mathsf{Loss}^{2,2}_{5}}\Big]_{s,s}\geq-\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\cdot\Big(1+\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},1}\Big)\mid\tau(x_{1})=s\bigg]`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\cdot\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbf{logit}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j^{\prime}_{2}}\mid\tau(x_{1})=s,\mathcal{E}_{2}\bigg]\frac{B}{n_{y}\cdot d}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\cdot\Big(1+\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}\Big)\mid\tau(x_{1})=s\bigg]\cdot\Big(1-O\big(\frac{1}{\log d}\big)\Big)\frac{2B}{n_{y}\cdot d}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0}^{q}).`$ |  |

Notice that

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\Big(1+\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}\Big)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle-\Big(1+\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},1}\Big)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=2\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},1}.`$ |  |

If
$`2\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},1}<c`$
for some small constant $`c>0`$, s.t., $`\frac{cB}{\log d}<1`$, then
$`\Lambda_{5,j^{\prime}_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}^{(\widetilde{T})}\leq cB`$.
Hence
$`\mathbf{logit}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j^{\prime}_{2}}\leq\frac{1}{d^{1-\frac{cB}{\log d}}}=o(1)`$,
and
([112](#A5.E112 "Equation 112 ‣ 2nd item ‣ Proof. ‣ E.3.3 Bounded Decrease of Attention to Related Context Clause ‣ E.3 Stage 1.2.1: Initial Growth of 𝐐 ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"))
is dominated by the positive term
([113](#A5.E113 "Equation 113 ‣ 3rd item ‣ Proof. ‣ E.3.3 Bounded Decrease of Attention to Related Context Clause ‣ E.3 Stage 1.2.1: Initial Growth of 𝐐 ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")).
Else, clearly,
([112](#A5.E112 "Equation 112 ‣ 2nd item ‣ Proof. ‣ E.3.3 Bounded Decrease of Attention to Related Context Clause ‣ E.3 Stage 1.2.1: Initial Growth of 𝐐 ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"))
can be cancelled out by the positive term
([113](#A5.E113 "Equation 113 ‣ 3rd item ‣ Proof. ‣ E.3.3 Bounded Decrease of Attention to Related Context Clause ‣ E.3 Stage 1.2.1: Initial Growth of 𝐐 ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")).
Therefore,

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{4,3}}{\mathsf{Loss}^{2,2}_{5}}\Big]_{s,s}\geq\Omega\Big(\frac{B}{n_{y}\cdot d}\Big).`$ |  |

As a consequence, together with the growth of
$`\mathbf{Q}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{4,3}+\mathbf{Q}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{4,4}`$,
and nearly no change of $`[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,p}]_{s,s^{\prime}}`$ in
[Lemma E.20](#A5.Thmlemma20 "Lemma E.20. ‣ E.3.2 Gradient Lemma ‣ E.3 Stage 1.2.1: Initial Growth of 𝐐 ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
we have
$`\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}`$
must start to decrease and cannot be larger than
$`O\big(\frac{\log\log d}{\log d}\big)`$. ∎

#### E.3.4 Attention gap is small

###### Lemma E.22.

If
[Induction E.1](#A5.Thminduction1 "Induction E.1. ‣ E.3 Stage 1.2.1: Initial Growth of 𝐐 ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
holds for all iterations $`<t`$, then for any sample
$`\mathbf{Z}^{2,1}`$, we have

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}\leq c_{1},`$ |  |

where $`c_{1}>0`$ is a small constant.

###### Proof.

Let $`\widetilde{T}`$ denote the first time
$`\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},1}\big|\tau(x_{1})=s\bigg]\geq\frac{1.0005\log d}{2B}`$.
Notice that
$`\left|\left[\mathbf{Q}_{4,p}^{(t)}\right]_{s,s^{\prime}}\right|\leq O\left(\frac{\left[\mathbf{Q}_{4,p}^{(t)}\right]_{s,s}}{d}\right)`$
for $`p\in\{3,4\}`$, thus for any sample $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,1}`$ satisfying
$`\tau(x_{1})=s`$, at time $`\widetilde{T}`$, we have

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},1}\geq\frac{1.0005\log d}{2B}.`$ |  |

Based on
[Lemma E.11](#A5.Thmlemma11 "Lemma E.11. ‣ Notations for gradient decompositions. ‣ E.2 Some Useful Bounds for Gradients ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
we will discuss the gradients of $`\mathbf{Q}_{4,3}`$ and
$`\mathbf{Q}_{4,4}`$ on different samples $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,1}`$. Since
$`\Lambda^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}`$,
$`\Lambda^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j^{\prime}_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}`$ is
guaranteed to be activated to the linear regime for
$`\mathbf{Z}^{2,1}\in\mathcal{E}_{1}`$, we only need to focus on the
main event $`\mathcal{E}_{1}`$.

From
[Lemma E.13](#A5.Thmlemma13 "Lemma E.13. ‣ E.3.1 Attention and Lambda Preliminaries ‣ E.3 Stage 1.2.1: Initial Growth of 𝐐 ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
at time $`\widetilde{T}`$, we have

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathbf{logit}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j_{2}^{\prime}}=\frac{e^{\frac{1.0005\log d}{2B}\cdot 2B}}{e^{\frac{1.0005\log d}{2B}\cdot 2B}+O(d)}\Big(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j_{2}}\Big)=\Big(1-O\big({1}/{d^{0.0005}}\big)\Big)\Big(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j_{2}}\Big).`$ |  |

Then by
[Lemma E.11](#A5.Thmlemma11 "Lemma E.11. ‣ Notations for gradient decompositions. ‣ E.2 Some Useful Bounds for Gradients ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
we can obtain the gradient on the event $`\mathcal{E}_{1}`$ as follows:

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\Big(\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,3,2,i}+\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,3,2,ii}+\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,3,2,iii}\Big)\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{1}}\bigg]`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\cdot\bigg(\big(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j_{2}}\big)\cdot\Big(V_{j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}(g_{2})-\Lambda^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~-\bigg(1-O\Big(\frac{1}{d^{0.0005}}\Big)\bigg)(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}})\cdot\Big(V_{j^{\prime}_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}(g_{2})-\Lambda^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma^{q}_{0})\bigg)\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{1}}\Bigg]`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\cdot\bigg(\Big(1-O\Big(\frac{1}{d^{0.0005}}\Big)\Big)(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j_{2}})\cdot\Big(\Lambda^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j^{\prime}_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}-\Lambda^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}\Big)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~+O\Big(\frac{1}{d^{0.0005}}\Big)(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j_{2}})\cdot\Big(V_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}},2}(g_{2})-\Lambda^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}\Big)\bigg)\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{1}}\Bigg],`$ |  |

on the other hand, we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\Big(\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,4,2,i}+\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,4,2,ii}+\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,4,2,iii}\Big)\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{1}}\bigg]`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\cdot\bigg(\big(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j_{2}}\big)\cdot\Big(V_{j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}(y_{1})-\Lambda^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~-\bigg(1-O\Big(\frac{1}{d^{0.0005}}\Big)\bigg)(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}})\cdot\Big(V_{j^{\prime}_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}(y_{1})-\Lambda^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma^{q}_{0})\bigg)\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{1}}\Bigg]`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\cdot\bigg(\Big(1-O\Big(\frac{1}{d^{0.0005}}\Big)\Big)(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j_{2}})`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~\cdot\Big(-V_{j^{\prime}_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}(y_{1})+\Lambda^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j^{\prime}_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}-\Lambda^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}\Big)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~-O\Big(\frac{1}{d^{0.0005}}\Big)(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j_{2}})\cdot\Lambda^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}\bigg)\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{1}}\Bigg].`$ |  |

Since at time $`\widetilde{T}`$, we have
$`\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},1}\leq\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},1}\leq\frac{1.005\log d}{2B}`$,
we have
$`\Lambda^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j^{\prime}_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}-\Lambda^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}\ll-V_{j^{\prime}_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}(y_{1})`$.
Therefore,

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{4,4}}\mathsf{Loss}_{5}^{2,2}\Big]_{s,s}+\Big[\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{4,3}}\mathsf{Loss}_{5}^{2,2}\Big]_{s,s}\geq\Omega\Big(\frac{B}{d}\Big)\cdot\mathbb{E}\bigg[1-\mathbf{logit}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j_{2}}\big|\tau(x_{1})=s\bigg],`$ |  |

which implies that $`[\mathbf{Q}_{4,4}]_{s,s}`$ will grow faster than
$`[\mathbf{Q}_{4,3}]_{s,s}`$, and thus the attention gap cannot be
further increased. ∎

#### E.3.5 At the End of Stage 1.2.1

###### Lemma E.23.

For all iterations
$`t\leq T_{1,2,1,s}=O\Big(\frac{n_{y}d}{\eta\log d}\Big)+O\bigg(\frac{d^{\frac{(0.1+c_{1}/2)B}{\log d}}}{\eta\log d}\bigg)`$,
we have
[Induction E.1](#A5.Thminduction1 "Induction E.1. ‣ E.3 Stage 1.2.1: Initial Growth of 𝐐 ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
holds, and at time $`T_{1,2,1,s}`$, we have

1.  1.
    $`[\mathbf{Q}^{(T_{1,2,1,s})}_{4,3}]_{s,s},[\mathbf{Q}^{(T_{1,2,1,s})}_{4,4}]_{s,s}\geq\Omega(1)`$;
2.  2.
    other $`|[\mathbf{Q}^{(T_{2,1,s})}_{4,p}]_{s,s^{\prime}}|`$ for
    $`p\in\{3,4\}`$, $`s^{\prime}\in\tau(\mathcal{X})\not=s`$ are at
    most $`\widetilde{O}(\frac{1}{d})`$.

###### Proof.

The existence of $`T_{1,2,1,s}`$ can be directly obtained by using
[Lemmas E.19](#A5.Thmlemma19 "Lemma E.19. ‣ E.3.2 Gradient Lemma ‣ E.3 Stage 1.2.1: Initial Growth of 𝐐 ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
[E.20](#A5.Thmlemma20 "Lemma E.20. ‣ E.3.2 Gradient Lemma ‣ E.3 Stage 1.2.1: Initial Growth of 𝐐 ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
[E.21](#A5.Thmlemma21 "Lemma E.21. ‣ E.3.3 Bounded Decrease of Attention to Related Context Clause ‣ E.3 Stage 1.2.1: Initial Growth of 𝐐 ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
and [E.22](#A5.Thmlemma22 "Lemma E.22. ‣ E.3.4 Attention gap is small ‣ E.3 Stage 1.2.1: Initial Growth of 𝐐 ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization").
Furthermore, $`[\mathbf{Q}^{(T_{1,2,1,s})}_{4,4}]_{s,s}\geq\Omega(1)`$
can be guaranteed since
[Lemma E.22](#A5.Thmlemma22 "Lemma E.22. ‣ E.3.4 Attention gap is small ‣ E.3 Stage 1.2.1: Initial Growth of 𝐐 ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
implies that
$`\mathbf{Attn}^{(T_{1,2,1,s})}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},1}\geq 0.3-\frac{c_{1}}{2}\pm\widetilde{O}(1/d)>0.2`$,
which means that $`[\mathbf{Q}^{(T_{1,2,1,s})}_{4,4}]_{s,s}`$ should at
least grow to a constant level compared to
$`|[\mathbf{Q}^{(T_{1,2,1,s})}_{4,p}]_{s,s^{\prime}}|=\widetilde{O}(1/d)`$
with $`p\in\{3,4\}`$.

We will handle $`[\mathbf{Q}^{(T_{1,2,1,s})}_{4,3}]_{s,s}`$ by means of
a proof by contradiction. Suppose that
$`\mathbf{Attn}^{(T_{1,2,1,s})}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},1}\geq 0.4-\widetilde{c}`$,
where $`\widetilde{c}=\frac{\log d}{8B}`$ is a sufficiently small
constant. Then denote $`\widetilde{T}`$ the first time that
$`\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},1}\big|\tau(x_{1})=s\bigg]\geq 0.4-2\widetilde{c}`$.
Notice that
$`\left|\left[\mathbf{Q}_{4,p}^{(t)}\right]_{s,s^{\prime}}\right|\leq O\left(\frac{\left[\mathbf{Q}_{4,p}^{(t)}\right]_{s,s}}{d}\right)`$
for $`p\in\{3,4\}`$, thus for any sample $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,1}`$ satisfying
$`\tau(x_{1})=s`$, at time $`\widetilde{T}`$, we have

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},1}\geq 0.4-2\widetilde{c}\pm\widetilde{O}(1/d).`$ |  |

- •
  If
  $`\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},0}\geq 2\varrho`$,
  then

  - –
    for $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,1}\in\mathcal{E}_{1}`$,
    $`\Lambda^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}`$ has
    already been activated to the linear regime. Furthermore,
    $`\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},0}\leq 0.2+2\widetilde{c}-0.2\leq 2\widetilde{c}`$,
    which implies $`1-\mathbf{logit}^{(1)}_{5,j_{2}}=1-o(1)`$. Thus, by
    [Lemma E.11](#A5.Thmlemma11 "Lemma E.11. ‣ Notations for gradient decompositions. ‣ E.2 Some Useful Bounds for Gradients ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
    we have

    |  |  |  |
    |----|----|----|
    |  |
    ``` math
    \mathbb{E}\bigg[\Big(\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,3,2,i}+\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,3,2,ii}+\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,3,2,iii}\Big)\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{1}}\bigg]\geq\Omega\Big(\frac{B}{d}\Big),
    ``` |  |

    while

    |  |  |  |
    |----|----|----|
    |  |
    ``` math
    \mathbb{E}\bigg[\Big(\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,4,2,i}+\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,4,2,ii}+\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,4,2,iii}\Big)\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{1}}\bigg]\leq-\Omega\Big(\frac{B}{d}\Big).
    ``` |  |
  - –
    for $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,1}\in\mathcal{E}_{2}`$,
    $`\Lambda^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j^{\prime}_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}=(\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}+\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},0})2B\leq(0.4+\frac{4}{3}\widetilde{c}-0.4+2\widetilde{c})2B=\frac{5\log d}{6}`$.
    Thus $`\mathbf{logit}^{(1)}_{5,j_{2}}=O(\frac{1}{d^{1/6}})`$. Hence
    by
    [Lemma E.11](#A5.Thmlemma11 "Lemma E.11. ‣ Notations for gradient decompositions. ‣ E.2 Some Useful Bounds for Gradients ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
    we have

    |  |  |  |
    |----|----|----|
    |  |
    ``` math
    \mathbb{E}\bigg[\Big(\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,3,2,i}+\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,3,2,ii}+\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,3,2,iii}\Big)\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{1}}\bigg]\geq-O\Big(\frac{B}{d^{7/6}n_{y}}\Big),
    ``` |  |

    while

    |  |  |  |
    |----|----|----|
    |  |
    ``` math
    \mathbb{E}\bigg[\Big(\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,4,2,i}+\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,4,2,ii}+\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,4,2,iii}\Big)\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{1}}\bigg]\leq-\Omega\Big(\frac{B}{n_{y}d}\Big).
    ``` |  |
  - –
    $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,1}\in\mathcal{E}_{3}`$, we can use the following
    naive bounds for $`p\in\{3,4\}`$:

    |  |  |  |
    |----|----|----|
    |  |
    ``` math
    |\mathbb{E}\bigg[\Big(\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,p,2,i}+\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,p,2,ii}+\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,p,2,iii}\Big)\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{1}}\bigg]|\leq O\Big(\frac{B}{dn_{y}}\Big).
    ``` |  |

  Putting them together, combining with the fact that the gradient
  contributed by
  $`\mathcal{E}_{4}\cup\mathcal{E}_{5}\cup\mathcal{E}_{6}`$ is
  negligible, we can conclude that

  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|
  |  | $`\displaystyle\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{4,3}}\mathsf{Loss}_{5}^{2,2}\Big]_{s,s}\geq\Omega(\frac{B}{d}),\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{4,4}}\mathsf{Loss}_{5}^{2,2}\Big]_{s,s}\leq-\Omega(\frac{B}{d}),`$ |  |

  which implies
  $`\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},1}`$
  cannot further increase above $`0.4-2\widetilde{c}`$.
- •
  If
  $`\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},0}<2\varrho`$,
  we shift our focus to the comparison between event $`\mathcal{E}_{2}`$
  $`\mathcal{E}_{3}`$,

  - –
    for $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,1}\in\mathcal{E}_{1}`$, we have

    |  |  |  |
    |----|----|----|
    |  |
    ``` math
    \mathbb{E}\bigg[\Big(\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,3,2,i}+\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,3,2,ii}+\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,3,2,iii}\Big)\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{1}}\bigg]\geq-\widetilde{O}(\sigma^{q}_{0}),
    ``` |  |

    while

    |  |  |  |
    |----|----|----|
    |  |
    ``` math
    \mathbb{E}\bigg[\Big(\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,4,2,i}+\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,4,2,ii}+\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,4,2,iii}\Big)\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{1}}\bigg]\leq\widetilde{O}(\sigma^{q}_{0}).
    ``` |  |
  - –
    for $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,1}\in\mathcal{E}_{2}`$, similarly as previous
    case, we have

    |  |  |  |
    |----|----|----|
    |  |
    ``` math
    \mathbb{E}\bigg[\Big(\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,3,2,i}+\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,3,2,ii}+\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,3,2,iii}\Big)\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{2}}\bigg]\geq-O\Big(\frac{B}{d^{7/6}n_{y}}\Big),
    ``` |  |

    while

    |  |  |  |
    |----|----|----|
    |  |
    ``` math
    \mathbb{E}\bigg[\Big(\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,4,2,i}+\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,4,2,ii}+\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,4,2,iii}\Big)\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{2}}\bigg]\leq O\Big(\frac{B}{d^{7/6}n_{y}}\Big).
    ``` |  |
  - –
    $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,1}\in\mathcal{E}_{3}`$,
    $`\Big|\Lambda^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j^{\prime}_{2},r_{g_{1}\cdot y_{1}}}-\Lambda^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}\Big|\leq 4\rho B=o(1)`$,
    hence, we have

    |  |  |  |
    |----|----|----|
    |  |
    ``` math
    \mathbb{E}\bigg[\Big(\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,3,2,i}+\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,3,2,ii}+\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,3,2,iii}\Big)\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{3}}\bigg]\geq\Omega\Big(\frac{B}{dn_{y}}\Big),
    ``` |  |

    while

    |  |  |  |
    |----|----|----|
    |  |
    ``` math
    \mathbb{E}\bigg[\Big(\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,4,2,i}+\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,4,2,ii}+\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,4,2,iii}\Big)\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{3}}\bigg]\leq O\Big(\frac{B\log\log d}{dn_{y}\cdot\log d}\Big).
    ``` |  |

  Putting them together, again we can conclude that

  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|
  |  | $`\displaystyle\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{4,3}}\mathsf{Loss}_{5}^{2,2}\Big]_{s,s}\geq\Omega(\frac{B}{n_{y}d}),\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{4,4}}\mathsf{Loss}_{5}^{2,2}\Big]_{s,s}\leq O\Big(\frac{B\log\log d}{dn_{y}\cdot\log d}\Big),`$ |  |

  which implies
  $`\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},1}`$
  cannot further increase above $`0.4-2\widetilde{c}`$.

Consequently, this leads to a contradiction, and
$`\mathbf{Attn}^{(T_{1,2,1,s})}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},1}<0.4-\widetilde{c}`$,
where $`\widetilde{c}=\frac{\log d}{8B}`$. Then it would follow that
$`\mathbf{Attn}^{(T_{1,2,1,s})}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}\geq\widetilde{c}`$,
and thus $`[\mathbf{Q}^{(T_{1,2,1,s})}_{4,3}]_{s,s}\geq\Omega(1)`$. ∎

### E.4 Stage 1.2.2: Convergence with Small Wrong Attention

Recall that
$`\mathsf{Loss}^{2,2}_{5,s}=-\mathbb{E}\Big[\log p_{F}(\mathbf{Z}_{\mathsf{ans},2,5}|\mathbf{Z}^{(2,1)})\big|\tau(x_{1})=s\Big]`$
for $`s\in\tau(\mathcal{X})`$.

###### Induction E.2.

Given $`s\in\tau(\mathcal{X})`$, let $`T_{1,2,2,s}`$ denote the first
time that $`\mathsf{Loss}^{2,2}_{5,s}`$ decreases below
$`\Theta\Big(e^{(-\frac{1}{2}+3.01c_{1})\cdot 2B}\Big)`$. For all
iterations $`T_{1,2,1,s}\leq t<T_{1,2,2,s}`$, we have the following
holds

1.  1.
    $`\Big[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}\Big]_{s,s}+\Big[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,4}\Big]_{s,s}`$
    monotonically increases;
2.  2.
    for $`p\in\{3,4\}`$, for $`j\in\tau(\mathcal{X})\not=s`$,
    $`|[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,p}]_{s,j}|\leq O(\frac{[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,p}]_{s,j}}{d})`$
    ;
3.  3.
    for any sample $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,1}`$, we have
    $`\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},1}\leq c_{1}`$
    for some sufficiently small constant
    $`c_{1}=\frac{1.005\log d}{B}>0`$;
4.  4.
    for any $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,1}`$, we have
    $`\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}\leq\min\Big\{\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},1}-c_{2},0\Big\}`$,
    where $`c_{2}=\frac{\log d}{4B}>0`$ is some sufficiently small
    constant.

#### E.4.1 Attention and Lambda Preliminaries

###### Lemma E.24.

If
[Induction E.2](#A5.Thminduction2 "Induction E.2. ‣ E.4 Stage 1.2.2: Convergence with Small Wrong Attention ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
holds for all iterations $`[T_{1,2,1,s},t)`$,then for any sample
$`\mathbf{Z}^{2,1}`$, we have

1.  1.
    $`\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},1}+\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},1}\in\Big[\frac{4}{3}c_{1},0.4\pm\widetilde{O}\big(\frac{1}{d}\big)\Big]`$;
2.  2.
    $`\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},0}\geq\Omega(1)`$;
3.  3.
    $`\big|\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},0}\big|\leq\widetilde{O}\big(\frac{1}{d}\big)`$.

###### Proof.

In the following, we focus on the main events $`\mathcal{E}_{1}`$,
$`\mathcal{E}_{2}`$, and $`\mathcal{E}_{3}`$, which correspond to cases
where some confused wrong predictions occur. We denote

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},1},\quad\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},1}\;=\;c+\widetilde{O}\Big(\tfrac{1}{d}\Big).
``` |  |

- •
  If
  $`\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}\geq\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},1}`$,
  then

  - –
    If $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,1}\in\mathcal{E}_{1}`$, we have

    |  |  |  |  |
    |----|----|----|----|
    |  |  | $`\displaystyle\log p_{F}(\mathbf{Z}_{\mathsf{ans},2,5}|\mathbf{Z}^{(2,1)})`$ |  |
````
