````math
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~+\sum_{j\neq j_{2}\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})}\mathbf{logit}_{5,j}\cdot\Big(\sum_{r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}}\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda_{5,j,r})\cdot`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~\Big(\mathbf{Attn}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot V_{j,r}(y_{0})+\sum_{\ell\neq 2}\mathbf{Attn}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},\ell}\cdot V_{j_{2},r}(g_{\ell})`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~-\big(1-\mathbf{Attn}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}-\mathbf{Attn}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\big)\Lambda_{5,j,r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~\pm\sum_{j\notin\tau(\mathcal{Y})}\mathbf{logit}_{5,j}\cdot\big(\mathbf{Attn}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}+\mathbf{Attn}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\big)\widetilde{O}(\sigma^{q}_{0})\bigg)\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s}\Bigg].`$ |  |

##### Notations for gradient decompositions.

Next we define some useful notations to further simplify the expressions
of gradient.

###### Lemma F.8.

Given $`F^{(T_{k-1})}`$ from the previous stage and
$`s\in\tau(\mathcal{X})`$, we define the following notations for the
gradient decompositions:

1.  1.
    for $`[\mathbf{Q}_{4,3}]_{s,s}`$ we have
    $`\big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}_{4,3}}\mathsf{Loss}^{L_{k},2}_{F^{(T_{k-1})},5}\big]_{s,s}=\mathbb{E}\big[\mathcal{N}_{s,3,L_{k},i}+\mathcal{N}_{s,3,L_{k},ii}+\mathcal{N}_{s,3,L_{k},iii}\big]`$,
    where

    |  |  |  |  |  |
    |----|----|----|----|----|
    |  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{s,3,L_{k},i}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\mathbf{Attn}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\cdot(1-\mathbf{logit}_{5,j_{2}})\cdot`$ |  | (120) |
    |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~\Big(\sum_{r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j_{2}}}\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda_{5,j_{2},r})\cdot\Big(V_{j_{2},r}(g_{2})-\Lambda_{5,j_{2},r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s},`$ |  |  |

    |  |  |  |  |  |
    |----|----|----|----|----|
    |  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{s,3,L_{k},ii}`$ | $`\displaystyle=-\mathbf{Attn}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\cdot\sum_{j\neq j_{2}\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})}\mathbf{logit}_{5,j}\cdot`$ |  | (121) |
    |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~\Big(\sum_{r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}}\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda_{5,j,r})\cdot\Big(V_{j,r}(g_{2})-\Lambda_{5,j,r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s},`$ |  |  |

    |  |  |  |  |  |
    |----|----|----|----|----|
    |  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{s,3,L_{k},iii}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\pm\mathbf{Attn}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\cdot\sum_{j\notin\tau(\mathcal{Y})}\mathbf{logit}_{5,j}\widetilde{O}(\sigma^{q}_{0})\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s}.~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~`$ |  | (122) |
2.  2.
    for $`[\mathbf{Q}_{4,4}]_{s,s}`$, we have
    $`\big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}_{4,4}}\mathsf{Loss}^{L_{k},2}_{F^{(T_{k-1})},5}\big]_{s,s}=\mathbb{E}\big[\mathcal{N}_{s,4,L_{k},i}+\mathcal{N}_{s,4,L_{k},ii}+\mathcal{N}_{s,4,L_{k},iii}\big]`$,
    where

    |  |  |  |  |  |
    |----|----|----|----|----|
    |  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{s,4,L_{k},i}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\mathbf{Attn}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}\cdot(1-\mathbf{logit}_{5,j_{2}})\cdot`$ |  | (123) |
    |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~\Big(\sum_{r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j_{2}}}\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda_{5,j_{2},r})\cdot\Big(V_{j_{2},r}(y_{1})-\Lambda_{5,j_{2},r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s},`$ |  |  |

    |  |  |  |  |  |
    |----|----|----|----|----|
    |  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{s,4,L_{k},ii}`$ | $`\displaystyle=-\mathbf{Attn}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}\cdot\sum_{j\neq j_{2}\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})}\mathbf{logit}_{5,j}\cdot`$ |  | (124) |
    |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~\Big(\sum_{r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}}\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda_{5,j,r})\cdot\Big(V_{j,r}(y_{1})-\Lambda_{5,j,r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s},`$ |  |  |

    |  |  |  |  |  |
    |----|----|----|----|----|
    |  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{s,4,L_{k},iii}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\pm\mathbf{Attn}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}\cdot\sum_{j\notin\tau(\mathcal{Y})}\mathbf{logit}_{5,j}\widetilde{O}(\sigma^{q}_{0})\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s}.~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~`$ |  | (125) |
3.  3.
    for the summation of $`[\mathbf{Q}_{4,3}]_{s,s}`$ and
    $`[\mathbf{Q}_{4,4}]_{s,s}`$, we have
    $`\big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}_{4,3}}\mathsf{Loss}_{5}^{2,2}\big]_{s,s}+\big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}_{4,4}}\mathsf{Loss}_{5}^{2,2}\big]_{s,s}=\mathbb{E}\big[\mathcal{N}_{s,2,i}+\mathcal{N}_{s,2,ii}+\mathcal{N}_{s,2,iii}\big]`$,
    where

    |  |  |  |  |
    |----|----|----|----|
    |  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{s,L_{k},i}`$ | $`\displaystyle=(1-\mathbf{logit}_{5,j_{2}})\cdot\Big(\sum_{r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j_{2}}}\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda_{5,j_{2},r})\cdot`$ |  |
    |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~\Big(-\mathbf{Attn}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot V_{j_{2},r}(y_{0})-\sum_{\ell\neq 2}\mathbf{Attn}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},\ell}\cdot V_{j_{2},r}(g_{\ell})`$ |  |
    |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~+\big(1-\mathbf{Attn}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}-\mathbf{Attn}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\big)\Lambda_{5,j_{2},r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s},`$ |  |
    |  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{s,L_{k},ii}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\sum_{j\neq j_{2}\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})}\mathbf{logit}_{5,j}\cdot\Big(\sum_{r\in\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}_{j}}\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda_{5,j,r})\cdot`$ |  |
    |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~\Big(\mathbf{Attn}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot V_{j,r}(y_{0})+\sum_{\ell\neq 2}\mathbf{Attn}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},\ell}\cdot V_{j,r}(g_{\ell})`$ |  |
    |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~-\big(1-\mathbf{Attn}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}-\mathbf{Attn}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\big)\Lambda_{5,j,r}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big)\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s},`$ |  |
    |  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{s,L_{k},iii}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\pm\sum_{j\notin\tau(\mathcal{Y})}\mathbf{logit}_{5,j}\cdot\big(\mathbf{Attn}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}+\mathbf{Attn}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\big)\widetilde{O}(\sigma^{q}_{0})\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s}.`$ |  |

#### F.1.2 Probabilistic Events

We introduce the following events:

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{L_{k},1}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\Bigg\{\frac{1}{L_{k}}\sum_{\ell\in[L_{k}]\setminus\{2\}}\mathds{1}\!\big\{g_{\ell}(y_{1})=g_{2}(y_{1})\big\}\;\leq\;U_{k}\Bigg\},`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{L_{k},2}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\big\{y_{0}=y_{1}\big\},`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{L_{k},3}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\Bigg\{\max_{y\in\mathcal{Y}}\sum_{\ell\in[L_{k}]\setminus\{2\}}\mathds{1}\!\big\{g_{\ell}(y)=g_{2}(y)\big\}\;\leq\;W_{k}\Bigg\}.`$ |  |

We set

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
U_{k}\;=\;\frac{1}{L_{k}}\,\Big\lceil 1+\frac{L_{k}}{8}\Big\rceil\;=\;\Theta(1),\qquad W_{k}\;=\;\begin{cases}\Theta\!\Big(\dfrac{\log n_{y}}{\log\!\big(\frac{4n_{y}}{L_{k}}\,\log n_{y}\big)}\Big),&L_{k}\leq n_{y}\log n_{y},\\[7.5pt]
\Theta\!\big(\dfrac{L_{k}}{n_{y}}\big),&L_{k}\geq n_{y}\log n_{y}.\end{cases}
``` |  |

By standard balls-into-bins tail bounds and maximum-load estimates
(e.g., \[[RS98](#bib.bibx82)\]), we obtain

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\mathbf{Pr}\!\big(\mathbf{Z}^{L_{k},1}\notin\mathcal{E}_{L_{k},1}\big)\;=\;\begin{cases}O\!\big(n_{y}^{-\,U_{k}L_{k}+1}\big),&L_{k}\ll n_{y},\\[2.5pt]
\exp\!\big(-\Theta(n_{y}\log n_{y})\big),&L_{k}=\Theta(n_{y}),\\[2.5pt]
\exp\!\big(-\Theta(L_{k}\log n_{y})\big),&L_{k}\gg n_{y},\end{cases}
``` |  |

and

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\mathbf{Pr}\!\big(\mathbf{Z}^{L_{k},1}\in\mathcal{E}_{L_{k},3}\big)\;=\;1-n_{y}^{-\Omega(1)}.
``` |  |

### F.2 Reducing the Wrong Attention

As discussed in
[Remark F.1](#A6.Thmremark1 "Remark F.1. ‣ F.1.1 Gradient Computations ‣ F.1 Preliminaries ‣ Appendix F Recursive Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
both $`\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{L_{k}}`$ and the attention gap remain
bounded by a small constant at the beginning of stage $`k`$. Hence, we
can directly proceed to stage 1.2.2 of the convergence analysis as
$`{\mathcal{T}}^{2}`$. Moreover, by the symmetry between
$`[\mathbf{Q}_{4,3}]_{s,s}`$ and $`[\mathbf{Q}_{4,4}]_{s,s}`$, we may,
without loss of generality, restrict attention to a particular
$`s\in\tau(\mathcal{X})`$ and analyze the corresponding loss
$`\mathsf{Loss}^{L_{k},2}_{5,s}`$ in what follows.

###### Induction F.1.

Given $`F^{(T_{k-1})}`$ from the previous stage
$`{\mathcal{T}}^{L_{k-1}}`$ with $`L_{k-1}=2^{k-1}`$ for $`k\geq 2`$,
let $`T_{k}`$ denote the first time that
$`\mathsf{Loss}^{L_{k},2}_{5,s}`$ decreases below
$`e^{(-\frac{1}{2}+{3.01c_{1}})\cdot 2B}`$. For all iterations
$`t\leq T_{k}`$, we have the following holds

1.  1.
    $`\big[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}\big]_{s,s}+\big[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,4}\big]_{s,s}`$
    monotonically increases;
2.  2.
    for any sample $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,1}`$, we have
    $`\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},1}\leq c_{1}`$
    for some sufficiently small constant
    $`c_{1}=\frac{1.005\log d}{2B}>0`$;
3.  3.
    for any $`\mathbf{Z}^{2,1}`$, we have
    $`\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}\leq\min\Big\{\frac{L_{k}-1}{L_{k}}\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{L_{k},2}-c_{2},0\Big\}`$,
    where $`c_{2}=\frac{\log d}{4B}>0`$ is some sufficiently small
    constant.
4.  4.
    for $`p\in\{3,4\}`$, for $`s^{\prime}\in\tau(\mathcal{X})\not=s`$,
    $`|\big[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,p}\big]_{s,s^{\prime}}|\leq O\bigg(\frac{[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,p}]_{s,s}}{d}\bigg)`$
    ; otherwise $`[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,p}]_{s,s^{\prime}}=0`$.

#### F.2.1 Attention and Lambda Preliminaries

###### Lemma F.9.

If
[Induction F.1](#A6.Thminduction1 "Induction F.1. ‣ F.2 Reducing the Wrong Attention ‣ Appendix F Recursive Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
holds for all iterations $`[T_{k-1},t)`$,then for any sample
$`\mathbf{Z}^{L_{k},1}`$, we have

1.  1.
    $`\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{L_{k}}\in\Big[\frac{4}{3}c_{1},2\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{L_{k-1}}\Big]`$;
2.  2.
    $`\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathbf{k}}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}\geq\Omega(1)`$
    for any
    $`\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\in\mathcal{I}^{L_{k},1}\setminus\{(\mathsf{ans},1),(\mathsf{pred},2)\}`$;
3.  3.
    $`\big|\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathbf{k}}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}\big|\leq\widetilde{O}\big(\frac{1}{d}\big)`$
    for any
    $`\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\in\mathcal{I}^{L_{k},1}\setminus\{(\mathsf{ans},1)\}`$.

###### Proof.

The highest loss occurs when the sample $`\mathbf{Z}^{L_{k}}`$ makes the
wrong answer highly confused with the correct answer. Thus, we consider
the case that $`g_{\ell}\cdot y_{0}=g_{2}\cdot y_{0}`$ for all
$`\ell\in[L_{k}]\setminus\{2\}`$ and $`y_{0}\neq y_{1}`$.

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\log p_{F}(\mathbf{Z}_{\mathsf{ans},2,5}|\mathbf{Z}^{(L_{k},1)})`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\Theta(1)\cdot\max\Bigg\{e^{\big(\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}+\frac{L_{k}-1}{L_{k}}\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{L_{k},2}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},1}\big)2B-\big(\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}-\frac{1}{L_{k}}\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{L_{k},2}\big)2B},`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad\qquad e^{\log d-\big(\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}-\frac{1}{L_{k}}\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{L_{k},2}\big)2B}\Bigg\}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\Theta\!\left(e^{(\frac{L_{k}-1}{L_{k}}\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{L_{k},2}+c_{1})2B-\big(\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}-\frac{1}{L_{k}}\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{L_{k},2}\big)2B}\right)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\Theta\!\left(e^{-\left(\tfrac{1}{2}-\frac{3}{2}\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{L_{k},2}-c_{1}\right)2B}\right).`$ |  |

Thus, if $`\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{L_{k}}\leq\tfrac{4}{3}c_{1}`$, we
must have

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\mathsf{Loss}_{5,s}^{2,2}\;\leq\;\Theta\!\left(e^{(-\tfrac{1}{2}+3\cdot\tfrac{2c_{1}}{3}+c_{1})2B}\right)=\Theta\!\left(e^{-(\tfrac{1}{2}+3c_{1})2B}\right),
``` |  |

which contradicts the definition of $`T_{1,2,2,s}`$. Therefore, it must
hold that $`\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{L_{k}}\geq\tfrac{4}{3}c_{1}`$ for
all $`t\leq T_{k}`$.

∎

###### Lemma F.10.

If
[Induction F.1](#A6.Thminduction1 "Induction F.1. ‣ F.2 Reducing the Wrong Attention ‣ Appendix F Recursive Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
holds for all iterations $`[T_{k-1},t)`$, then given
$`\mathbf{Z}^{L_{k},1}\notin\mathcal{E}_{L_{k},2}`$, we have

1.  1.
    for the prediction $`j_{2}`$, we have

    |  |  |  |  |
    |----|----|----|----|
    |  |  | $`\displaystyle\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}=\Big(\sum_{\ell\in[L_{k}]}\mathds{1}_{g_{\ell}(y_{1})=g_{2}(y_{1})}\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},\ell}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},0}\Big)\cdot 2B+O\Big(\frac{B}{n_{y}}\Big)+O(\delta).`$ |  |
2.  2.
    for the prediction $`j^{\prime}_{2}=\tau\big(g_{2}(y_{0})\big)`$, we
    have

    |  |  |  |  |
    |----|----|----|----|
    |  |  | $`\displaystyle\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j^{\prime}_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}=\Big(\sum_{\ell\in[L_{k}]}\mathds{1}_{g_{\ell}(y_{0})=g_{2}(y_{0})}\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},\ell}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},1}\Big)\cdot 2B+O\Big(\frac{B}{n_{y}}\Big)+O(\delta).`$ |  |
3.  3.
    for other $`j=g_{2}(y)\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})`$, noticing that for this
    case $`y\neq y_{0},y_{1}`$, then we have

    |  |  |  |  |
    |----|----|----|----|
    |  |  | $`\displaystyle\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r_{g_{2}\cdot y}}=\Big(\sum_{\ell\in[L_{k}]}\mathds{1}_{g_{\ell}(y)=g_{2}(y)}\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},\ell}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},0}\Big)\cdot 2B`$ |  |
    |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~+O\Big(\frac{B}{n_{y}}\Big)+O(\delta).`$ |  |

###### Lemma F.11.

If
[Induction F.1](#A6.Thminduction1 "Induction F.1. ‣ F.2 Reducing the Wrong Attention ‣ Appendix F Recursive Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
holds for all iterations $`[T_{k-1},t)`$, then given
$`\mathbf{Z}^{L_{k},1}\in\mathcal{E}_{L_{k},2}`$, we have

1.  1.
    for the prediction $`j_{2}`$, we have

    |  |  |  |  |
    |----|----|----|----|
    |  |  | $`\displaystyle\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}=\sum_{\ell\in[L_{k}]}\mathds{1}_{g_{\ell}(y_{1})=g_{2}(y_{1})}\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},\ell}\cdot 2B+O\Big(\frac{B}{n_{y}}\Big)+O(\delta).`$ |  |
2.  2.
    for other $`j=\tau(g_{2}(y))\in\tau(\mathcal{Y})`$ with
    $`y\neq y_{1}`$, then we have

    |  |  |  |  |
    |----|----|----|----|
    |  |  | $`\displaystyle\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r_{g_{2}\cdot y}}=\Big(\sum_{\ell\in[L_{k}]}\mathds{1}_{g_{\ell}(y)=g_{2}(y)}\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},\ell}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},0}\Big)\cdot 2B`$ |  |
    |  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~~~~~+O\Big(\frac{B}{n_{y}}\Big)+O(\delta).`$ |  |

#### F.2.2 Gradient Lemma

###### Lemma F.12.

If
[Induction F.1](#A6.Thminduction1 "Induction F.1. ‣ F.2 Reducing the Wrong Attention ‣ Appendix F Recursive Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
holds for all iterations $`t\in[T_{k-1},T_{k})`$, given
$`s\in\tau(\mathcal{X})`$, we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}}{\mathsf{Loss}^{2,2}_{5}}\Big]_{s,s}+\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,4}}{\mathsf{Loss}^{2,2}_{5}}\Big]_{s,s}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq\Omega(B/d)\mathbb{E}\bigg[\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{L_{k}}\cdot(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}})\big|\tau(x_{1})=s,\mathcal{E}_{L_{k},1}\cap\mathcal{E}_{L_{k},2}^{c}\bigg].`$ |  |

###### Proof.

By
[Induction F.1](#A6.Thminduction1 "Induction F.1. ‣ F.2 Reducing the Wrong Attention ‣ Appendix F Recursive Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
[Lemma F.10](#A6.Thmlemma10 "Lemma F.10. ‣ F.2.1 Attention and Lambda Preliminaries ‣ F.2 Reducing the Wrong Attention ‣ Appendix F Recursive Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
and
[Lemma F.11](#A6.Thmlemma11 "Lemma F.11. ‣ F.2.1 Attention and Lambda Preliminaries ‣ F.2 Reducing the Wrong Attention ‣ Appendix F Recursive Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
we have

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,L_{k},i}\Big]`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~=\mathbb{E}\Big[(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}})\cdot\Big(-\sum_{\ell\neq 2}\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},\ell}\cdot V_{j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}(g_{\ell})-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot V_{j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}(y_{0})`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\quad+\big(1-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\big)\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big]`$ |  | (126) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~~~\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s}\mathds{1}_{\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}\leq B}.`$ |  |  |

Similarly,

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,L_{k},ii}\Big]`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle~~=\mathbb{E}\Big[\sum_{y\neq y_{1}}\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{2}(y))}\cdot\mathbf{sReLU}^{\prime}(\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{2}(y)),r_{g_{2}\cdot y}})`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\Big(\sum_{\ell\neq 2}\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},\ell}\cdot V_{\tau(g_{2}(y)),r_{g_{2}\cdot y}}(g_{\ell})+\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot V_{\tau(g_{2}(y)),r_{g_{2}\cdot y}}(y_{0})`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad-\big(1-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\big)\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{2}(y)),r_{g_{2}\cdot y}}\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})\Big)\pm\widetilde{O}(\delta^{q})\Big]\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s}.`$ |  |

Consider the event $`\mathcal{E}_{L_{k},1}`$. For
$`\mathbf{Z}^{L_{k},1}\in\mathcal{E}_{L_{k},1}\cap\mathcal{E}^{c}_{L_{k},2}`$,
we have $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}\leq B`$, since

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\sum_{\ell\neq 2}\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},\ell}\cdot V_{j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}(g_{\ell})\mathds{1}_{g_{\ell}(y_{1})=j_{2}}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot V_{j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}(y_{0})`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq 2\Big(\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}+(U_{k}-1/L_{k})\cdot\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{L_{k}}\Big)B`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq 2\Big(\tfrac{1-\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{L_{k}}+c_{1}}{2}+(U_{k}-1/L_{k})\cdot\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{L_{k}}\Big)B`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=2\Big(\tfrac{1+c_{1}}{2}-(\tfrac{1}{2}+1/L_{k}-U_{k})\cdot\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{L_{k}}\Big)B.`$ |  |

Thus, provided $`\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{L_{k}}\geq 4c_{1}/3`$ and
choosing $`U_{k}=\left\lfloor{1+\frac{L_{k}}{8}}\right\rfloor/L_{k}`$,
we indeed have $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}\leq B`$.

- •
  For $`j=\tau(g_{2}(y))`$ in $`\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,L_{k},ii}`$:

  - –
    $`y=y_{0}\neq y_{1}`$,

    |  |  |  |  |  |
    |----|----|----|----|----|
    |  |  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{\ell\neq 2}\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},\ell}\cdot V_{\tau(g_{2}(y)),r_{g_{2}\cdot y}}(g_{\ell})+\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot V_{\tau(g_{2}(y)),r_{g_{2}\cdot y}}(y_{0})`$ |  |  |
    |  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq\sum_{\ell\neq 2}\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},\ell}\cdot V_{\tau(g_{2}(y)),r_{g_{2}\cdot y}}(g_{\ell})\mathds{1}_{g_{\ell}(y)\neq g_{2}(y)}`$ |  |  |
    |  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq-O\Big(\tfrac{B}{n_{y}}\Big)\cdot\big(1-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\big).`$ |  | (127) |
  - –
    $`y\neq y_{0},y_{1}`$. If there exists at least one $`{\ell}\neq 2`$
    such that $`g_{\ell}(y)=g_{2}(y)`$, then by cancellation,

    |  |  |  |  |  |
    |----|----|----|----|----|
    |  |  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{\ell\neq 2}\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},\ell}\cdot V_{\tau(g_{2}(y)),r_{g_{2}\cdot y}}(g_{\ell})+\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot V_{\tau(g_{2}(y)),r_{g_{2}\cdot y}}(y_{0})`$ |  |  |
    |  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq-O\Big(\tfrac{B}{n_{y}}\Big)\cdot\big(1-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\big).`$ |  | (128) |

    Else, pick $`y^{\prime}\neq y,y_{1}`$ so that
    $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{2}(y^{\prime})),r_{g_{2}\cdot y^{\prime}}}\geq\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{2}(y)),r_{g_{2}\cdot y}}`$.
    Then

    |  |  |  |
    |----|----|----|
    |  |
    ``` math
    \mathbf{logit}_{5,\tau(g_{2}(y))}\leq O\Big(\tfrac{1}{n_{y}}\Big)\big(1-\mathbf{logit}_{5,j_{2}}\big),
    ``` |  |

    which implies

    |  |  |  |  |  |
    |----|----|----|----|----|
    |  |  | $`\displaystyle\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j}\Big(\sum_{\ell\neq 2}\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},\ell}\cdot V_{\tau(g_{2}(y)),r_{g_{2}\cdot y}}(g_{\ell})+\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot V_{\tau(g_{2}(y)),r_{g_{2}\cdot y}}(y_{0})\Big)`$ |  |  |
    |  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq-O\Big(\tfrac{B}{L_{k}n_{y}}\Big)\cdot\big(1-\mathbf{logit}_{5,j_{2}}\big)\cdot\big(1-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\big).`$ |  | (129) |
- •
  For $`\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,L_{k},i}`$:

  - –
    If
    $`\mathbf{Z}^{L_{k},1}\in\mathcal{E}_{L_{k},1}\cap\mathcal{E}^{c}_{L_{k},2}`$,
    then

    |  |  |  |  |  |
    |----|----|----|----|----|
    |  |  | $`\displaystyle-\sum_{\ell\neq 2}\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},\ell}\cdot V_{j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}(g_{\ell})-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot V_{j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}(y_{0})`$ |  |  |
    |  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad+\big(1-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\big)\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}`$ |  |  |
    |  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq-\sum_{\ell\neq 2}\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},\ell}\mathds{1}_{g_{\ell}=j_{2}}\cdot V_{j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}(g_{\ell})-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot V_{j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}(y_{0})`$ |  |  |
    |  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad+\big(1-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\big)\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}`$ |  |  |
    |  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq\Big(-(U_{k}-\tfrac{1}{L_{k}})\cdot 2B+\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}\Big)\cdot\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{L_{k}}\;\;\geq\;\;\big(\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}-\tfrac{B}{4}\big)\cdot\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{L_{k}}.`$ |  | (130) |
  - –
    Else, for
    $`\mathbf{Z}^{L_{k},1}\in\mathcal{E}^{c}_{L_{k},1}\cup\mathcal{E}_{L_{k},2}`$,
    note that having more identical predictions
    $`g_{\ell}(y_{1})=j_{2}`$ or less distraction (i.e.,
    $`y_{0}=y_{1}`$) increases
    $`\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}`$. Hence

    |  |  |  |
    |----|----|----|
    |  |
    ``` math
    \mathbb{E}\big[(1-\mathbf{logit}_{5,j_{2}})\mid\mathcal{E}^{c}_{L_{k},1}\cup\mathcal{E}_{L_{k},2}\big]\;\leq\;O(1)\cdot\mathbb{E}\big[(1-\mathbf{logit}_{5,j_{2}})\mid\mathcal{E}_{L_{k},1}\cap\mathcal{E}^{c}_{L_{k},2}\big],
    ``` |  |

    and

    |  |  |  |  |  |
    |----|----|----|----|----|
    |  |  | $`\displaystyle\Big|-\sum_{\ell\neq 2}\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},\ell}\cdot V_{j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}(g_{\ell})-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},0}\cdot V_{j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}(y_{0})`$ |  |  |
    |  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad+\big(1-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\big)\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}\Big|\;\leq\;O(1)\cdot\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{L_{k}}\cdot B.`$ |  | (131) |

Putting the above together, we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}}{\mathsf{Loss}^{2,2}_{5}}\Big]_{s,s}+\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,4}}{\mathsf{Loss}^{2,2}_{5}}\Big]_{s,s}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Big[\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,L_{k},1}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{L_{k},1}\cap\mathcal{E}_{L_{k},2}^{c}}\Big]+\mathbb{E}\Big[\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,L_{k},1}\mathds{1}_{\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}\leq B}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}^{c}_{L_{k},1}\cup\mathcal{E}_{L_{k},2}}\Big]`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbb{E}\Big[\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,L_{k},2}\Big]\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0}^{q})`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Big[(\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,L_{k},1}+\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,L_{k},2})\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{L_{k},1}\cap\mathcal{E}_{L_{k},2}^{c}}\Big]`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbb{E}\Big[\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,L_{k},1}\mathds{1}_{\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}\leq B}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}^{c}_{L_{k},1}\cup\mathcal{E}_{L_{k},2}}\Big]+\mathbb{E}\Big[\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,L_{k},2}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}^{c}_{L_{k},1}\cup\mathcal{E}_{L_{k},2}}\Big]\pm\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0}^{q}).`$ |  |

First, by
([127](#A6.E127 "Equation 127 ‣ 1st item ‣ 1st item ‣ Proof. ‣ F.2.2 Gradient Lemma ‣ F.2 Reducing the Wrong Attention ‣ Appendix F Recursive Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")),
([128](#A6.E128 "Equation 128 ‣ 2nd item ‣ 1st item ‣ Proof. ‣ F.2.2 Gradient Lemma ‣ F.2 Reducing the Wrong Attention ‣ Appendix F Recursive Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")),
([130](#A6.E130 "Equation 130 ‣ 1st item ‣ 2nd item ‣ Proof. ‣ F.2.2 Gradient Lemma ‣ F.2 Reducing the Wrong Attention ‣ Appendix F Recursive Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")),

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[(\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,L_{k},1}+\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,L_{k},2})\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{L_{k},1}\cap\mathcal{E}_{L_{k},2}^{c}}\Big]`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq\mathbb{E}\Big[(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}})\Big(\big(\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}-\tfrac{B}{4}\big)\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{L_{k}}\Big)-(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}})\Big(O(\tfrac{B}{n_{y}})\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{L_{k}}+\max_{y\neq y_{1}}\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{2}(y)),r_{g_{2}\cdot y}}\Big)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\hskip 213.39566pt\cdot\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{L_{k},1}\cap\mathcal{E}_{L_{k},2}^{c}}\Big]`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq\Omega(B/d)\cdot\mathbb{E}\Big[\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{L_{k}}\cdot(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}})\mid\tau(x_{1})=s,\mathcal{E}_{L_{k},1}\cap\mathcal{E}_{L_{k},2}^{c}\Big].`$ |  |

Moreover, by
([131](#A6.E131 "Equation 131 ‣ 2nd item ‣ 2nd item ‣ Proof. ‣ F.2.2 Gradient Lemma ‣ F.2 Reducing the Wrong Attention ‣ Appendix F Recursive Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")),

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,L_{k},1}\mathds{1}_{\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}\leq B/2}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}^{c}_{L_{k},1}\cup\mathcal{E}_{L_{k},2}}\Big]`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq-\mathbf{Pr}(\mathbf{Z}^{L_{k},1}\notin\mathcal{E}^{c}_{L_{k},1})\cdot O(B/d)\cdot\mathbb{E}\Big[\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{L_{k}}\cdot(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}})\mid\tau(x_{1})=s,\mathcal{E}^{c}_{L_{k},1}\cup\mathcal{E}_{L_{k},2}\Big]`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq-\Big(O(1/n^{\Omega(1)})+1/n_{y}\Big)\cdot O(B/d)\cdot\mathbb{E}\Big[\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{L_{k}}\cdot(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}})\mid\tau(x_{1})=s,\mathcal{E}_{L_{k},1}\cap\mathcal{E}_{L_{k},2}^{c}\Big],`$ |  |

and, by
([127](#A6.E127 "Equation 127 ‣ 1st item ‣ 1st item ‣ Proof. ‣ F.2.2 Gradient Lemma ‣ F.2 Reducing the Wrong Attention ‣ Appendix F Recursive Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")),
([128](#A6.E128 "Equation 128 ‣ 2nd item ‣ 1st item ‣ Proof. ‣ F.2.2 Gradient Lemma ‣ F.2 Reducing the Wrong Attention ‣ Appendix F Recursive Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")),
([129](#A6.E129 "Equation 129 ‣ 2nd item ‣ 1st item ‣ Proof. ‣ F.2.2 Gradient Lemma ‣ F.2 Reducing the Wrong Attention ‣ Appendix F Recursive Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")),

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,L_{k},2}\mathds{1}_{\mathcal{E}^{c}_{L_{k},1}\cup\mathcal{E}_{L_{k},2}}\Big]\;\geq\;\tfrac{1}{n_{y}}\cdot O(B/d)\cdot\mathbb{E}\Big[\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{L_{k}}\cdot(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}})\mid\tau(x_{1})=s,\mathcal{E}^{c}_{L_{k},1}\cup\mathcal{E}_{L_{k},2}\Big].`$ |  |

Therefore,

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}}{\mathsf{Loss}^{2,2}_{5}}\Big]_{s,s}+\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,4}}{\mathsf{Loss}^{2,2}_{5}}\Big]_{s,s}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq\Omega(B/d)\cdot\mathbb{E}\Big[\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{L_{k}}\cdot(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}})\mid\tau(x_{1})=s,\mathcal{E}_{L_{k},1}\cap\mathcal{E}_{L_{k},2}^{c}\Big].`$ |  |

∎

###### Lemma F.13.

If
[Induction F.1](#A6.Thminduction1 "Induction F.1. ‣ F.2 Reducing the Wrong Attention ‣ Appendix F Recursive Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
holds for all iterations $`t\in[T_{k-1},T_{k})`$, given
$`s\in\tau(\mathcal{X})`$, for $`[\mathbf{Q}_{4,p}]_{s,s^{\prime}}`$,
$`p\in\{3,4\}`$, $`s^{\prime}\not=s\in\tau(\mathcal{X})`$, we have

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\Big|\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,p}}{\mathsf{Loss}^{2,2}_{5}}\Big]_{s,s^{\prime}}\Big|\leq O(\frac{1}{d})\Big|\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,p}}{\mathsf{Loss}^{2,2}_{5}}\Big]_{s,s}\Big|.`$ |  |

#### F.2.3 Attention gap is small

###### Lemma F.14.

If
[Induction F.1](#A6.Thminduction1 "Induction F.1. ‣ F.2 Reducing the Wrong Attention ‣ Appendix F Recursive Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
holds for all iterations $`t\in[T_{k-1},T_{k})`$, then for any sample
$`\mathbf{Z}^{L_{k},1}`$, we have

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}\leq c_{1},`$ |  |

where $`c_{1}>0`$ is a small constant.

###### Proof.

The proof follows the same high-level idea as
[Lemma E.22](#A5.Thmlemma22 "Lemma E.22. ‣ E.3.4 Attention gap is small ‣ E.3 Stage 1.2.1: Initial Growth of 𝐐 ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization").
The key observation is that for each $`\mathbf{Z}^{L_{k},1}`$, there
always exists at least one $`y\neq y_{1}`$ such that, for some index
$`j`$ appearing in the gradient term $`\mathcal{N}^{(t)}_{s,L_{k},2}`$,
we have

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r_{g_{2}\cdot y}}\;\geq\;2\big(\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}\big)B.
``` |  |

Let $`\widehat{\mathcal{J}}`$ denote the set of indices $`j`$ achieving
$`\arg\max_{y\notin\{y_{1}\}}\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,j,r_{g_{2}\cdot y}}`$, and
pick one such index $`j^{\prime}=g_{2}(y^{\prime})`$. Define
$`\widetilde{T}`$ to be the first time when
$`\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}\geq c_{1}`$.
At this time, we have

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\sum_{j\in\widehat{\mathcal{J}}}\mathbf{logit}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j}\;=\;\Big(1-O(d^{-\Omega(1)})\Big)\big(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j_{2}}\big).
``` |  |

Plugging this into the gradient expressions yields

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,3,L_{k},i}+\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,3,L_{k},ii}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\quad=\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\cdot\bigg(\Big(1-O(d^{-\Omega(1)})\Big)\big(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j_{2}}\big)\cdot\Big(\Lambda^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j^{\prime},r_{g_{2}\cdot y^{\prime}}}-\Lambda^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}\Big)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad\quad+O(d^{-\Omega(1)})\big(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j_{2}}\big)\cdot\Big(V_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}},2}(g_{2})-\Lambda^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}\Big)\bigg)\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s},`$ |  |

and similarly

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,4,L_{k},i}+\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,4,L_{k},ii}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\quad=\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\cdot\bigg(\Big(1-O(d^{-\Omega(1)})\Big)\big(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j_{2}}\big)\cdot\Big(-V_{j^{\prime}_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}(y_{1})+\Lambda^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j^{\prime},r_{g_{2}\cdot y^{\prime}}}-\Lambda^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}\Big)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad\quad+O(d^{-\Omega(1)})\big(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j_{2}}\big)\cdot\Big(V_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}},2}(y_{1})-\Lambda^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}\Big)\bigg)\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s}.`$ |  |

Since
$`\Lambda^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j^{\prime},r_{g_{2}\cdot y^{\prime}}}-\Lambda^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}\leq 0`$
and

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
-V_{j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}(y_{1})-\Lambda^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}+\Lambda^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y^{\prime}}}\;\geq\;\Omega(B),
``` |  |

we obtain

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\Big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{4,4}}\mathsf{Loss}_{5}^{2,2}\Big]_{s,s}+\Big[\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{4,3}}\mathsf{Loss}_{5}^{2,2}\Big]_{s,s}\;\;\geq\;\;\Omega\!\left(\tfrac{B}{d}\right)\cdot\mathbb{E}\big[1-\mathbf{logit}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j_{2}}\;\big|\;\tau(x_{1})=s\big].
``` |  |

Therefore, the quantity
$`\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}`$
cannot further increase, and we conclude that

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}\;\leq\;c_{1}.
``` |  |

∎

###### Lemma F.15.

If
[Induction F.1](#A6.Thminduction1 "Induction F.1. ‣ F.2 Reducing the Wrong Attention ‣ Appendix F Recursive Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
holds for all iterations $`t\in[T_{k-1},T_{k})`$, then for any sample
$`\mathbf{Z}^{L_{k},1}`$, we have

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{ans},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{{\mathsf{ans},1}\rightarrow\mathsf{pred},2}\leq\frac{L_{k}-1}{L_{k}}\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{L_{k}}-c_{2},`$ |  |

where $`c_{2}=\frac{\log d}{2B}>0`$ is a small constant.

###### Proof.

The argument parallels
[Lemma E.34](#A5.Thmlemma34 "Lemma E.34. ‣ E.4.3 Attention gap is small ‣ E.4 Stage 1.2.2: Convergence with Small Wrong Attention ‣ Appendix E Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case for Short-Length ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
but in the recursive setting we first note that for every
$`\mathbf{Z}^{L_{k},1}\in\mathcal{E}_{L_{k},2}^{c}`$,

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\Lambda^{(t)}_{5,\tau(g_{2}(y_{0})),\,r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{0}}}\;\leq\;\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},1}+\frac{L_{k}-1}{L_{k}}\,\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{L_{k}}.
``` |  |

Let $`\widetilde{T}`$ be the first time such that

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\mathbb{E}\Big[\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},1}+\frac{L_{k}-1}{L_{k}}\,\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{L_{k}}\;\Big|\;\tau(x_{1})=s\Big]\;\leq\;\frac{\log d}{4.02\,B}.
``` |  |

Then, for any $`y`$,

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\Lambda^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,\tau(g_{2}(y)),\,r_{g_{2}\cdot y}}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\;=\;2\big(\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}+\!\!\sum_{\ell\neq 2}\!\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},\ell}\,\mathds{1}_{g_{\ell}(y)=g_{2}(y)}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},0}\big)\,B\;\leq\;\frac{\log d}{2.01}.`$ |  |

Hence the corresponding wrong-class logit mass is small:

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\mathbf{logit}^{(1)}_{5,j_{2}}\;\leq\;O\!\big(d^{-1.01/2.01}\big)\,\big(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(t)}_{5,j_{2}}\big).
``` |  |

Plugging this into the gradient decomposition, we obtain

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,3,L_{k},i}+\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,3,L_{k},ii}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad=\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}\,\Big((1-\mathbf{logit}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j_{2}})\big(V_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}},2}(g_{2})-\Lambda^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}\big)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad-O\!\big(d^{-1.01/2.01}\big)\,(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j_{2}})\sum_{y\neq y_{0}}\big(V_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y},2}(g_{2})-\Lambda^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y}}\big)\Big)\,\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad\geq\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}\cdot\Omega(1)\cdot(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j_{2}})\cdot\big(V_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}},2}(g_{2})-\Lambda^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}\big)\,\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s},`$ |  |

and

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,4,L_{k},i}+\mathcal{N}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{s,4,L_{k},ii}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad=\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}\,\Big((1-\mathbf{logit}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j_{2}})\big(V_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}},2}(y_{1})-\Lambda^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}\big)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad-O\!\big(d^{-1.01/2.01}\big)\,(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j_{2}})\sum_{y\neq y_{0}}\big(V_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y},2}(y_{1})-\Lambda^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y}}\big)\Big)\,\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad\leq-\,\mathbf{Attn}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}\cdot\Omega(1)\cdot(1-\mathbf{logit}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j_{2}})\cdot\Lambda^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j_{2},r_{g_{2}\cdot y_{1}}}\,\mathds{1}_{\tau(x_{1})=s}.`$ |  |

Therefore,

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{4,3}}\mathsf{Loss}_{5}^{2,2}\big]_{s,s}`$ | $`\displaystyle\;\geq\;\Omega\!\left(\frac{B}{d}\right)\cdot\mathbb{E}\big[\,1-\mathbf{logit}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j_{2}}\,\big|\,\tau(x_{1})=s,\,\mathcal{E}_{L_{k},2}^{c}\big],`$ |  | (132) |
|  | $`\displaystyle\big[-\nabla_{\mathbf{Q}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{4,4}}\mathsf{Loss}_{5}^{2,2}\big]_{s,s}`$ | $`\displaystyle\;\leq\;-\,\Omega\!\left(\frac{B}{d}\right)\cdot\mathbb{E}\big[\,1-\mathbf{logit}^{(\widetilde{T})}_{5,j_{2}}\,\big|\,\tau(x_{1})=s,\,\mathcal{E}_{L_{k},2}^{c}\big].`$ |  | (133) |

Finally, recall that

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}+\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},1}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},1}\;=\;\frac{e^{[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}]_{s,s}}-e^{[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,4}]_{s,s}}-e^{\widetilde{O}(1/d)}}{e^{[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}]_{s,s}}+e^{[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,4}]_{s,s}}+e^{\widetilde{O}(1/d)}}.
``` |  |

Combining the two gradient inequalities shows that the quantity

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}+\frac{L_{k}-1}{L_{k}}\,\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{L_{k}}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},1}
``` |  |

must strictly decrease at time $`\widetilde{T}+1`$. ∎

#### F.2.4 At the end of stage

###### Lemma F.16 (At the end of stage).

[Induction F.1](#A6.Thminduction1 "Induction F.1. ‣ F.2 Reducing the Wrong Attention ‣ Appendix F Recursive Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
holds for all iterations
$`T_{k-1}<t\leq T_{k}={O}(\frac{\mathsf{poly}(d)}{\eta B})`$. At the end
of stage $`k`$, we have

1.  1.
    Attention concentration:
    $`\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{L_{k}}\leq 5.04c_{1}`$ for some small
    constant $`c_{1}=\frac{1.005\log d}{B}>0`$;
2.  2.
    Loss convergence:
    $`\mathsf{Loss}^{L_{k},2}_{F^{(T_{k-1})},5}\leq e^{(-\frac{1}{2}+3.01c_{1})2B}=\frac{1}{\mathsf{poly}d}`$.

###### Proof.

By
[Lemmas F.9](#A6.Thmlemma9 "Lemma F.9. ‣ F.2.1 Attention and Lambda Preliminaries ‣ F.2 Reducing the Wrong Attention ‣ Appendix F Recursive Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
and [F.12](#A6.Thmlemma12 "Lemma F.12. ‣ F.2.2 Gradient Lemma ‣ F.2 Reducing the Wrong Attention ‣ Appendix F Recursive Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
the diagonal entries $`[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,3}]_{s,s}`$ and
$`[\mathbf{Q}^{(t)}_{4,4}]_{s,s}`$ keep increasing until the total
wrong-attention mass satisfies
$`\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}\geq\tfrac{4c_{1}}{3}`$. Combining
[Lemmas F.12](#A6.Thmlemma12 "Lemma F.12. ‣ F.2.2 Gradient Lemma ‣ F.2 Reducing the Wrong Attention ‣ Appendix F Recursive Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
[F.13](#A6.Thmlemma13 "Lemma F.13. ‣ F.2.2 Gradient Lemma ‣ F.2 Reducing the Wrong Attention ‣ Appendix F Recursive Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
[F.14](#A6.Thmlemma14 "Lemma F.14. ‣ F.2.3 Attention gap is small ‣ F.2 Reducing the Wrong Attention ‣ Appendix F Recursive Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
and [F.15](#A6.Thmlemma15 "Lemma F.15. ‣ F.2.3 Attention gap is small ‣ F.2 Reducing the Wrong Attention ‣ Appendix F Recursive Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
there exists a stopping time

|     |                                                            |     |
|-----|------------------------------------------------------------|-----|
|     |
       ``` math
       T_{k}\;=\;O\!\left(\frac{\mathsf{poly}(d)}{\eta B}\right),
       ```                                                         |     |

such that
[Induction F.1](#A6.Thminduction1 "Induction F.1. ‣ F.2 Reducing the Wrong Attention ‣ Appendix F Recursive Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
holds for all $`t\in[T_{1},\,T_{k})`$.

To obtain an upper bound on $`\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{L_{k}}`$ at the
end of stage $`k`$, it suffices to work on the main event
$`\mathcal{E}_{L_{k},3}`$.

*Case 1: $`L_{k}=O(1)`$.* For each $`y\in\mathcal{Y}`$, at most one
index $`\ell\neq 2`$ can satisfy $`g_{\ell}(y)=g_{2}(y)`$, and thus the
number of collisions per $`y`$ is uniformly bounded by $`W_{k}`$. Hence,

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\log p_{F}\!\big(Z_{\mathsf{ans},2,5}\mid Z^{(2,1)}\big)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq\Theta(1)\cdot\exp\!\left(\log d-\big(\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}+\sum_{\ell\neq 2}\!\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},\ell}\,\mathds{1}_{g_{\ell}(y_{1})=g_{2}(y_{1})}-\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{ans},0}\big)\,2B\right)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq\Theta(1)\cdot\exp\!\left(\log d-\big(\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}+(1/L_{k}-1/L_{k})\,\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{L_{k}}\big)\,2B\right)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq\Theta\!\left(\exp\!\big(-(\tfrac{1}{2}+\tfrac{c_{1}}{2}-\tfrac{\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{L_{k}}}{2}-\tfrac{\log d}{B})\,2B\big)\right),`$ |  |

where the last line uses
$`\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}\leq\tfrac{1}{2}(1-\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{L_{k}}+c_{1})=\tfrac{1+c_{1}}{2}-\tfrac{\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{L_{k}}}{2}`$.

*Case 2: $`L_{k}=\omega(1)`$.* If $`L_{k}\leq n_{y}\log n_{y}`$ then
$`W_{k}/L_{k}=O(1/L_{k})`$; otherwise $`W_{k}/L_{k}=O(1/n_{y})`$. In
either regime $`W_{k}/L_{k}=o(1)`$, and we similarly obtain

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\log p_{F}\!\big(Z_{\mathsf{ans},2,5}\mid Z^{(2,1)}\big)`$ | $`\displaystyle\geq\Theta(1)\cdot\exp\!\left(\log d-\big(\mathbf{Attn}^{(t)}_{\mathsf{ans},1\to\mathsf{pred},2}+(o(1)-1/L_{k})\,\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{L_{k}}\big)\,2B\right)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq\Theta\!\left(\exp\!\big(-(\tfrac{1}{2}+\tfrac{c_{1}}{2}-\tfrac{\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{L_{k}}}{2}-\tfrac{\log d}{B})\,2B\big)\right).`$ |  |

At convergence we therefore have

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\tfrac{1}{2}+\tfrac{c_{1}}{2}-\tfrac{\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{L_{k}}}{2}-\tfrac{\log d}{B}\;\;\geq\;\;\tfrac{1}{2}-3.01\,c_{1},
``` |  |

which rearranges to

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{L_{k}}\leq 2(3.02c_{1}+0.5c_{1}-c_{1})=5.04c_{1}.
``` |  |

In particular, since $`B/\log d`$ can be taken as a sufficiently large
constant, we obtain the clean stage-end bound

|     |                                                |     |
|-----|------------------------------------------------|-----|
|     |
       ``` math
       \epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{L_{k}}\;=\;O(c_{1}).
       ```                                             |     |

∎

### F.3 Proof of Main Theorem

###### Theorem F.1 (Recursive self-training (Restatement)).

Assume the distribution $`\mathcal{D}^{L}`$ induced from
$`\textbf{LEGO}(\mathcal{X},\mathcal{G},\mathcal{Y})`$ satisfies
[Assumption 3.1](#S3.Thmassumption1 "Assumption 3.1 (Asymptotic regime). ‣ 3 Problem Formulation ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
[3.2](#S3.Thmassumption2 "Assumption 3.2 (LEGO distribution 𝒟^𝐿,𝒟^{𝐿,𝐿^′}). ‣ 3.1 Data Distribution ‣ 3 Problem Formulation ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
and
[5.1](#S5.Thmassumption1 "Assumption 5.1 (Symmetry group actions). ‣ 5 Learning CoT on Symmetry Group Actions on ℤ_𝑛 ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
and assume the transformer network satisfies
Assumption [3.3](#S3.Thmassumption3 "Assumption 3.3 (Initialization). ‣ 3.3 LEGO Task via CoT Reasoning and Training Objective ‣ 3 Problem Formulation ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
[3.4](#S3.Thmassumption4 "Assumption 3.4 (Logit clipping). ‣ Additional Technical Assumptions. ‣ 3.3 LEGO Task via CoT Reasoning and Training Objective ‣ 3 Problem Formulation ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
[3.5](#S3.Thmassumption5 "Assumption 3.5 (Block-sparse attention matrix). ‣ Additional Technical Assumptions. ‣ 3.3 LEGO Task via CoT Reasoning and Training Objective ‣ 3 Problem Formulation ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
and $`n_{y}<m\ll\log^{2}d`$. Then for any
$`1\leq k<\log_{2}|\mathcal{X}|`$, the transformer $`F^{(T_{k})}`$
trained via
Algorithm [2](#algorithm2 "Algorithm 2 ‣ 5 Learning CoT on Symmetry Group Actions on ℤ_𝑛 ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization")
up to length $`L_{k}=2^{k}`$ and
$`T_{k}=O(\frac{\mathsf{poly}(d)}{\eta})`$ satisfies: $`F^{(T_{k})}`$ is
able to solve $`{\mathcal{T}}^{L_{k+1}}`$ with $`{L_{k+1}}=2^{k+1}`$,
i.e.,

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\mathrm{Acc}_{L_{k+1}}\bigl(F^{(T_{k})}\bigr)=1-O(1/\mathsf{poly}(d)).
``` |  |

###### Proof.

By
[Lemma F.16](#A6.Thmlemma16 "Lemma F.16 (At the end of stage). ‣ F.2.4 At the end of stage ‣ F.2 Reducing the Wrong Attention ‣ Appendix F Recursive Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
at the time $`T_{k}`$, we have
$`\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{L_{k}}\leq 5.04c_{1}`$, combining with
[Induction F.1](#A6.Thminduction1 "Induction F.1. ‣ F.2 Reducing the Wrong Attention ‣ Appendix F Recursive Learning the Attention Layer: Symmetry Case ‣ Transformers Provably Learn Chain-of-Thought Reasoning with Length Generalization"),
non-diagonal entry of $`\mathbf{Q}_{p,q}`$ remains close to $`0`$, thus
moving to the next stage, we have
$`\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{L_{k+1},\ell}\leq 4\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{L_{k}}\leq 20.16c_{1}`$
(notice that $`20.16c_{1}`$ could be sufficiently small e.g., $`0.01`$
since we can set a reasonably large B) for all $`\ell<L_{k+1}`$.
Moreover, $`\Delta^{L_{k+1},\ell}\leq\Delta^{L_{k},1}`$. Hence, we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{Z^{L_{k+1}}\sim\mathcal{D}^{L_{k+1}}}\bigg[\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{Z}_{\mathsf{ans},\ell+1}\sim p_{F}^{(T_{k})}(\cdot|Z^{L_{k+1},\ell})}[\mathds{1}_{\{\widehat{Z}_{\mathsf{ans},\ell+1}\neq Z_{\mathsf{ans},\ell+1}\}}]\bigg]`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq O(1)\cdot\mathbb{E}_{Z^{L_{k+1}}\sim\mathcal{D}^{L_{k+1}}}\bigg[1-\mathbf{logit}_{5,\tau(\mathbf{Z}_{\mathsf{ans},\ell+1,5})}\bigg]`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq O(1)\cdot e^{\big(-(\frac{1}{2}-\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{L_{k+1}}-(\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{L_{k+1}}-c_{2})/2-\epsilon_{\mathsf{attn}}^{L_{k+1}})\big)\cdot 2B}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq O(1)\cdot e^{(-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\log d}{4B}+\frac{5}{2}\cdot 0.01)2B}=O\Big(\frac{1}{\mathsf{poly}d}\Big).`$ |  |

Thus
$`\mathrm{Acc}_{L_{k+1}}(F^{T_{k}})\geq 1-O\big(\frac{1}{\mathsf{poly}d}\big)`$.
∎
````
